Method: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建物理仿真平台，支持四种人形机器人（Unitree G1/H1、Internal-A/B），通过分层控制栈（强化学习运动策略+可互换PD或MPC路径跟踪器）实现控制。采用3D高斯泼溅重建与艺术家设计的场景，通过多代理指令生成流程（生成器-评审器-释义器+人工验证）构建包含933条碰撞感知参考轨迹的数据集，每条配有一条细粒度指令和三种风格变体。

Result: 在四种模型和四种人形平台上，JanusVLN取得最高平均成功率43.55%和nDTW 48.38。在20个情节的Unitree G1仿真到现实迁移实验中，导航误差与仿真强相关（r=0.935），平均绝对差0.68m，平均轨迹相似度0.782 nDTW。

Conclusion: 结果表明VLN模型、控制器与人形机器人形态之间存在显著的交互效应，物理执行是实现真实世界人形导航的关键环节。平台提供易扩展的接口，新机器人和新VLN模型可低成本集成。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) for humanoid robots poses challenges existing benchmarks fail to address: bipedal locomotion imposes physical constraints absent from wheeled agents, humanoid morphologies vary across platforms, and egocentric observations are distorted by locomotion-induced camera dynamics. We present HumanoidVLN, a physics-grounded simulator and benchmark for VLN across diverse humanoid embodiments. Built on NVIDIA Isaac Sim, our platform supports an extensible set of humanoid configurations, demonstrated on four robots (Unitree G1, Unitree H1, Internal-A, Internal-B) spanning 10-12 lower-body DoF and heights from 1.17m to 1.80m, via a hierarchical control stack combining a reinforcement learning locomotion policy with interchangeable PD or MPC path trackers. New robots and VLN models integrate with minimal effort; we demonstrate compatibility with NaVILA, DualVLN, StreamVLN, and JanusVLN. Environments are drawn from artist-designed scenes and 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions, filtered for navigable areas exceeding 100 square meters. Instructions are generated by a dual generator-reviewer plus paraphraser multi-agent pipeline with human-in-the-loop verification, yielding 933 collision-aware reference episodes, each paired with one fine-grained instruction and three coarse-grained stylistic variants (formal, natural, casual). Across four models and four embodiments, JanusVLN achieves the highest mean success rate of 43.55% and nDTW of 48.38. In a 20-episode sim-to-real pilot with DualVLN and the Unitree G1, navigation errors correlate strongly (r=0.935), with a mean absolute difference of 0.68m and mean trajectory similarity of 0.782 (+/-0.188) nDTW. These results highlight the interaction between VLN models, controllers, and humanoid embodiments under physical execution. Code, benchmark, and data will be released upon acceptance at https://humanoid-vln.github.io/.

</details>


### [173] [AMR-Pose: An Active LED Marker-Based Relative Pose Estimation Framework With Probabilistic Switching PnP for Cooperative AUVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12866)
*Zeyu Sha,Xiaorui Wang,Mingyang Yang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AMR-Pose，一种基于主动LED标记的相对位姿估计框架，用于自主水下机器人（AUV）之间的可靠相对定位。通过整合紧凑的LED标记模块（一个红色中心LED加三个蓝色外围LED）和概率切换PnP估计器（PSwPnP），在复杂水下光学退化条件下实现鲁棒的六自由度相对位姿估计。水池实验和闭环领航-跟随实验验证了其准确性和实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV之间的可靠相对位姿估计对协同海洋探索、采样和多机器人协调至关重要。然而，水下环境中存在严重的浊度、光照变化、反射和特征间歇性遮挡等光学退化问题，使得基于视觉的相对定位极具挑战性，现有方法难以在复杂水下条件下保持鲁棒性。

Method: 提出的AMR-Pose框架由两部分组成：1）紧凑标记模块，安装在领航AUV上，由一个红色中心LED和三个蓝色外围LED构成，在复杂水下条件下提供独特视觉特征；2）概率切换Perspective-n-Point估计器（PSwPnP），结合SE(3)上的Lie群位姿传播、概率标记关联和可见性自适应测量融合，实现鲁棒的六自由度相对位姿估计。该框架根据标记可见性动态调整估计过程，在部分观测和可见性切换期间保持几何一致性和时间稳定性。

Result: 在水箱实验中，以运动捕捉系统为真值，验证了AMR-Pose在挑战性水下条件下实现了准确、平滑且鲁棒的相对位姿估计。闭环领航-跟随实验进一步证明了该方法在合作水下机器人中用于实时相对位姿反馈的可行性。

Conclusion: AMR-Pose通过主动LED标记与概率切换PnP估计器的组合，有效解决了水下光学退化环境中的相对位姿估计难题，并展示了在真实合作水下机器人系统中的实时应用潜力。

Abstract: Reliable relative pose estimation between autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) is critical for cooperative ocean exploration, sampling, and multi-robot coordination. However, achieving robust vision-based relative localization in underwater environments remains challenging due to severe optical degradation, including turbidity, illumination variations, reflections, and intermittent feature occlusions. This paper presents AMR-Pose, an active LED marker-based relative pose estimation framework for cooperative AUVs. A compact marker module consisting of one red central LED and three blue peripheral LEDs is developed and integrated onto the leader AUV to provide distinctive visual features under complex underwater conditions. Building upon the detected marker observations, a probabilistic switching Perspective-n-Point estimator (PSwPnP) is developed by combining Lie-group pose propagation on $SE(3)$, probabilistic marker association, and visibility-adaptive measurement fusion for robust six-degree-of-freedom relative pose estimation. The proposed framework dynamically adapts the estimation process according to marker visibility, maintaining geometric consistency and temporal stability during partial observations and visibility transitions. Extensive water-tank experiments with motion-capture ground truth validate that AMR-Pose achieves accurate, smooth, and robust relative pose estimation under challenging underwater conditions. Closed-loop leader-follower experiments further demonstrate its feasibility for real-time relative pose feedback in cooperative underwater robotics.

</details>


### [174] [Temporal GRPO: Beyond Trajectory-Level Credit in Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13026)
*Yao Zhou,Hang Gao,Fengge Wu,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal GRPO，通过构造可检测的任务阶段、对齐阶段特定的动作区间，并仅对进入相同阶段的rollout进行比较，解决了GRPO-based VLA后训练中轨迹级信用混叠问题，在RoboTwin 2.0和LIBERO-Long上显著提升任务成功率和采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于GRPO的VLA（视觉-语言-动作）策略后训练中，一个rollout层面的优势被应用于轨迹中的每一个动作。当一个rollout完成了多个有效阶段但在后续阶段失败时，早期阶段产生的有效动作会被错误地惩罚，这被称为轨迹级信用混叠（trajectory-level credit aliasing），导致学习效率低下。

Method: 提出Temporal GRPO方法，核心步骤包括：(1) 构造可检测的任务阶段；(2) 将每个rollout与特定阶段的动作区间对齐；(3) 仅比较进入同一阶段的rollout来计算阶段级优势；(4) 在单次策略更新中将这些阶段优势应用于对应的时间区间。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0基准上，Temporal GRPO提升了任务成功率和采样效率，在不同任务长度上均有一致的性能提升。在LIBERO-Long上的对照更新实验表明，该方法能够保留共享的前置阶段技能，并将改进集中在rollout结果开始分化的首个阶段。

Conclusion: Temporal GRPO通过细粒度的阶段级信用分配，有效解决了轨迹级信用混叠问题，为长视界VLA任务的后训练提供了更精确、更高效的优化信号，是一种优于传统GRPO的VLA后训练方法。

Abstract: Outcome-driven reinforcement learning offers a scalable way to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) policies from sparse task-success feedback. In common GRPO-based VLA post-training, one rollout-level advantage is applied to every action in the trajectory. A rollout that completes several valid stages but fails later can therefore penalize the actions that produced its earlier progress. We call this trajectory-level credit aliasing. Temporal GRPO addresses this problem by constructing detectable task stages, aligning each rollout with stage-specific action intervals, and comparing only rollouts that have entered the same stage. The resulting stage advantages are applied to their corresponding intervals in a single policy update. On RoboTwin 2.0, Temporal GRPO improves task success and sample efficiency, with consistent gains across task horizons. Controlled updates on LIBERO-Long preserve shared prerequisite stages and concentrate improvement at the first stage where rollout outcomes diverge.

</details>


### [175] [H2R-Bench: Benchmarking Human-to-Robot Manipulation Video Generation in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13049)
*Dingyi Rong,Yue Shi,Chaofan Ma,Jiezhang Cao,Zongrui Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Yao Mu,Guangtao Zhai,Ning Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出H2R-Bench基准，用于评估视频世界模型从人类操作视频到机器人的跨具身操作视频生成能力，评测了11个SOTA视频生成模型在6个操作类别和2种机器人具身上的表现，发现当前模型在具身一致性、功能交互和任务执行方面仍存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模操作数据对机器人学习至关重要，但机器人示教数据采集昂贵且难以扩展；人类第一视角操作视频丰富但跨具身迁移困难（人手与机器人末端执行器差异大）。视频世界模型有望从人类观察中合成机器人视角操作视频，但其跨具身迁移能力尚未被系统评估。

Method: 构建H2R-Bench基准：(1) 每个实例包含人类演示视频、目标具身约束和源接地标注（任务目标、动作事件、功能接触、物体响应）；(2) 从五个维度评估生成视频：目标状态完成度、动作事件完成度、功能接触迁移、具身正确性和一般视频质量；(3) 在6个操作类别和2种机器人具身上基准测试11个SOTA视频生成模型。

Result: 评测表明当前视频世界模型在人类到机器人操作迁移方面能力有限，即使是领先模型也经常在具身一致性、功能交互和任务执行上失败。H2R-Bench提供了系统化的诊断框架。

Conclusion: H2R-Bench作为系统化诊断基准，可用于评估视频世界模型能否弥合人类到机器人的具身鸿沟，并将人类操作观察转化为机器人中心的训练资源。

Abstract: Large-scale manipulation data is essential for robot learning, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains expensive and difficult to scale. Meanwhile, abundant egocentric human manipulation videos provide rich behavioral experiences, but transferring them across embodiments remains challenging due to differences between human hands and robotic end-effectors. Recent advances in video world models offer a promising pathway to synthesize robot-centric manipulation videos from human observations, while their cross-embodiment transfer capability remains largely unexplored. Therefore, we introduce H2R-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating cross-embodiment human-to-robot manipulation video generation, where models transform egocentric human demonstrations into robot manipulation videos under specified embodiments. Each benchmark instance contains a human demonstration video, target embodiment constraints, and source-grounded annotations covering task goals, action events, functional contacts, and object responses. H2R-Bench evaluates generated videos through five dimensions, including goal-state completion, action-event completion, functional contact transfer, embodiment correctness, and general video quality. We benchmark eleven state-of-the-art video generation models across six manipulation families and two robot embodiments. Our evaluation reveals that current video world models remain limited in human-to-robot manipulation transfer: even leading models often fail in embodiment consistency, functional interaction, and task execution. H2R-Bench provides a systematic diagnostic framework for evaluating whether video world models can bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap and convert human manipulation observations into robot-centric training resources.

</details>


### [176] [Semantic Radiance Fields as Simulators for Spatial Reasoning in Real-World Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13095)
*Nico Heider,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Katarzyna Wasielewska-Michniewska,Przemysław Hołda,Martin Schieck,Marcin Paprzycki,Maria Ganzha,Bogdan Franczyk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用语义辐射场（SRF）作为具身代理空间推理的模拟器，统一了几何保真与语义可查询性，支持从真实场景重建并用于训练和评估空间推理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练和评估具身代理的空间推理需要既几何保真又语义可查询的多样化环境。合成模拟器提供真值语义但缺乏真实感；基于真实场景重建的模拟器外观真实但默认缺乏语义真值。现有方法无法同时满足这两项要求。

Method: 提出使用语义辐射场（SRF）作为空间推理模拟器。SRF将预训练视觉模型生成的2D语义分割提升为3D辐射场，联合编码几何、外观和逐类语义身份。该表示从真实场景的带位姿RGB图像重建，支持新视角合成、语义查询和自由空间查询，并可接入物理引擎。

Result: 以果园苹果采摘任务为例，展示了SRF驱动的模拟器，辐射场同时提供相机渲染、语义真值以及物理引擎所需的占用查询，验证了该方案的有效性。

Conclusion: SRF能够在单一接地表示中统一几何、外观和语义，高效生成多样化的真实世界环境，用于训练和评估空间推理模型，弥补了合成模拟器与真实重建模拟器之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Training and evaluating spatial reasoning in embodied agents requires diverse environments that are both geometrically faithful and semantically queryable. Synthetic simulators offer ground truth semantics but sacrifice realism; simulators based on reconstructions of real-world environments have realistic appearance but lack ground truth semantics by default. We propose using Semantic Radiance Fields (SRF) as simulators for spatial reasoning agents. SRFs are a representation that unifies these requirements by lifting 2D semantic segmentations from pretrained vision models into a 3D radiance field that jointly encodes geometry, appearance, and per-class semantic identity. The resulting fields are reconstructed from posed RGB captures of real scenes and support novel-view synthesis, semantic and free-space queries within a single grounded representation. This enables the efficient generation of diverse real-world environments to train and evaluate spatial reasoning models. As an example application, we outline an SRF-driven simulator for an orchard apple-reaching task, in which the radiance field supplies camera rendering, semantic ground truth, and occupancy queries to a physics engine.

</details>


### [177] [S2-HWM: Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model for Long-Horizon Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13103)
*Shuzhe Zhang,Xin Zhu,Yinling Qian,Qiong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: S2-HWM是一种稀疏事件结构化分层世界模型，通过从原始潜轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据来协调事件级管理器与原始步执行器，并利用事件转移模型实现变时长预测，在SurRoL手术机器人任务上将成功率提升至98.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程手术机器人操控任务面临奖励稀疏、有意义交互变化间隔不规则的问题。现有世界模型以原始步分辨率进行想象，无法显式建模可变时长的任务进度；手动指定阶段虽能提供中间结构，但其任务特定边界难以与状态相关的交互转换对齐。

Method: S2-HWM包含三个核心组件：(1) 从原始潜轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据，用于调度事件级管理器的目标更新；(2) 每个选定的潜目标条件化原始步执行器的动作直到下一次更新；(3) 学习的稀疏事件形成变时长片段用于事件转移模型(ETM)，该模型预测边界随机状态、片段时长和累积片段奖励，通过链接事件级预测实现超出原始想象视野的变时长延续。

Result: 在SurRoL基础上的PegTransfer任务中，S2-HWM实现了98.7%的成功率，比扁平化的GAS DreamerV3基线高出22.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 自学习的事件证据能够自动发现与状态相关交互转换一致的变时长任务结构，分层世界模型通过事件级抽象有效解决了长时程稀疏奖励操控问题。

Abstract: Long-horizon surgical robot manipulation is challenging because task rewards are sparse, while meaningful interaction changes occur at irregular intervals. Existing world-model agents typically imagine at primitive-step resolution, leaving variable-duration task progress implicit. Manually specified stages can provide intermediate structure, but their task specific boundaries are difficult to align with state-dependent interaction transitions. We propose S2-HWM, a Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model that learns sparse event evidence from primitive latent trajectories to coordinate an event-level manager and a primitive-step worker. The event evidence schedules manager goal updates, and each selected latent goal conditions the worker's primitive actions until the next update. The learned event evidence also forms variable-duration segments for an Event Transition Model (ETM), which predicts the next?boundary stochastic state, segment duration, and accumulated segment reward. Chaining these event-level predictions provides a variable-duration continuation beyond the primitive imagination horizon for manager learning, while the worker retains primitive-step actor-critic learning. On a SurRoL-based PegTransfer task, S2-HWM achieves a success rate of 98.7%, outperforming the flat GAS DreamerV3 baseline by 22.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [178] [FAM-DQ: A Dual-Quadrotor-Based Fully Actuated Aerial Manipulator for High-Torque Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13220)
*Xuwei Yang,Ruoyu Ren,Ziqian Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双四旋翼的全驱动空中机械臂FAM-DQ，通过将两个四旋翼推进模块安装在中央框架两端并使用齿轮驱动的伺服机构调节指向，实现了6自由度解耦的末端执行器控制，具备全向操控能力和增强的扭矩输出，实验验证其最大扭矩达1.019 N·m，扭矩质量比为2.28 N·m/kg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欠驱动空中机械臂存在强位置-姿态耦合问题，而全驱动平台设计常面临结构复杂、有效载荷有限和扭矩输出不足等挑战，难以满足空中物理交互任务对大交互力和扭矩的精确控制需求。

Method: 设计双四旋翼全驱动空中机械臂FAM-DQ，将两个四旋翼推进模块通过被动关节安装于中央框架两端，利用齿轮驱动伺服机构调节指向方向，从而实现6自由度解耦的末端执行器控制、全向操控能力和增强的扭矩输出。

Result: 实验验证了轨迹跟踪、姿态跟踪、静态扭矩测量和螺丝驱动等性能，FAM-DQ最大扭矩达到1.019 N·m，总质量仅0.447 kg，扭矩质量比为2.28 N·m/kg。

Conclusion: FAM-DQ通过创新的双四旋翼架构结合齿轮驱动的设计，成功解决了传统空中机械臂位置-姿态耦合问题，实现了兼具轻量化与高扭矩输出的全向空中物理交互能力。

Abstract: Aerial physical interaction requires aerial manipulation platforms to generate large interaction forces and torques while maintaining precise end-effector control. However, conventional underactuated aerial manipulators suffer from strong position-attitude coupling, whereas fully actuated platform designs often face structural complexity, limited payload capacity, and insufficient torque output. This paper presents FAM-DQ, a dual-quadrotor based fully actuated aerial manipulator designed for high-torque physical interaction tasks. By mounting two quadrotor propulsion modules at the ends of a central frame through passive joints, while using a gear-driven servo to regulate the pointing direction, FAM-DQ achieves decoupled $6$-DoF end-effector control with omnidirectional manipulation capability and enhanced torque output. Experiments including trajectory tracking, attitude tracking, static torque measurement, and screw driving validate the proposed design. FAM-DQ achieves a maximum torque of $1.019~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m}$ with a total mass of $0.447~\mathrm{kg}$, corresponding to a torque-to-mass ratio of $2.28~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m/kg}$.

</details>


### [179] [Manufacturing Complex Airtight Soft Pneumatic Actuators for Soft Robotics: Process Evaluation and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13233)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对多种制造工艺（热缩成型、硅胶浇铸、粉末/液态增材制造、FDM）制造复杂软体气动执行器进行了系统评估，通过工艺筛选、基线制造、失效分析和工艺改进来区分工艺固有限制与可修正缺陷，最终发现FDM因缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步改善而最具适应性，并提出了一种基于制造约束与执行器架构兼容性的设计制造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造复杂软体气动执行器需要同时满足几何保真度、柔顺性、结构完整性和气密性，而现有制造工艺在这些要求之间存在权衡，缺乏系统性的制造导向评估来指导工艺选择。

Method: 对六种制造路线（热缩成型、硅胶浇铸、粉末基和液态基增材制造、FDM）进行四阶段评估：工艺筛选、基线制造、失效分析、工艺改进。对比各工艺的固有限制与可修正缺陷，并考察气密性与挤出路径架构、壁厚及支撑结构的关系。

Result: 热缩成型受限于几何贴合度；浇铸受限于模具可及性和粘接界面；粉末基方法因封闭通道内残留材料受限；数字光处理受限于材料属性和后处理要求。FDM因缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步消除而成为最灵活的路线。气密性不仅取决于名义壁厚还取决于挤出路径架构，无支撑几何在内部后处理受限时至关重要。

Conclusion: 建立了实用的面向制造的设计（design-for-manufacturing）方法，将执行器架构与制造约束的兼容性作为工艺选择的核心依据，为开发复杂、柔顺、气密的软体气动执行器提供实践指导。

Abstract: Manufacturing complex soft pneumatic actuators remains challenging because geometric fidelity, compliance, structural integrity, and airtightness must be achieved simultaneously. This study presents a manufacturing-focused evaluation of several fabrication routes for complex pneumatic structures, including heat-shrink forming, silicone casting, powder- and liquid-based additive manufacturing, and fused deposition modeling (FDM). The processes were assessed through process screening, baseline fabrication, failure analysis, and process improvement to distinguish inherent process limitations from correctable manufacturing defects. Heat-shrink forming was limited by geometric conformity, casting by mold accessibility and bonded interfaces, powder-based methods by residual material trapped within enclosed passages, and digital light processing by the material properties and post-processing requirements of the investigated system. FDM provided the most adaptable route because its dominant defects could be progressively reduced through process optimization. The results further showed that airtightness depends not only on nominal wall thickness but also on extrusion-path architecture, while support-free geometry is important when access for internal post-processing is limited. These findings establish a practical design-for-manufacturing approach in which process selection is guided by the compatibility between actuator architecture and manufacturing constraints. The proposed approach provides practical guidance for developing complex, flexible, and airtight soft pneumatic actuators for soft robotic applications

</details>


### [180] [Predictive Relative-Velocity Steering for Safe Robotic Manipulator Teleoperation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13284)
*Changhao Hu,Zeyi Liu,Songqiao Hu,Shuang Liu,Zihan Meng,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级、模块化的主动防碰撞框架，在末端执行器速度指令层面运行。通过基于碰撞时间（TTC）的预测模块和Rodrigues旋转公式偏转相对速度矢量，在保持速度大小不变的情况下改变方向，有效避免了死锁问题，仿真和物理实验均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作机器人虽能实现类人灵巧运动，但人类操作员在网络延迟或注意力受限的情况下，难以及时有效地躲避突然出现的障碍物，存在安全风险。现有方法（如基于人工势场APF的方法）存在死锁问题，且复杂遥操作管线中的点云处理延迟会影响防碰撞的及时性。

Method: 该方法是一个轻量级模块化框架，直接作用于末端执行器速度指令层。首先对点云进行预处理，然后基于碰撞时间（TTC）并结合超调保护来预测潜在碰撞，随后使用Rodrigues旋转公式对相对速度矢量进行偏转。偏转仅改变相对速度方向而保持其大小不变，从而缓解APF方法的死锁问题。预测模块补偿了复杂遥操作管线中的点云处理延迟，轻量设计支持高频控制。

Result: 在多种仿真场景中，该方法比基线方法实现了更高的末端执行器碰撞避免率；在物理机器人系统上的实验进一步验证了其防碰撞有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过在速度指令层进行轻量级主动防碰撞，能够在遥操作场景中有效应对突发障碍物，兼顾实时性与安全性，且优于传统基线方法，适用于实际物理系统。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled robotic manipulators to perform dexterous, human-arm-like motions. However, human operators may fail to avoid suddenly appearing obstacles promptly and effectively, particularly under network latency or limited attention, thereby creating safety risks. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight and modular framework for proactive collision avoidance, operating directly at the end-effector velocity-command level. After preprocessing the point cloud, the framework first predicts potential collisions based on time-to-collision (TTC) with integrated overshoot protection, and subsequently rotates the relative-velocity vector using Rodrigues' rotation formula. The deflection changes only the direction of the relative velocity while preserving its magnitude, thereby mitigating the deadlock problem commonly encountered by conventional artificial potential field (APF) methods. The prediction module compensates for point-cloud processing latency introduced by complex teleoperation pipelines, while the lightweight design enables the high-frequency control required for teleoperation. Simulations across diverse scenarios show that the proposed method achieves a higher end-effector collision avoidance rate than the baseline methods. Experiments on a physical robotic system further validate its collision-avoidance effectiveness.

</details>


### [181] [NestDex: Nested Policy Learning with Copilot Assisted Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13362)
*James Zhao,Jinhe Tang,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NestDex是一种嵌套策略学习框架，通过让操作员用单自由度离合器控制手臂并调节预学习的手部技能而非直接指定手指轨迹，降低了灵巧操作演示采集的难度，并训练出可同时控制手臂和手的独立外层策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作学习受限于一致、完整任务的演示数据难以采集。与平行夹爪操作不同，灵巧任务要求操作员在全过程中协调手臂运动与精细、接触丰富的手指行为，直接指定完整手指轨迹负担过重。

Method: NestDex采用嵌套策略框架：内层手部策略根据最新的本体感觉历史自适应调整动作，视觉-语言选择器为每个任务阶段激活合适的技能，操作员通过单自由度离合器调节活动的手部技能；手部动作变分自编码器提供紧凑的手部动作目标，同时将手臂指令保留在关节空间中；生成的演示用于训练独立的外层视觉运动策略，该策略在部署时同时控制手臂和手而无需内层策略。

Result: 在真实世界灵巧操作实验中，NestDex提高了演示的可靠性与效率，经验评估支持有效的自主策略学习。

Conclusion: NestDex通过将灵巧操作演示采集从直接控制全部手指关节转变为调控预学习的技能集合，显著降低了数据收集负担，并实现了有效的自主策略学习。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation promises substantially richer robot interaction with the physical world, but learning these behaviours remains constrained by the difficulty of collecting consistent, complete-task demonstrations. Unlike parallel-jaw manipulation, dexterous tasks require the operator to coordinate arm motion with precise, contact-rich finger behaviour throughout the task. We introduce NestDex, a nested policy-learning framework that reduces this burden by using learned hand skills to assist demonstration collection. The operator controls the arm and regulates the active hand skill through a single-DoF clutch, rather than directly specifying the full finger trajectory. The inner hand policy adapts its motion from the latest proprioceptive history, while a vision-language selector activates the appropriate skill for each task stage. The resulting demonstrations train a separate outer visuomotor policy that controls both the arm and hand without the inner policies at deployment. A hand-action variational autoencoder provides compact hand-action targets while retaining arm commands in joint space. Across real-world dexterous manipulation experiments, NestDex improves demonstration reliability and efficiency, and the resulting empirical evaluations support effective autonomous policy learning. Video Demo are available at project website https://aus.bot/research/nestdex.

</details>


### [182] [FIRE-VLA: Failure-Informed Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13395)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIRE-VLA是一个失败驱动的自进化框架，通过将GRPO中无法解决的失败转化为特权监督信号，利用冻结的回合初始模型副本进行自我蒸馏，在保持采样式评估的同时显著降低自动驾驶VLA模型的规划误差和失败发生率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在组内相对奖励下学习，当所有采样轨迹都较差时，相对信号只能对失败排序，无法识别失败区域之外的错误行为，缺乏超出现有失败分布的能力。

Method: 当低奖励、低多样性的组出现时，触发从冻结的回合初始模型副本进行自我蒸馏。教师和学生参数规模相同，但教师能观测隐藏的未来轨迹；监督信号遵循学生的生成前缀且仅限制在答案token上，同时GRPO对所有组保持激活。更新后的策略成为下一轮的教师，使路由的失败分布随策略变化，无需外部更大的教师。

Result: 在6,019个来自150个nuScenes保留场景的样本上，FIRE-VLA保持可比较的单样本规划性能，将G=4的平均L2误差从1.848降至1.500米，评估持续失败率从13.03%降至11.20%。平均误差的降低主要来源于罕见的严重轨迹而非普通轨迹的均匀改善。

Conclusion: FIRE-VLA证明将未解决的失败转化为同规模教师的自蒸馏监督，可以有效引导策略离开失败区域，是一种无需外部大模型即可持续进化的高效VLA训练范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning improves autonomous-driving vision-language-action (VLA) models by evaluating trajectories sampled from the current policy. Group relative policy optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within each rollout group. When all sampled trajectories are poor, this relative signal can rank failures without identifying behavior outside the failed region. We introduce FIRE-VLA, a failure-informed self-evolution framework that converts such unresolved failures into privileged supervision for the next policy. Low-reward, low-diversity groups trigger self-distillation from a frozen round-start copy of the same model. Teacher and student have the same parameter scale, but only the teacher observes the hidden future trajectory. Supervision follows the student's generated prefix and is restricted to answer tokens, while GRPO remains active for every group. The updated policy supplies the teacher for the next round, allowing the routed failure distribution to change with the policy without requiring a larger external teacher. Starting from the same Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT checkpoint, the comparison matches student rollout and policy-update counts. On 6,019 examples from 150 held-out nuScenes scenes, FIRE-VLA retains comparable single-sample planning, reduces G=4 mean L2 from 1.848 to 1.500 m, and lowers evaluation-persistent failure prevalence from 13.03% to 11.20%. The reduction in mean error arises mainly from rare severe rollouts rather than uniform improvement across ordinary trajectories.

</details>


### [183] [Capstan-driven Continuum Surgical Robot: Design, Modeling, and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13396)
*Gang Zhang,Yufu Qiu,Junyan Yan,Wenhui Zeng,Wenlong Lu,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"驱动-感知协同设计"（actuation-perception co-design）理念的集成设计-建模-感知框架，通过在电机安装支架中引入柔性元件实现实时缆索张力测量，并基于多体短厚梁模型的并行计算框架实现连续体手术机器人的实时形态与力感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形状和力感知一直是紧凑型绞盘驱动连续体手术机器人发展的关键瓶颈，主要难点在于在受限的绞盘组件内获取缆索张力信息。

Method: 采用设计-建模-感知一体化方法：(1) 在驱动系统电机安装支架中引入柔性元件，利用缆索反作用力产生微变形实现实时张力测量，不占用紧凑绞盘空间；(2) 提出基于多体短厚梁模型的并行计算框架，捕获短梁段剪切效应及多缆协同交互，实现实时性能；(3) 结合近端多轴力/力矩传感器作为额外测量锚点，实现稳定的形状和力感知。

Result: 在单段和双段构型的连续体机器人上进行了实验验证，实现了实时尖端位姿估计以及接触力和接触位置感知。

Conclusion: 该框架在不牺牲紧凑绞盘结构的前提下实现了缆索张力反馈，使集成感知在绞盘驱动连续体手术机器人上成为可能。

Abstract: Shape and force sensing have long been critical bottlenecks in the development of compact capstan-driven continuum surgical robots, primarily due to the difficulty of obtaining cable tension information within the confined capstan assembly. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents an integrated design-modeling-sensing approach based on the concept of actuation-perception co-design. A compliant element is introduced into the motor mounting bracket of the drive system, enabling micro-deformation under the cable reaction force and thereby allowing real-time cable tension measurement without occupying the compact capstan space. To address the modeling complexity arising from unconventional joint configurations introduced by the spatial cable routing strategy, a parallel computation framework based on a multibody short-thick-beam model is proposed, which captures shear effects in short beam segments and synergistic multi-cable interactions while achieving real-time performance. Building on this framework, stable shape and force sensing is achieved by incorporating a proximal multi-axis force/torque sensor as an additional measurement anchor. Following this design-modeling-sensing framework, capstan-driven continuum surgical robots with single- and dual-segment configurations are developed. Experimental results validate the proposed framework in both single- and dual-segment continuum robots, demonstrating real-time tip pose estimation together with contact force and location perception. By enabling cable tension feedback without compromising the compact capstan architecture, the proposed framework makes integrated perception feasible for capstan-driven continuum surgical robots.

</details>


### [184] [Deliberate Practice: Learning Robot Skills under a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13415)
*Shivam Vats,Sudarshan Harithas,Mete Tuluhan Akbulut,Arvind Raghunathan,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为Deliberate Practice (DP)的主动技能学习算法，用于在有限练习预算下自动学习机器人技能。该算法通过双线性规划精确计算预算最优的技能练习分配方案，使机器人在长时程操作任务中最大化累积奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实机器人场景中，练习技能的时间和资源通常是有限的（即小练习预算），而顺序任务需要学习多个技能。现有方法未能系统地考虑如何在这种预算约束下最优地分配练习时间——既需要兼顾技能的"可学性"（是否能在预算内掌握），又需要考虑技能解锁的任务计划所能带来的累积奖励，这是一个组合优化难题。

Method: DP算法首先估计每个技能的掌握时间和其解锁任务计划的累积奖励；随后构建一个双线性规划（bilinear program）来精确求解预算最优的技能练习分配方案，该问题可借助现成的优化求解器直接获得最优解；最后通过模拟和真实世界的长时程操作任务实验验证其有效性。

Result: 在模拟仿真和真实机器人长时程操作任务中，DP方法能够在有限练习预算下最优地分配练习时间，获得对任务规划有用的策略，并显著改善长时程规划的绩效。实验验证了其预算最优性以及在实际场景中的可行性。

Conclusion: Deliberate Practice提供了一种可证明预算最优的主动技能学习框架，将技能练习分配问题转化为可精确求解的双线性规划，为机器人在资源受限条件下高效学习顺序任务技能提供了理论保障和实用方案。

Abstract: We consider the problem of autonomously learning robot skills under a limited practice budget for sequential tasks. We propose an active skill learning algorithm, \emph{Deliberate Practice (DP)}, that computes a provably \emph{budget-optimal} allocation---practicing skills that maximize expected cumulative reward while being learnable within the budget. DP estimates both the time needed to master skills and the cumulative reward of the task plans that the skills unlock. Computing a budget-optimal allocation is challenging as it requires reasoning about combinatorially many skill plans over a large practice budget. Our key contribution is a bilinear program that can compute this exactly using off-the-shelf solvers. Through simulated and real-world experiments on long-horizon manipulation tasks, we show that our approach allows robots to optimally use limited practice time to acquire useful policies and improve long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [185] [Attention from Action, for Action: Emergent Visual Bottlenecks for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13422)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nick Heppert,Johannes Fabian Hahn,Abhinav Valada,Florian T. Pokorny,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Seeker是一种从动作中学习注意力的任务与状态条件化读出模块，利用冻结的DINOv3特征迭代更新查询，生成任务进展感知的感兴趣区域（ROI），无需外部空间标注，显著提升视觉运动学习的数据效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ROI接口常依赖外部空间标签（如注视点、目标类别、可供性标注），而免标签方案（如动作衍生裁剪）虽无需额外标注，但编码了关于事件时序、代理点和裁剪尺度的固定选择；当控制所需的视觉证据偏离末端执行器或随任务进展连续变化时，这些裁剪会失准。

Method: Seeker从冻结的DINOv3特征出发，通过迭代更新查询并汇聚视觉证据，仅凭动作监督生成任务进展感知的ROI。该ROI作为空间接口，用于RGB裁剪、掩码引导的背景增强以及点云过滤。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界实验中，Seeker较无裁剪、增强和动作衍生裁剪基线显著提升数据效率与鲁棒性。真实机器人上，平均域内成功率从最佳基线的48.3%提升至76.7%，光照/背景变化下的成功率从20.0%提升至60.0%。

Conclusion: Seeker证明仅通过动作监督即可学习任务感知的注意力区域，避免了外部标注依赖，同时克服了固定裁剪在时序和空间上的局限性，在域内性能与域偏移鲁棒性方面均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Visual bottlenecks that focus policy inputs on regions of interest (ROIs) can improve data-efficient visuomotor learning by separating where to look from how to act. Many ROI interfaces rely on external spatial labels, such as gaze, object classes, or affordance annotations. Label-free alternatives often derive crops from trajectories by detecting gripper or motion events and centering a fixed crop at the projected end-effector. Such action-derived crops are useful spatial priors that require no additional labels, but they encode fixed choices about event timing, proxy points, and crop scale. When the visual evidence needed for control lies away from the end-effector or changes continuously with task progress, these crops can become misaligned. We propose Seeker, a task- and state-conditioned readout that learns attention from action. Starting from frozen DINOv3 features, Seeker iteratively updates a query with gathered visual evidence, producing progression-aware ROIs solely from action supervision. The learned ROI serves as a spatial interface for RGB cropping, mask-guided background augmentation, and point-cloud filtering. In simulation and the real world, Seeker improves data efficiency and robustness over no-crop, augmentation, and action-derived crop baselines. On real robots, Seeker raises average in-domain success from the best baseline's 48.3% to 76.7% and success under lighting/background shifts from 20.0% to 60.0%.

</details>


### [186] [ContactGuard: Pre-Contact Execution Monitoring with Action-Conditioned Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13438)
*Gehan Zheng,Matthew Johnson-Roberson,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ContactGuard，一种接触前执行监控器，用于检测 chunked visuomotor 策略在接触前可能导致的失败。它通过训练潜在世界模型预测策略动作块在多视角视觉嵌入空间中的短期后果，并利用轻量级失败探针在接触事件前判定失败并中止执行，无需修改底层策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务中，失败往往在机器人与物体发生实际接触后才被发现，尤其在腕部相机设置下，糟糕的接近动作可能在传统检测器反应之前就已推挤、错失、滑动或扰动物体。现有方法缺乏在接触前预测失败的能力。

Method: ContactGuard 包含两个核心组件：(1) 潜在世界模型，从无标注的机器人轨迹中训练，用于在计划动作下预测紧凑的多视角视觉嵌入（而非像素级视频预测）；(2) 轻量级失败探针，从小规模标注的接触前片段中训练。部署时，ContactGuard 在即将发生的接触事件前锚定预测，在策略自身动作下前向滚动世界模型，并验证预测的接触后潜在表示来判断是否失败。

Result: 在真实世界的接触丰富操作任务中，ContactGuard 相比直接预测和损坏动作消融基线具有更高的失败预测准确率，并能作为接触前中止信号迁移到真实机器人上，且无需修改底层策略。

Conclusion: ContactGuard 通过潜在空间世界模型实现了接触前失败预测与中止，有效弥补了接触后检测的滞后性，为接触丰富操作提供了一种无需调整底层策略的即插即用安全监控方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation failures are often detected only after the robot has committed to contact. This is especially limiting in wrist-camera setups: close gripper--object views help observe contact, but a poor approach may already push, miss, slip, or disturb the object before conventional detectors react. We introduce \emph{ContactGuard}, a pre-contact execution monitor for chunked visuomotor policies. Given the policy's planned action chunk, ContactGuard predicts its short-horizon consequence in latent visual space and aborts if the predicted future latent indicates likely failure. Its latent world model is trained from unlabelled robot trajectories to predict compact multi-view visual embeddings under planned actions, avoiding pixel-level video prediction. A lightweight failure probe is then trained from a small labelled set of pre-contact clips. At deployment, ContactGuard anchors prediction before an imminent contact event, rolls the model forward under the policy's own actions, and verifies the predicted post-contact latent. Across real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ContactGuard predicts failure more accurately than direct and corrupted-action ablations, and transfers to live robot as a pre-contact abort signal without modifying the underlying policy.

</details>


### [187] [Mind the Context: Continual Learning of Socially Appropriate Robot Actions via Environmental-Social Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13448)
*Rafal Robert Karpinski,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Nikhil Churamani,Yiming Luo,Maartje M. A. de Graaf,Davide Dell'Anna,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出显式解耦双分支（EDD）框架，用于社交机器人的领域增量持续学习，显式分离环境知识与社交主体知识，并通过基于回放的排练机制缓解遗忘，使机器人能在多种室内场景中学习适当的动作（如清洁、服务、开启对话）。实验表明EDD优于多个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人需要在多样环境中运行，相似布局可能意味着不同的社会适当行为（如在拥挤的家庭中开启对话可接受，但在办公室会议中则具干扰性）。这些规范和场景无法预先全部预见，机器人需要持续学习以适应序列经验并保留已有知识。然而先前工作未针对领域增量设置进行研究——机器人在该设置下逐渐遇到多样上下文（如客厅、会议室、办公室、走廊），环境线索（空间是否开放或堆满家具）与社交线索（人或智能体如何围绕机器人布置）共同塑造动作的适当性。

Method: EDD框架显式将环境相关知识与社交主体相关知识分离为两个分支，并采用基于重放的排练机制来缓解灾难性遗忘，同时学习机器人动作（如清洁、服务、开启对话）在多个室内领域中的适当性。

Result: 实验表明EDD优于多个最先进基线，消融研究进一步评估了不同的解耦策略以及对领域排序的敏感性。

Conclusion: EDD框架填补了社交机器人领域增量持续学习的空白，通过显式解耦环境与社交知识并借助重放机制，实现了在跨室内场景中的有效知识保留与适应。代码已公开。

Abstract: Social robots are expected to operate across diverse environments, where similar arrangements can imply different socially appropriate actions, e.g., starting a conversation may be acceptable in a crowded home but disruptive in an office meeting. Because such norms and environments cannot all be anticipated in advance, robots require continual learning (CL) to adapt from sequential experience while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Prior work has studied CL for generating socially appropriate robot actions, but it has not addressed domain-incremental settings in which the robot incrementally encounters diverse contexts (e.g., living room, meeting room, office, hallway), where both environmental (e.g., whether the space is open or cluttered with furniture) and social cues (e.g., how people or other agents are positioned around the robot) jointly shape the appropriateness of robot actions. We address this gap with the Explicit Disentanglement Dual-Branch (EDD) framework. EDD explicitly separates environmental and social-agent related knowledge and uses replay-based rehearsal to mitigate forgetting while learning the appropriateness of robot actions (e.g., cleaning, serving, starting a conversation) across several indoor domains. Experiments show that EDD outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines, and ablation studies further evaluate different disentanglement strategies and the sensitivity to domain ordering. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Cambridge-AFAR/Mind-the-Context.git.

</details>


### [188] [Decoding Task Progress from VLA Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13474)
*Atiksh Bhardwaj,Edward Weiyi Duan,Prithwish Dan,Wei-Chiu Ma,Preston Culbertson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用机械可解释性技术，对视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）π0.5的残差流进行线性探测，发现"任务进度"（归一化的轨迹剩余时间）可从激活中线性读出，且该信号在预训练的PaliGemma骨干中已存在。该线性探针可泛化到未见任务、对语言反事实敏感，虽不能有效操控策略，但可作为无标签OOD检测器用于监测部署中的VLA，与SOTA方法竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型正快速部署为通用操作策略，但缺乏理解其内部表征和运行时监控的基本工具。作者希望借助机械可解释性的思路，探究VLA内部是否编码了可读的语义量（如任务进度），从而为部署中的策略监控提供轻量级、可解释的路径。

Method: 对π0.5的残差流进行线性探测（linear probing），验证任务进度信号的可线性读出性；在预训练PaliGemma骨干上检查该信号是否在机器人数据训练前即存在；训练单一线探针验证其对未见任务的泛化及对语言反事实的敏感性；将探针作为标签无关的OOD检测器，检测停滞的任务进度，并与SOTA方法对比。

Result: 发现任务进度信号在π0.5激活中线性可读；该信号在PaliGemma预训练骨干中已存在；单线性探针可泛化至未见任务，并在多提示数据上训练时对语言反事实产生变化，但无法实现有意义策略操控；作为OOD检测器，能有效检测停滞的任务进度，性能与SOTA方法相当。

Conclusion: VLA模型含有丰富的、线性可读的内部语义表征（如任务进度）；学习读取这些信号为监测部署中的视觉运动策略提供了轻量、可解释的途径，对模型监控和安全性有重要启示。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are moving rapidly towards deployment as general-purpose manipulation policies, but we currently lack basic tools for understanding what these models represent internally or for monitoring them at runtime. Leveraging ideas from mechanistic interpretability, we probe the residual stream of $π_{0.5}$ and find that task progress, the normalized time remaining in a trajectory, is linearly readable from the activations. We find that this signal is present in the pretrained PaliGemma backbone prior to training on any robot-specific data. A single linear probe generalizes to unseen tasks and varies under language counterfactuals when trained on multi-prompt data, but does not enable meaningful steering of the policy. These properties make the signal directly useful for instrumenting deployed VLAs. We use the probe as a simple label-free OOD detector, which detects stalled task progress, and find it competitive with state-of-the-art methods. Our results suggest that VLAs have rich, linearly readable internal representations of semantic quantities like task progress, and that learning to read these signals offers a lightweight, interpretable path toward monitoring deployed visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [189] [A Browser-Native Digital Test Range for Benchmarking 4D Ocean-Glider Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13511)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种引导式、免安装的浏览器原生数字试验场，可将选定海域转化为可复现的四维海洋滑翔机规划器评估实验环境，并验证了其在多规划器对比评估中的有效性与可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋滑翔机规划器的重复原位评估需要稀缺的载具、操作人员、布放与回收资源，且海洋条件无法为竞争算法重置，导致评估成本高、可复现性差。

Method: 构建浏览器原生数字试验场，提供从区域选择、任务级测深、时间/深度强迫、科学目标设定、任务分解、航线规划、流场平流执行、观测量生成到评分的全流程引导；核心贡献是统一的"规划-观测"契约，将载具、传感和评估器假设统一于手动航线、内置算法及导入的外部规划器输出，导出工件形成数据集级记录。

Result: 受控OSSE实验评估5种经典规划器，在2个场景、3个确定性种子和标定的60小时视界下完成全部54个任务无硬性违规；规划器排名与潜航策略效应揭示了操作性-科学性权衡。公开部署提供了场外审计以界定当前运动学边界；源锁定GliderFlight 1.2.0通过Pyodide/WebAssembly实现原生到浏览器的等价性。

Conclusion: 该数字试验场提供了科学可追溯、组件合格化的任务级部署前实验操作空间，为海洋滑翔机规划器评估建立了可复现、免安装的标准化通道。

Abstract: Repeated in-situ evaluation of ocean-glider planners requires scarce vehicles, operators, deployment and recovery resources, and ocean conditions that cannot be reset for competing algorithms. We present a guided, installation-free browser-native digital test range that transforms a selected region into a reproducible four-dimensional experiment. The system leads users from regional domain selection through mission-scoped bathymetry, time/depth forcing, science objectives, optional task decomposition, route specification, current-advected execution, observation generation, and scoring. Its primary contribution is a common plan-to-observation contract unifying vehicle, sensing, and evaluator assumptions across manual routes, transparent built-in algorithms, and imported classical or learned-planner outputs, while exported artifacts form dataset-ready records. A controlled Observing System Simulation Experiment (OSSE) evaluates five classical planners in two episodes, three deterministic seeds, and a calibrated 60-hour horizon. All 54 missions completed and recovered without hard violations, while planner rankings and dive-policy effects revealed operational-scientific tradeoffs. An authentic public deployment supplied a field-referenced audit to scope current kinematic boundaries. Separately, source-locked GliderFlight 1.2.0 achieved native-to-browser parity through Pyodide/WebAssembly, establishing a pathway for high-fidelity multi-tier simulation. The resulting operational space is scientifically traceable and component-qualified for mission-scale pre-deployment experimentation.

</details>


### [190] [HumanTracker: Towards Comprehensive and Human-Aligned Motion Tracking Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13555)
*Dairu Liu,Zekun Qi,Jiayu Zeng,Ruixi Yu,Yu Guan,Yintianrun Zhang,Xuchuan Chen,Sikai Liang,Zekai Li,Chenghuai Lin,Xinqiang Yu,Wenyao Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanTracker基准和HumanScore评价指标，用于使类人运动追踪评估既与人类感知对齐又可规模化扩展。基准包含约153小时的专业表演者光学运动轨迹，按四个运动族组织并配有文本标签；HumanScore是基于12K运动对（24K运动）训练的对齐人类偏好的度量，能比传统运动学指标更好地预测人类偏好并揭示接触与稳定性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类人运动追踪评估与人类视频感知不一致：运动学误差仅平均帧级位姿差异，遗漏了最重要的物理伪影（如支撑不稳定、脚部滑动、触地时机错误等不正确接触）；同时现有测试集规模小且缺乏多样性，难以覆盖接触密集、长时程行为。

Method: 1) 构建HumanTracker基准：收集约153小时多专业表演者的光学运动轨迹，组织为四个运动家族并配文本标签以支持细粒度诊断；2) 提出HumanScore指标：在12K运动对（包含24K运动）上训练偏好对齐度量，用于替代传统运动学指标进行评估。

Result: 在多种代表性最先进追踪器上的实验表明，HumanScore能更好地预测人类偏好，并能揭示传统运动学指标常常遗漏的接触和稳定性失败。

Conclusion: HumanTracker与HumanScore为类人运动追踪评估提供了更接近人类感知且可扩展的解决方案，弥补了传统运动学指标在物理伪影检测方面的不足。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking is central to teleoperation and whole-body imitation, yet evaluation often disagrees with what people perceive in videos. Kinematic errors average per-frame pose differences but miss the physical artifacts that matter most, particularly unstable support and incorrect contacts such as foot skating and mistimed touch-downs. Meanwhile, widely used test suites are small and lack the diversity needed to stress contact-rich, long-horizon behaviors. We introduce HumanTracker to make humanoid tracking evaluation both perceptually aligned and scalable. The HumanTracker benchmark contains approximately 153 hours of optical motion trajectories from multiple professional performers, organized into four motion families with text labels for fine-grained diagnosis. We further propose HumanScore, a preference-aligned metric trained on 12K motion pairs containing 24K motions. Across representative state-of-the-art trackers, HumanScore better predicts human preferences and reveals contact and stability failures that kinematic metrics often miss.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [From Observation to Intervention: Memory in Brains and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12377)
*Morteza Salehjahromi,Shayan A. Zadegan,Amgad Muneer,Jia Wu*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出大脑与大语言模型（LLM）可通过共同的功能性问题（信息表征、部分线索恢复、新信息写入/更新、状态扰动）进行比较，而非依赖解剖结构对应。两者的核心差异在于实验可及性：LLM虽缺乏情境化记忆，但允许对内部状态进行直接、可重复的操作，这为将广泛的记忆问题转化为更精确的生物假说提供了新机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑和LLM是两种根本不同的记忆系统，但缺乏系统性的功能比较框架。人类研究虽揭示了稀疏概念响应、时间绑定、快速联想形成等记忆现象，但选择性干预受限；啮齿类研究虽提供因果通路却只能触及广泛回路。作者希望利用LLM具备的独特实验可及性，弥合生物记忆研究与人工智能记忆研究之间的鸿沟，将LLM作为生成更精确生物学假说的工具。

Method: 本文采用功能比较与实验逻辑转移的方法论。通过界定五个共有的功能性记忆问题（表征位置、部分线索恢复、信息写入/更新、状态扰动），将生物系统（突触、神经元集群、海马-皮层交互、可塑性）与LLM系统（权重、激活、上下文窗口、检索系统、外部存储）逐一映射对照，并比较人类、啮齿类及LLM三种对象在记忆干预上的实验可及性差异。

Result: 作者指出LLM并非在记忆能力本身占优，而是在实验可及性方面占优：其对内部状态和信息存储提供非同寻常的直接、可重复操作能力。这一不对称性构成了新的研究机会，使LLM的工具能够帮助将关于检索、更新、持续性、可逆性及非预期效应的广泛记忆问题转化为更锐利的生物学假说。

Conclusion: 大脑与LLM之间富有成效的桥梁在于转移实验逻辑而非解剖结构。LLM可作为一种新型实验平台，推动记忆研究的假设生成与验证，但其角色定位是辅助工具而非替代生物模型。

Abstract: Brains and large language models (LLMs) are fundamentally different memory systems, but they can be compared through shared functional questions: where memory-related information is represented, how partial cues recover broader associations, how new information is written or updated, and how memory-related states can be perturbed. In biological systems, these questions span synapses, neuronal ensembles, hippocampal-cortical interactions, and plasticity; in LLMs, they span weights, activations, context windows, retrieval systems, and external stores. The comparison is therefore functional and experimental rather than anatomical. Human studies reveal sparse concept responses, temporal binding, rapid association formation, episode-specific coding, and recall-related reactivation, but selective intervention remains limited. Rodent studies provide more selective causal access to learning-related ensembles, whereas human and macaque interventions usually affect broader circuits. LLMs lack lived episodic memory, yet they permit unusually direct and repeatable manipulation of internal states and stored information. We argue that this asymmetry creates a new opportunity. LLMs are not ahead in memory itself, but in experimental access. Their tools may help turn broad questions about retrieval, updating, persistence, reversibility, and unintended effects into sharper biological hypotheses. The productive bridge is to transfer experimental logic, not anatomical parts.

</details>


### [192] [A Bayes-Markov Neuromorphic Model of Cortical Orientation Selectivity: A Computational Re-implementation and Quantitative Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12388)
*Abolfazl Moslemi,Milad Sarabadani,Fatemeh Sefidian,Hossein Peyvandi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文对Shirazi提出的贝叶斯-马尔可夫模型进行了计算显式地重实现与定量仿真研究，验证了V1方向选择性可以从非定向LGN输入中通过概率推断产生，并提供了速率编码和脉冲编码两种实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V1初级视觉皮层方向选择性的涌现机制是计算神经科学的核心问题之一。Shirazi的贝叶斯-马尔可夫模型提出了一个概率解释框架，但该框架缺乏明确的计算实现和定量验证，需要重建数学模型并进行系统仿真来评估其有效性。

Method: 重建贝叶斯-马尔可夫数学模型的完整推导，描述完全由LGN驱动的新型更新规则，实现向量化仿真框架以保留原本的局部团操作并支持系统参数扫描。评估通过方向调谐曲线、方向选择性指数（OSI）、受控LGN噪声扰动、对比度测试和模型变体对比进行。此外，使用泄漏积分发放和Hodgkin-Huxley神经元构建脉冲SCI层实现，检验速率编码SCI场能否通过时间显式神经活动表达。

Result: 仿真支持贝叶斯-马尔可夫框架的核心定性行为：产生锐利的方向选择性，对中等LGN噪声具有鲁棒性，并成功实现了推断抑制场的时间显式脉冲编码证明。

Conclusion: 该研究为贝叶斯-马尔可夫模型提供了可重复的计算实现和定量验证，确认了局部概率推断机制足以从非定向输入中涌现方向选择性，且该抑制场可由生物可解释的脉冲神经元活动实现。

Abstract: The emergence of orientation selectivity in the primary visual cortex (V1) remains a central question in computational neuroscience. Shirazi's Bayes-Markov model proposed a probabilistic explanation for how orientation-selective inhibition can arise from non-oriented lateral geniculate nucleus (LGN) inputs through local inference. In that formulation, the activity pattern of striate cortical inhibitory (SCI) cells is estimated from the LGN activity pattern by a maximum a posteriori (MAP) criterion over a two-layer hierarchical Markov random field, and the resulting inference is implemented through a local parallel relaxation algorithm. We provide a computationally explicit re-implementation and quantitative simulation study of this framework. We reconstruct the mathematical model, describe its fully LGN-driven update rule, and implement a vectorized simulation framework that preserves the original local clique operations while making systematic parameter sweeps feasible. We evaluate the model using orientation tuning curves, an orientation selectivity index (OSI), controlled LGN noise perturbations, contrast tests, and model-variant comparisons. We further add a spiking SCI-layer realization using leaky integrate-and-fire and Hodgkin-Huxley neurons to examine whether the rate-coded SCI field can be expressed through temporally explicit neural activity. The simulations support the central qualitative behavior of the Bayes-Markov framework: sharp orientation selectivity, robustness to moderate LGN noise, and a biologically interpretable proof-of-concept spiking realization of the inferred inhibitory field.

</details>


### [193] [Evaluation Resolution Confounds Learning-Rule Comparisons in Model-Brain RSA of Early Visual Cortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12408)
*Nils Leutenegger*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个关键的实验设置问题：在表征相似性分析（RSA）中，用来评估卷积网络与脑相似性的图像分辨率会强烈影响结论。在32px训练分辨率下，未训练或局部训练的网络的V1表征与反向传播训练的网络相当甚至更优，但在224px分辨率下，V1差距显著扩大并呈单调增长趋势，缺口主要由图像细节而非池化导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RSA研究因生物似合理的规则（如反馈对齐、预测编码、STDP）无法在大规模训练，通常在小网络和小图像（32x32 CIFAR）上训练，再与高分辨率的脑响应数据比较。这种训练/评估分辨率不匹配可能系统性地扭曲关于学习规则如何产生类脑表征的结论。

Method: 在六个分辨率（32px至224px）下系统评估未训练/局部训练/反向传播训练网络的V1-FMRI相似性；测试四种候选机制（分辨率匹配、低层Gabor与像素结构、未训练基线的归一化状态、全局亮度统计量的收敛），并使用保持卷积权重逐位一致的干预实验排除三种机制；另设计实验在保持训练分辨率的同时将池化位置扩增12倍，以分离图像细节与池化对效应的贡献。

Result: 未训练网络与反向传播网络的V1差距从32px时的-0.001±0.007变为224px时的+0.044±0.006，在六种分辨率上单调增长（n=5 seeds）。该效应在人脑fMRI、单种子猕猴电生理、训练轨迹以及ImageNet ResNet-50和Swin-Tiny transformer中均成立。五种候选机制中有四种被排除；将图像细节限制在训练分辨率而池化位置扩增12倍时，效应消失约90%，表明依赖的是图像细节而非池化。此外，单标量亮度值达到rho=0.075的V1 RDM相关性，几乎与未训练网络（0.076）持平，限制了该比较范式的分辨率上限。唯一跨分辨率稳定的学习效应是LOC区域中反向传播优于未训练网络。

Conclusion: 评估分辨率是RSA比较中一个被忽视但核心的混杂因素。当前的"小网络+小图像"范式可能严重低估反向传播相对于简单/生物似合理规则在V1的类脑优势，并夸大了未训练网络的竞争力。未来的神经-网络相似性研究必须在匹配的分辨率下进行评估，否则结论可能只是评估设置的伪影。

Abstract: Representational similarity analysis (RSA) is increasingly used to ask which learning rules give convolutional networks brain-like representations. Because biologically plausible rules such as feedback alignment, predictive coding and STDP do not scale, studies that include them train small networks on small images (typically 32x32 CIFAR) and then compare them to brain responses modeled at much higher resolution. We find that a common result in this setting, that untrained or locally trained networks rival or beat backpropagation at early visual cortex, depends strongly on the resolution at which the network is evaluated. The V1 gap between an untrained network and a backpropagation-trained one widens from -0.001 +/- 0.007 at the 32px training resolution to +0.044 +/- 0.006 at 224px, growing monotonically across six resolutions (n=5 seeds). It holds in human fMRI and, directionally, in single-seed macaque electrophysiology, along the training trajectory, and for an ImageNet ResNet-50 and a Swin-Tiny transformer trained at 224px. Four candidate mechanisms are tested and none accounts for it: train/eval resolution matching, low-level Gabor and pixel structure, the normalization state of the untrained baseline, and convergence of the pooled descriptor toward a global brightness statistic; three are excluded by interventions holding the convolutional weights bit-identical. A fifth experiment locates the effect: capping image detail at the training resolution while letting the pooled positions grow 12-fold removes about 90% of it, so the dependence is carried by image detail rather than by pooling. Separately, a single scalar luminance value per image reaches rho = 0.075 against the V1 RDM, essentially matching the untrained network's 0.076, which bounds what this style of comparison can resolve. The one learning effect that holds across resolution is backprop above untrained, at LOC.

</details>


### [194] [A Hierarchical Energy-Based Model for Multimodal Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12398)
*Subir Varma*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出IM-LEPP（集成多模态潜在基于能量的预测处理），一个层级化的基于能量的多模态认知模型，扩展了先前单模态的LEPP模型以整合视觉与语言。该模型将认知建模为潜在状态在学习的能量景观中流动，采用中枢-辐条（hub-and-spoke）层级架构，其基础是Lambon Ralph等人的受控语义认知框架：视觉对象、场景和语言单元的预测编码管线汇聚于一个模拟前颞叶的共享无模态中枢。各管线自身的预测由当前中枢状态条件化而非被覆盖，既保留了管线特异性身份，又使每个预测反映完整的多模态语境。作者展示了该架构能对注意力现象（如非注意盲视、Necker立方体双稳态）给出机理层面的解释，其结构也能再现或催生心理语言学的既有发现（如惊异理论、N400/P600 ERP成分、花园路径重析），并对Transformer语言模型在下词预测轨迹敏感性上提出了可证伪的对比。论文还讨论了相对LLM的数据高效语言习得、概述了语义/情景记忆子系统、将模型定位在预测编码、自由能原理、JEPA和层级时间记忆等框架中，并提出具体的实验预测来检验其核心主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将先前提出的单模态能量基预测处理模型（LEPP）扩展为整合视觉与语言的多模态认知模型，从而在一个统一框架内解释跨模态的认知现象（注意力、语言加工等），并提供一个有别于当前占主导地位的Transformer架构的、具有理论深度的生成式认知建模范式。

Method: IM-LEPP采用层级化的hub-and-spoke架构，基于受控语义认知框架（Lambon Ralph等），让视觉对象、场景和语言单元的预测编码管线汇聚于一个模拟前颞叶的共享无模态中枢。关键设计是条件化而非覆写机制——每条管线的预测由当前中枢状态所条件化，确保管线特异性信息保留的同时融入多模态语境。模型将认知刻画为潜在状态在学习的能量景观中的流动（类比统计力学之于热力学），而非对神经回路的直接模拟。

Result: 该架构为注意力现象（非注意盲视、Necker立方体双稳态）提供了机制性解释；其结构能重现或催生心理语言学的既有发现，包括惊异理论、N400/P600 ERP成分和花园路径重析；并与Transformer语言模型在下一词预测的轨迹敏感性方面构成了可证伪的对比。此外还展示了相对LLM更优的数据效率、概述了语义/情景记忆子系统。

Conclusion: 作者将IM-LEPP在预测编码、自由能原理、JEPA和层级时间记忆等既有框架中进行了理论定位，并提出具体的实验预测来检验模型的核心主张，强调这是一个可证伪的、具有有效理论地位的生成式多模态认知模型。

Abstract: We propose IM-LEPP (Integrated Multimodal Latent Energy-based Predictive Processing), a hierarchical, energy-based model of multimodal cognition that extends a previously proposed single-modality model (LEPP) to integrate vision and language. Following the view that generative neural networks are effective theories of cognitive dynamics, analogous to how statistical mechanics relates to thermodynamics, IM-LEPP models cognition as latent states flowing through learned energy landscapes rather than as an account of neural circuitry. The architecture is a hub-and-spoke hierarchy, grounded in the controlled semantic cognition framework of Lambon Ralph et al., in which predictive-coding pipelines for visual objects, scenes, and linguistic units converge on a shared amodal hub modeled on the anterior temporal lobe. Each pipeline's own prediction is conditioned by, rather than overwritten by, the current hub state, preserving pipeline-specific identity while letting every prediction reflect the full multimodal context. We show this architecture gives a mechanistic account of attentional phenomena such as inattentional blindness and Necker-cube bistability, and that its structure recovers or motivates independently established findings in psycholinguistics, including surprisal theory, the N400/P600 ERP components, and garden-path reanalysis, alongside a falsifiable contrast with transformer language models on trajectory-sensitivity in next-word prediction. We also discuss data-efficient language acquisition relative to LLMs, outline a semantic/episodic memory subsystem, situate the model against predictive coding, the free-energy principle, JEPA, and Hierarchical Temporal Memory, and propose concrete experimental predictions to test its central claims Key Words: predictive processing; predictive coding; energy-based models; diffusion models; effective theory; computational neuroscience.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇观点性论文，指出生成式AI社区对"推理"缺乏统一的操作性定义，导致推理评估的构念效度无法验证，阻碍了可信自主推理的量化进展。作者基于文献综合提出操作性定义——将有效且健全的推理定位为一种可学习的、基于规则的过程——并提供了一份AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主推理是AI领域最具科学和经济价值的话题之一，但生成式AI社区尚未在"推理"的操作性定义上达成共识，且往往隐含地排斥逻辑学和可验证自动推理中的历史处理方式。这种定义模糊性使推理评估的构念效度不可验证，从而削弱了面向可信自主推理的量化进展。

Method: 本文为立场论文（position paper），方法包括：(1) 对文献进行综合梳理，提出操作性定义，将推理定位为一种可学习的、基于规则的过程，并区分有效性与健全性；(2) 制定一份AI推理研究沟通最佳实践的检查清单。

Result: 提出了两方面的贡献：一是基于文献综合给出"推理"的操作性定义，确立有效且健全的推理作为基于规则的可学习过程；二是设计了一份用于规范AI推理研究沟通的报告检查清单。

Conclusion: 作者主张定义模糊性是当前自主推理研究中的关键问题但可以被解决，通过提供操作化定义和最佳实践清单，有助于使AI推理研究的评估更具可验证性，从而支持构建可信的自主推理系统。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [196] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntegrityBench基准，用于评测语言模型在机构压力下维护科研诚信的能力，发现前沿模型在峰值压力下约有1/3的诚信关键决策失败，且模型规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被部署为"共同科学家"，但其在机构压力下维护科研诚信的能力尚不明确，缺乏系统评测工具。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，包含36个配对任务，覆盖3个领域和4个研究阶段，采用5级隐式-显式压力协议，评测不当行为分类、伦理行动推理和基于工件（artifact）的决策制定三个维度，并评测18个前沿模型变体。

Result: 在峰值压力下，模型约有1/3的诚信关键决策失败；显式压力导致模型顺从不当行为，而隐式情境重构更常导致对合法研究任务的过度拒绝；模型规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解这些失败。值得注意的是，分类能力弱的模型在基于工件的决策上反而表现更好（85.7 vs 79.4），表明三个维度在结构上是分离的。

Conclusion: 前沿模型的三个诚信维度在结构上分离，正确伦理行动并不需要准确的分类能力。模型可能在看似有帮助的同时潜藏诚信失败，形成两类部署风险：助长科研不端行为和侵蚀对AI辅助研究的信任。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [197] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，论证现代AI对齐方法作为双重用途技术，可能被恶意行为者滥用为审查和操纵工具，并呼吁社区讨论这一风险并提出缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为AI对齐技术最初旨在防止有害输出，但其机制本质上可以被反向利用，成为信息控制和操纵的工具。随着AI被广泛采用为信息来源、经济权力不对称加剧以及政治环境趋向威权化，这种双重用途风险正被迅速放大，因此需要立即展开讨论。

Method: 通过将当前对齐技术映射到可能的滥用场景和实际案例，系统性地展示"完美对齐"模型的内在追求如何无意中为恶意行为者提供了不断改进的信息主导工具。

Result: 论文揭示了对齐机制（如RLHF、护栏、内容过滤等）在审查和操纵方面的可滥用性，并指出现有的对齐研究路线可能使这一问题日益严重。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁AI安全社区正视对齐机制被故意滥用的可能性，并提出对抗这一双重用途潜力的缓解策略，以在技术进步与现实政治风险之间找到平衡。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [198] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个基于ETHICS构建的500项道德判断基准，结合新的人类标注者和LLM对最终标签及支持性理由的标注，发现模型与人类在最终标签上高度一致，但在理由层面存在系统性分歧，表明标签一致性不等同于道德对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常用"与人类判断一致"作为评估LLM对齐的代理指标，但仅有最终标签一致无法证明人类标注者和模型依赖相同的道德依据。两个智能体可能得出相同判断，却诉诸不同的原则、情境假设或对情境的解释，因此需要区分标签层面的对齐与理由层面的对齐。

Method: 构建一个包含500个条目的ETHICS衍生基准，覆盖五个道德判断领域（包括伤害、尊重、守诺、公正、应得等范畴），收集新的人类标注者和多个LLM（前沿模型与开源模型家族）对最终标签及支持理由的标注，对比标签层面的多数一致性，并在理由层面进行系统性分析，考察模型与人类标注者在道德范畴注意力分配上的差异。

Result: 前沿模型和开源模型家族在最终标签上与人类标注者多数意见的一致性通常很高；然而理由层面的分析揭示了系统性分歧——即使最终标签与人类多数一致，模型仍会在各类道德范畴（如伤害、尊重、守诺、公正、应得、借口相关性）之间重新分配注意力权重。

Conclusion: 一致性不应被等同于对齐。仅基于标签的评估可能产生误导性的乐观结果，除非补充对模型判断中所表达的理由、原则和道德优先级进行分析。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [199] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream系统，其核心是LLM-MR-CNP协议——将经典契约网协议（CNP）扩展到多轮协商框架，结合语义CFP表述、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证。在异构移动边缘-云基础设施中实现去中心化调度，实验表明延迟违规率降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个代理时冲突解决率达0.91，效用较基于规则的多轮基线提升22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统在异构移动边缘-云基础设施中运行时，面临工作负载波动、资源争用和严格的QoS要求，使得去中心化调度变得复杂。现有调度方法难以同时处理动态环境中的定性上下文信息和严格的资源/QoS硬约束，需要一种融合LLM语义理解能力与确定性约束验证的新型协商协议。

Method: 提出LLM-MR-CNP协议，作为经典CNP的扩展，包含五个关键组件：语义CFP（调用提案）表述、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证。边缘集群代理基于局部观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文生成自然语言卸载提案，同时确保硬性资源和QoS约束保持确定性约束。实验基于阿里ASI追踪数据，从三个层面评估：单轮vs多轮CNP、基于规则vs LLM辅助精炼、固定模型单轮vs多轮协商。

Result: 在评估配置下，MAS-DecStream将延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个代理时冲突解决率达到0.91，效用较基于规则的多轮基线提升22%。单独的25案例分析显示模型和提示词依赖的精度-成本权衡。结果表明多轮CNP精炼是协议层面的主要增益来源，LLM辅助在定性及不确定运行时上下文处理方面增加价值。

Conclusion: 多轮CNP精炼是系统性能改进的主要驱动力，而LLM辅助协商在处理定性、不确定运行时上下文方面具有补充价值。该研究为边缘-云异构环境中结合LLM语义推理与确定性约束保证的去中心化调度提供了初步证据，展示了提高调度效用和QoS履约的潜力。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [200] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该立场论文主张在高风险决策场景中，AI系统需要实现"认知对齐"（cognitive alignment），即系统应以与用户相似的方式进行推理，并忠实地传达其推理过程，以确保可理解性和可信赖性，并提出了相应的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地充当决策辅助、决策委托或自主决策者，现有对齐方法主要关注价值观对齐，而忽视了推理方式与用户认知的一致性。作者认为认知错位是AI在许多设想的应用中被采用的主要障碍，用户既需要对系统可信，也需要系统在实际上述值得信赖。

Method: 本文采用立场论文的论述方式，通过综述认知对齐改善可理解性与可信赖性的现有证据，同时提供新的调查数据，展示许多用户在AI对判断或行动的解释对他们很重要时，认为认知对齐是"必不可少的"；并据此指出现有对齐方法与实现认知对齐需求之间的差距，提出研究议程。

Result: 调查数据表明许多用户认为认知对齐是"必不可少的"；综述证据支持认知对齐能改善可理解性和可信赖性；识别出现有对齐方法的不足，勾勒出实现认知对齐需要填补的差距。

Conclusion: 认知错位是AI在许多应用中被采纳的潜在障碍，实现认知对齐对于创造用户既愿意依赖又有理由依赖的AI系统至关重要，有必要开展专门研究来解决这一方向上的对齐缺口。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [201] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型的安全对齐行为具有语言依赖性。在核打击等高危场景中，用日语提示可将Claude Sonnet 4.6和Gemini Pro 3.1等模型的攻击率大幅降低（如Claude从40%降至0%、93%降至17%），且机制在于模型被要求"用日语推理"，而非输入语言本身——用日语推理时模型自发产生道德词汇。其他五个模型无语言效应但几乎总是发动攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地用于战略和咨询场景，但其安全对齐评估通常仅用英语进行。作者质疑：提示语言是否可能改变模型在高风险场景中的决策？如果仅用英语评估安全行为，可能遗漏其他语言中存在的风险和防护机制。

Method: 使用单回合博弈论小场景：模型向核武国家提供是否攻击无防守对手的建议，提示刻意保持非道德化且各语言间策略一致。测试六个提供商的九种模型（含Claude家族、Gemini等），并通过跨语言实验隔离机制——在英语提示中要求模型用日语推理，以区分"输入语言"与"推理语言"的影响。

Result: 日语提示显著降低Claude Sonnet 4.6的攻击率（不必要攻击场景从40%降至0%，争议场景从93%降至17%），Gemini Pro 3.1也从53%降至13%。跨语言实验证明是推理语言驱动效应（英语提示中要求用日语推理，攻击率从93%降至37%）。用日语推理时模型自发产生"道德成本""百万生命"等提示中不存在的道德词汇。另外五个模型无语言效应，且几乎所有条件下都发动攻击。效应仅在模型英语时已表现出犹豫时才出现。

Conclusion: LLM安全行为是语言依赖的，仅用英语评估会遗漏其他语言中编码的风险和防护。语言效应需要推理语言触发，且依赖模型本身具备的一定安全倾向。这提示安全评估需要多语言化。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [202] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Flow Transformer，通过双流架构将推理计算按阶段（prefill与decode）分离分配：主流处理prompt并写KV cache，辅流仅在最后prompt位置后激活用于续写预测，二者共享大部分权重和缓存，实现decode阶段计算增强而不影响prefill成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理累积成本日益重要，且prefill（并行、计算受限）和decode（串行、内存带宽受限）两个阶段对硬件压力不同。传统宽度或深度扩展同时增加两个阶段的成本，因为每一层在两个阶段都会被评估。作者希望探索是否可以将额外的学习计算仅分配给续写预测，同时保持prompt-wide的主要计算和单一持久KV cache。

Method: 提出Dual-Flow Transformer架构。主流（primary flow）是完整因果语言模型，处理prompt并写入KV cache；辅流（auxiliary flow）在prompt处理阶段被省略，仅从最终prompt位置开始激活，增加续写预测计算但不写入持久状态也不影响主流。两流共享主要attention、MLP和输出矩阵，使用独立的token嵌入和轻量耦合。权重共享和主缓存复用创造了在执行中重用加载权重和缓存键值的机会。在MoE模型中，分离使主辅expert fan-out成为prefill成本、续写成本和预测质量的独立控制项。

Result: 在匹配token的比较中，Dual-Flow在多种架构和数据配置下实现了更低的验证损失。在MoE模型中，实验研究了两类机制：在固定prefill expert计算下增加decode计算，以及在固定decode expert预算下在两流之间重新分配。实验揭示了prefill-decode质量权衡，并展示了按阶段分配expert的潜力。

Conclusion: Dual-Flow Transformer证明存在将额外计算仅分配到续写预测（decode阶段）的可行方案，通过双流设计同时保持prefill计算开销和单一KV缓存不变，在MoE场景下进一步展示了按推理阶段控制计算分配的灵活性。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [203] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA方法用于跨领域零样本/少样本个性化对话生成，通过按证据质量校准适应性更新强度并解耦用户与领域偏好先验，在HiCUPID等基准上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨领域零样本/少样本个性化生成中，现有适应性方法在稀疏证据下难以校准更新幅度导致过拟合，而历史迁移方法常将用户偏好与源领域伪影纠缠，产生不可靠的个性化先验和负迁移。

Method: 提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA：(1) 以元学习的LoRA初始化作为适应起点和先验中心，根据支持集规模和预测不确定性动态调整更新强度，以限制稀疏或模糊证据下的过拟合；(2) 将个性化先验功能性地解耦为用户组件（人类可读提示词表达稳定偏好）和领域组件（保持拓扑的软标记进行领域特定隐空间条件化）。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上一致优于强基线。在HiCUPID上，相对于最佳竞争基线，跨域胜率退化降低47.9%；在未见用户冷启动场景下胜率提升110.2%。

Conclusion: 通过按证据质量校准适应更新并解耦用户与领域偏好先验，可以有效缓解稀疏证据下的过拟合和负迁移问题，实现更可靠的跨领域个性化。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed. We therefore functionally decompose personalization priors into user and domain components, using a human-readable prompt for stable preferences and topology-preserving soft tokens for domain-specific hidden-space conditioning. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and personalization tasks show consistent gains over strong baselines. On HiCUPID, our method reduces cross-domain win-rate degradation by 47.9% relative to the best competing baseline and improves win rate by 110.2% under unseen-user cold start.

</details>


### [204] [Research Assistant: AstraZeneca's Agentic System for R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12395)
*Piotr Grabowski,Mohamed Alameen,Jorge Bretones,Sabina Cardell,Miguel Carmona,Gavin Edwards,Ben Grainger,Sameh Hassan,Erik Jansson,Artur Kuziakhmetov,Albert Maristany,Hebatallah Mohamed,Andriy Nikolov,Sebastian Nilsson,Mark O'Donoghue,James Pacileo,Ashiq Sultan,Alex Voegele,Michael Ughetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AstraZeneca内部开发的基于LLM的研究助手系统（Research Assistant），该系统通过聊天式界面整合科学文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全资源、表达数据及内部实验系统等多源证据，支持快速问答和复杂多步研究任务，并已在公司研发工作流中大规模部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助科学家和临床研究人员高效探索生物医学问题，将分散在多个异构数据源中的证据整合到统一界面中，以支持日常研发工作流并加速科学决策。

Method: 系统采用聊天式界面，提供两种工作模式：快速模式用于直接问答，多步模式用于复杂研究任务。响应基于检索到的证据进行地面化（grounding），并链接回原始数据源。系统架构整合了科学文献、知识图谱、化学数据、临床试验、安全资源、表达数据和内部实验系统。

Result: 该系统已在AstraZeneca内部大规模部署，支持日常研发工作流。论文记录了系统架构、关键产品设计决策以及大规模部署过程中获得的经验教训。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的研究助手系统在制药研发场景中具有实际价值，通过整合多源证据和地面化响应能够有效支持科学探索；同时，大规模部署过程中积累的经验教训为类似企业内部LLM系统的构建提供了参考。

Abstract: We describe Research Assistant, an internal LLM-based system developed at AstraZeneca to help scientists and clinicians explore biomedical questions across a broad range of data sources. The system provides a chat-style interface that brings together evidence from scientific literature, knowledge graphs, chemistry, clinical trials, safety resources, expression data, and internal experimental systems. It supports both a fast mode for direct question answering and a multi-step mode for more complex research tasks. Responses are grounded in retrieved evidence and linked back to the original sources, allowing users to review and further explore the underlying data. In this technical note, we outline the system architecture, the main design choices behind the product, and lessons learned from deploying it at scale to support day-to-day R&D workflows across AstraZeneca.

</details>


### [205] [Large Language Models Can Follow Instructions, But Not Many at Once: Phase Transitions in Compositional Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12426)
*Mariya I. Vasileva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出约束饱和评估（CSE），一个程序化生成的基准，系统性地变化同时满足的约束数量（k=1-12），对15个模型、36种约束类型、369,753次检查进行确定性规则验证。研究发现：①单约束通过率逐渐下降，但全约束联合通过率急剧崩溃（k=8时单约束约41%的模型全通过仅5.7%）；②结构约束比词汇约束每增加一个约束损失2倍以上的基线能力；③失败近乎独立，导致累积呈乘性加速，可靠指令遵循在5-6个约束后崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型越来越多地部署在需要同时满足多个显式约束（推理结构、安全边界、输出模式）的场景中。尽管单个约束能被熟练处理，但组合约束场景（多约束须联合成立）的退化方式、退化机制及缓解可能性仍缺乏系统性刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出约束饱和评估（CSE）基准：程序化生成任务，系统性地改变同时施加的约束数量k（1-12），每个约束由确定性、基于规则的验证器打分，完全不依赖LLM作为裁判。覆盖15个模型、36种约束类型，共369,753次检查。

Result: 三个主要发现：①单约束通过率呈渐进可预测的衰减，但全部k个约束同时满足的概率呈崩溃式下降——在k=8时，单约束通过率约41%的模型，八约束全通过仅5.7%；②不同约束退化不均：结构约束每增加一个约束比词汇约束多损失2倍基线能力，二者之间隔着一个"理解-维持差距"，将需要持续追踪的约束与对组合免疫的二元决策约束区分开；③失败几乎独立，使累积变得乘性；残余耦合关联共享的输出特征（如错误的句子数会使所有读取它的约束失败），而非成对干扰。超过5-6个约束时可靠指令遵循崩溃：最强模型在7个约束时探针级成功率低于50%，15个模型中的12个在3个或更少约束时即低于此阈值。

Conclusion: 组合约束遵循存在一个明确的容量瓶颈（约5-6个约束）：在此之上模型的可靠指令遵循能力迅速崩溃。约束失败以近似独立的方式累积，使得联合成功率呈乘性衰减，这一机制揭示了为何多约束场景下模型表现远差于单约束表现。结构约束比词汇约束更脆弱，表明不同约束类型对组合压力的鲁棒性存在系统性差异，为未来缓解策略（如面向结构约束的训练）提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings that require simultaneous adherence to multiple explicit constraints - reasoning structure, safety boundaries, output schemas. Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally generated benchmark that systematically varies the number of simultaneous constraints (k), with every constraint scored by a deterministic, rule-based verifier and zero LLM-judge involvement: 15 models, 36 constraint types, 369,753 checks at k=1-12. Three findings emerge. First, per-constraint pass rate decays gradually and predictably, while the chance of satisfying all k constraints collapses - a model passing individual constraints at ~41% at k=8 succeeds on all eight just 5.7% of the time. Second, constraints do not degrade equally: structural constraints lose 2x more baseline capability per added constraint than lexical ones, ordered by a comprehension-maintenance gap that separates constraints requiring sustained tracking from binary decisions immune to composition. Third, failures are nearly independent, which is what makes the accumulation multiplicative; the residual coupling that does exist tracks shared output features rather than pairwise interference - a wrong sentence count fails every constraint that reads it. Reliable instruction following breaks down beyond 5-6 simultaneous constraints: probe-level success falls below 50% at 7 constraints for the strongest model, and at 3 or fewer for 12 of 15.

</details>


### [206] [MindMemOS: A Portable and Self-Evolving Memory Operating Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12428)
*Kaichao Liang,Yuqi Cui,Hao Kong,Xinyuan Huang,Guohaotian Hou,Qingcan Kang,Liang Chen,Yiyang Yin,Ke Ye,Jiaquan Guo,Da Chen,Lingan Zeng,Yixing Peng,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI
