Method: ParliamentRAG的核心贡献是主题依赖的权威模型，该模型根据当前查询估计每位发言人的权威度，融合可解释组件如职业、教育背景和先前发言记录。系统流程为：根据用户查询检索相关发言片段，识别跨越议会各党团的主题相关专家，然后生成综合各方视角的摘要并附带支持性引文。评估采用两级别协议，结合自动化指标和六位领域专家的盲法A/B人工评估，在15个政策主题上与Google NotebookLM对比。

Result: ParliamentRAG在政治群体覆盖度上达到0.97（对比NotebookLM的0.95），引用忠实度达到完美的1.00（对比0.95），并在来源相关维度上获得更强专家偏好；而NotebookLM在散文导向维度上表现更强。

Conclusion: ParliamentRAG通过主题依赖的权威模型和跨党团专家识别机制，有效解决了RAG应用于议会场景的三个核心风险，在来源可靠性和引用准确性方面显著优于对比系统，但其散文写作质量仍有提升空间，可考虑结合NotebookLM在行文方面的优势进一步完善。

Abstract: Parliamentary proceedings are a primary record of democratic deliberation, yet their volume and fragmentation make multi-perspective access difficult for citizens, journalists, and researchers. Applying Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to parliamentary transcripts introduces three specific risks: dominance of the most frequent speakers, inability to weight speakers according to topical expertise, and citation misattribution in politically sensitive text. We present ParliamentRAG, a RAG system for the Italian Chamber of Deputies that addresses these risks jointly. Its core contribution is a topic-dependent authority model that estimates each speaker's authority as a function of the current query, combining interpretable components such as profession, education, and previous interventions. Given a user query, the system retrieves relevant speech chunks, identifies topic-relevant experts across parliamentary groups, and generates a summary synthesizing their perspectives, accompanied by supporting quotations. ParliamentRAG is evaluated against Google NotebookLM on 15 policy topics via a two-level protocol combining automated metrics and blind A/B human evaluation by six domain experts. The system achieves higher coverage across political groups (0.97 vs. 0.95), perfect quotation faithfulness (1.00 vs. 0.95), and stronger expert preferences on source-related dimensions, while NotebookLM remains stronger on prose-oriented dimensions.

</details>


### [271] [Beyond Final Scores: A Systematic Evaluation of Agents for Long-Horizon AI Research and Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13417)
*Yiwei Li,Wanli Yang,Hexiang Tan,Xiangzhou Huang,Zhengyu Chen,Ziran Li,Borun Chen,Shanglin Lei,Huaisheng Zhu,Hao Tian,Fei Sun,Xunliang Cai,Jingang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对七个前沿模型在36个长时程任务上进行了系统评估，提出了一套基于规则度量的框架，通过Solution Framing、Execution和Feedback Control三个维度刻画运行内行为，并通过受控对比评估任务内外的经验复用能力。结果发现当前智能体更像"工程优化器"而非"完全自主研究者"：能制定和实现实用方案，但运行间性能波动大，最强方案主要改编或组合已有技术，真正的方法论新颖性仍然稀缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注最终得分，无法揭示进展在哪里获得或丢失，也无法反映累积经验是否改善后续决策。为了理解自主智能体在长时程实验中的真实能力状态，需要超越终结性得分的系统性评估方法。

Method: 提出新的评估框架，包含三个方面：(1) 使用基于规则的度量，通过Solution Framing（方案构建）、Execution（执行）和Feedback Control（反馈控制）三个维度刻画智能体的运行内行为；(2) 通过受控对比来评估任务内与跨任务的经验复用能力；(3) 在36个长时程任务上对7个前沿模型进行系统评测。

Result: 当前智能体可以制定和实现实用方案，但运行间性能波动显著；它们的最强解决方案主要依赖改编或组合已有技术，方法论新颖性不足。详细分析表明，相似的最终结果背后可能存在不同的过程瓶颈，经验复用既可能帮助也可能误导后续决策，且框架设计（harness design）会影响性能稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究为改进模型训练、推理时策略、经验管理和框架设计提供了具体方向。智能体当前处于"工程优化器"阶段，距离完全自主的科研能力仍有差距。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly capable of improving models, systems, and other technical artifacts through long-horizon experimentation. To understand the current state of this capability, however, evaluation must go beyond final scores, which neither reveal where progress is gained or lost nor indicate whether accumulated experience improves later decisions. We therefore present a systematic evaluation of seven frontier models on 36 long-horizon tasks based on a new framework that uses rule-based metrics to characterize within-run behavior through Solution Framing, Execution, and Feedback Control and controlled comparisons to assess experience reuse within and across tasks. The results show that current agents operate more like engineering optimizers than fully autonomous researchers: they can formulate and implement practical solutions, but their performance varies substantially across runs, their strongest solutions mainly adapt or combine established techniques, and genuine methodological novelty remains rare. Detailed analysis reveals that observed performance is shaped by multiple factors, including distinct process bottlenecks behind similar final outcomes, experience reuse that can help or mislead subsequent decisions, and harness designs that affect performance stability. These findings suggest concrete directions for improving model training, inference-time strategies, experience management, and harness design.

</details>


### [272] [Enhancing Virtual Agents through SLMs and Edge-Computing: An Exploratory Evaluation of Think and Memory Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13420)
*Aimilios Hadjiliasi,Louis Nisiotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索使用小语言模型（SLMs）在边缘计算环境中支持具身智能虚拟代理的认知组件，聚焦于CEAA架构中的"思考"和"记忆"过程，并在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上开发评估了一个基于Qwen2.5模型的边缘代理网关系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能虚拟代理需要在复杂虚拟世界和元宇宙中持续运行，具备适应性和上下文感知能力，然而实现认知能力的代理在概念和技术上都具有挑战性。利用边缘计算和生成式AI语言模型的进展，探索SLMs支持边缘端认知组件运行的可能性，以实现高效、情境化的交互体验。

Method: 基于CEAA（认知具身代理架构）框架，选择了"Think"（推理）和"Memory"（记忆）两个核心认知编排与持久化过程作为研究对象。开发了一个边缘虚拟代理网关系统，在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上部署不同规模的Qwen2.5模型，通过一系列仿真实验评估系统处理服务请求和记忆驱动对话的能力，重点测量路由准确率、记忆读取性能和延迟。

Result: 仿真实验验证了SLM驱动的原型代理系统能够部分实现CEAA中的选定认知过程，展示了系统在路由准确率、记忆读取性能和延迟方面的可行性与能力，证明边缘端认知"大脑"可以高效运行。

Conclusion: SLMs在边缘计算环境下能够有效支持具身智能代理的部分认知组件运行，为在沉浸式虚拟世界中开发具有高效、情境化认知能力的具身代理提供了一条可行的技术路径，部分实现了CEAA框架的核心认知过程。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent virtual agents are expected to operate as persistent, adaptive, and context-aware entities within complex virtual and Metaverse worlds. However, implementing cognitively capable agents in such environments is conceptually and technologically challenging. Among a range of blueprints and development approaches, the Cognitive Embodied Agent Architecture (CEAA) has been developed as an implementation-oriented framework for architecting components of perception, memory, reasoning, planning, and embodied action. Considering the recent advances in edge computing and generative AI language models, this paper explores the use of Small Language Models (SLMs) to support edge-based operation of selected CEAA components, focusing on "Think" and "Memory" as processes central to cognitive orchestration and persistence of virtual agents in interactive virtual worlds. An edge-based virtual agent gateway system was developed and evaluated on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX using Qwen2.5 models of different sizes, exploring the system's capability to process service requests and handle memory-driven conversations. A series of simulation experiments evaluated routing accuracy, memory-read performance, and latency, demonstrating an SLM-driven prototype agent system that partially implements selected CEAA processes to support the development of embodied agents whose cognitive "brain" can operate efficiently and contextually for interactive experiences in immersive virtual worlds.

</details>


### [273] [RAIL: An Automatic Classifier of the Artificial Intelligence Readiness Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13428)
*Juan Irving Vasquez,Juan Terven,Laura-Ivoone Garay-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出统一AI就绪度等级（AIRL）——一个九等级序数标尺，基于环境证据阶梯构建，并辅以维度上限、泛化锚定规则与分配纪律，使就绪度等级可仅从自然语言描述判定；同时提出RAIL方法，即通过独立的LLM专家小组（一个证据代理加六个维度代理）以确定性最小规则聚合判决，并由首席专家不对称复核，避免单一LLM分类器的过度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能技术成熟度的评估对投资决策、项目管理和政策监测至关重要，但现有就绪度框架异构且难以自动应用：TRL在AI领域缺乏AI特有的门控标准，MLTRL依赖内部过程产物，AI/数据就绪维度模型的尺度难以直接比较。因此需要一个统一、可自动化判定的AI就绪度框架。

Method: 本文提出两方面的贡献：（1）统一AI就绪度等级（AIRL），一个九等级序数标尺，基于环境证据阶梯构建，并辅以维度上限（涵盖规格、数据存在性、数据质量、数据合法性、专家知识和算法成熟度）、泛化锚定规则和明确的分配纪律；（2）提出RAIL（独立性LLM专家就绪度评估），即一个专家小组分类器，包括一个证据代理和六个独立维度代理，每个都是职责范围狭窄的LLM，其判决由确定性最小规则聚合，并由首席专家在不对称权限下复核——只能确认或降低小组建议，不能超过上限。

Result: 该方法在多个研究工作的分析中进行了测试，显示出良好的一致性，并且避免了单一LLM分类器的过度估计问题。

Conclusion: AIRL与RAIL组合提供了一种可判定、可自动化且不易过度估计的AI就绪度评估方案，统一了既有框架并解决了现有方法在AI场景下的适用性与可比性缺陷。

Abstract: Assessing the maturity of artificial intelligence technologies is essential for investment decisions, project management, and policy monitoring, yet the available readiness frameworks are heterogeneous and difficult to apply automatically: the adaptation of Technology Readiness Levels to AI lacks AI-specific gating criteria, the Machine Learning Technology Readiness Levels presuppose access to internal process artifacts, and AI/data readiness dimension models employ scales that resist direct comparison. This paper makes two contributions. First, we unify these three frameworks into the Unified AI Readiness Level (AIRL), a nine-level ordinal scale built on an environmental evidence ladder and complemented by dimensional caps (covering specification, data existence, data quality, data legality, expert knowledge, and algorithmic maturity) together with a generality-anchoring rule and explicit assignment disciplines, so that a readiness level becomes decidable from a natural-language description of the work alone. Second, we propose RAIL (Readiness Assessment via Independent LLM-experts), a panel-of-experts classifier that operationalizes the scale: one evidence agent and six independent dimension agents, each a large language model with a narrowly scoped mandate, deliver verdicts that a deterministic minimum rule aggregates and a chief expert reviews under asymmetric authority, confirming or lowering the panel's recommendation but never raising it above the caps. The method was tested in the analysis of several research works showing consistency and avoiding overestimation from monolithic LLM classifiers.

</details>


### [274] [Academic League of Artificial Intelligence - An Integrative Perspective of Teaching, Research, and Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13447)
*Alison R. Panisson,Maria Eduarda W. M. Vianna,Italo Firmino da Silva,Heitor Henrique da Silva,Rafaela Fernandes Savaris,Bernardo Pandolfi Costa,Martin Augusto Gagliotti Vigil,Jim Lau,Agenor Hentz,Andréa Sabedra Bordin,Alexandre Leopoldo Gonçalves,Roberto Rodrigues-Filho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西圣卡塔琳娜联邦大学人工智能学术联盟（LIA）采用的组织框架，该框架以学生为中心、基于项目的方式整合了教学、研究和大学拓展三大支柱。通过民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织，该框架培养了技术和横向能力，并通过竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库和具有社会影响的人工智能应用等代表性项目得到验证，为工程和计算教育中的学术联盟提供了灵活且可复制的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术联盟已成为促进课外教育和加强大学与社会融合的重要机制，但如何设计一个能够同时整合教学、研究和大学拓展三大支柱的组织框架仍然缺乏系统性指导，本文旨在提出并验证这样一个可复制的框架。

Method: 本文采用案例研究方法，基于巴西圣卡塔琳娜联邦大学人工智能学术联盟（LIA）的实际组织经验，提出并描述了一种结合民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织的框架，并通过多个代表性项目（竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库、社会影响AI应用）进行展示和验证。

Result: 该框架成功地将多元的教育、科研和拓展活动纳入统一组织架构中，同时促进了领导力、科学产出、社区参与和知识保存。经验表明该框架是灵活且可复制的，能够有效整合大学三大支柱。

Conclusion: 所提出的组织框架为学术联盟及类似学生组织在工程和计算教育中整合教学、研究和大学拓展三大支柱提供了实用的指导，具有可复制性和推广价值。

Abstract: Academic leagues have become important mechanisms for promoting extracurricular education and strengthening the integration between universities and society. This paper presents the organizational framework adopted by the Academic League of Artificial Intelligence (LIA) at the Federal University of Santa Catarina (UFSC), designed to integrate teaching, research, and university extension through a student-centered, project-based approach. The framework combines democratic governance, collaborative learning, and dynamic project organization to foster both technical and transversal competencies. The framework is illustrated through representative initiatives, including competition teams, study groups, open lectures, knowledge repositories, and AI-powered applications with social impact. These projects demonstrate how diverse educational, scientific, and extension activities can be developed within a common organizational structure while promoting leadership, scientific production, community engagement, and knowledge preservation. The reported experience indicates that the proposed framework provides a flexible and replicable model for integrating the three university pillars into engineering and computing education, offering practical guidance for academic leagues and similar student organizations.

</details>


### [275] [A Unifying Perspective on Causal World Models: From Observations to Representations to Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13456)
*Avinash Kori,Fabrizio Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从因果视角系统性地研究世界模型（World Models），提出了因果世界模型（Causal WMs, CWMs）的形式化定义，强调世界模型不仅需要具备生成能力，还必须捕捉系统中实体属性、实体间交互以及实体与环境间的因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型主要侧重于生成能力（如预测和规划），但缺乏对环境中因果结构的刻画。为了让智能体能够超越训练分布进行预测、规划和行动，世界模型需要编码与因果相关的信息，而不仅仅是对观测的统计生成。作者希望将世界模型与因果表示学习、因果发现、结构因果模型等进行理论上的连接，从而为智能体的因果推理和决策提供基础。

Method: 作者从多个抽象层次（从感知观测到概念级的环境动态结构表征）出发，提出CWM的形式化定义，并将其融入现有因果表示学习、目标中心学习、因果发现、结构因果模型和基于模型的决策制定的框架中。同时，作者结合可辨识性（identifiability）文献，分析了CWM的各个组成部分在何种条件下能从数据中恢复，以及可以恢复到何种等价类。

Result: 论文提供了一个统一的理论框架，将世界模型与因果推理进行形式化连接，明确了CWM的定义、任务支撑以及各组成部分的可辨识性条件。

Conclusion: 世界模型应该建立在支持因果推理和明智决策的表征与结构之上，而不仅仅是生成式预测。CWM为这一方向提供了理论基础，并敞开了与因果表示学习和可辨识性理论深入结合的研究前景。

Abstract: World Models (WM) are increasingly seen as a foundation for intelligent agents that can predict, plan, and act beyond their training distribution. In this paper, we study WMs from a causal perspective across multiple levels of abstraction, ranging from perceptual observations to building a conceptual representation of the structure governing the environment dynamics. We argue that useful WMs must go beyond generative capabilities alone: they should also capture entity properties, entity-to-entity interactions, and entity-to-environment interactions that determine and explain the dynamics of a system. We provide a formal definition of Causal WMs (CWMs) grounded in the tasks they are intended to support, connecting world modelling with existing work in causal representation learning, object-centric learning, causal discovery, structural causal models, and model-based decision-making. Finally, we relate CWMs to the literature on identifiability, clarifying when the components of a WM can be recovered from data and up to which equivalence. With this, we ground WMs in representations and structures that support causal reasoning and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [276] [MARC v1: An Open-Source Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical AI Reasoning and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13476)
*Saisha Shetty,Satvik Tripathi,Austin Lin,Colin Zhao,Theodore Kim,Don Enwerem,Jacinta Arnold,Shahriar Faghani,Tessa S Cook*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARC是一个开源的多智能体临床推理框架，用确定性多智能体编排替代单体LLM提示，包含提取、推理、答案生成和评估四个角色分工智能体，并引入Decomposer模块自动生成任务特定提示，支持API和本地CPU部署，全部通过YAML配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决临床推理中单体LLM提示方法缺乏可解释性、难以进行故障归因以及需要繁琐的人工提示工程的问题，使临床领域专家无需编程技能即可使用。

Method: 采用确定性多智能体编排架构，协调提取、推理、答案生成和评估四个角色专属智能体，通过显式上下文传递和可追踪的中间输出实现分级故障归因；引入Decomposer模块从自然语言描述自动生成任务特定智能体提示；通过YAML实现完全配置化，支持API和本地CPU兼容部署。

Result: 提供了一个模型无关、可解释、对无编程经验临床专家友好的开源框架，实现了无需代码修改的灵活部署。

Conclusion: MARC通过多智能体确定性编排和自动化提示生成，为临床推理提供了一种更透明、可调试且易于访问的替代方案，代码已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: We present Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination (MARC), an open-source framework that replaces monolithic LLM prompting with deterministic multi-agent orchestration for clinical reasoning. MARC coordinates role-specialized agents for extraction, reasoning, answer generation, and evaluation, with explicit context passing and traceable intermediate outputs, enabling stage-wise failure attribution. We additionally introduce a Decomposer module that generates task-specific agent prompts from a plain-language description, eliminating manual prompt engineering. The framework supports both API-based and local CPU-compatible deployments and is entirely configurable via YAML, without code modifications. MARC is designed to be model-agnostic, interpretable, and accessible to clinical domain experts without programming expertise. The full framework is available at https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1.

</details>


### [277] [AlayaWorld: Interactive Long-Horizon World Modeling - Full Technical Report (v1.1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13492)
*AlayaWorld Team,Kaipeng Zhang,Chuanhao Li,Yifan Zhan,Yongtao Ge,Yuanyang Yin,Jiaming Tan,Kang He,Liaoyuan Fan,Mingliang Zhai,Ruicong Liu,Xiaojie Xu,Xuangeng Chu,Zhen Li,Zhengyuan Lin,Zhixiang Wang,Zian Meng,Zihui Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlayaWorld的改进版本，核心思路是让条件信号在潜在表示和时间结构上尽可能与生成内容一致。主要改动包括用流式3D点缓存渲染器替代深度扭曲空间记忆，并将视觉条件编码到与生成视频一致的因果VAE潜在空间。整体引入六项具体修改以提升条件与生成内容的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件信号与生成内容在潜在表示和时间结构上的不一致会导致生成质量下降或条件控制不准确。此前版本使用深度扭曲的空间记忆和静态帧图像条件，与生成内容在表征空间和时间结构上存在差异，需要改进条件信号的表征与集成方式。

Method: 提出"条件信号应与生成内容在潜在表示和时间结构上尽可能匹配"的设计原则，据此做出两大改变：(1)用流式3D点缓存渲染器替代深度扭曲空间记忆；(2)将视觉条件编码到与生成视频一致的因果VAE潜在空间。具体包含六项修改：运动感知潜在条件替代静态帧条件、空间记忆的因果连续编码、像素空间时间记忆窗口对齐、硬内存丢失（移除token而非置零）、统一训练与推理的VAE编解码协议、移除相机AdaLN分支以完全依赖重渲染空间条件提供视角控制。

Result: 论文未在摘要中给出具体数值结果，但表明通过上述六项修改，新版本在条件信号与生成内容的一致性上显著提升，从而改进了条件控制下的视频生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过使条件信号在潜在空间和时间结构上对齐生成内容，AlayaWorld的改进版本提升了条件生成的一致性与控制能力。未来可在此基础上进一步探索更高效的条件表征方式。

Abstract: This report presents an improved version of AlayaWorld. While the backbone architecture, chunk-wise autoregressive generation scheme, and training data remain unchanged from the previous release, we substantially revise how conditioning signals are represented and integrated into the model. The new design is guided by a simple principle: conditioning signals should match the generated content as closely as possible in both latent representation and temporal structure. To this end, we make two major changes. First, we replace the previous depth-warping-based spatial memory with a streaming 3D point-cache renderer. Second, we redesign the conditioning pipeline so that visual conditions are encoded in the same causal-VAE latent space, with temporal statistics consistent with those of the generated video. Concretely, the new version introduces six modifications: (1) replacing static-frame image conditioning with motion-aware latent conditioning; (2) causally encoding re-rendered spatial memory as a continuous sequence; (3) aligning the temporal-memory window in pixel space; (4) adopting hard memory dropout that removes memory tokens rather than zeroing them; (5) unifying the VAE encoding and decoding protocol across training and inference; and (6) removing the camera AdaLN branch, such that viewpoint control is provided entirely through the re-rendered spatial condition.

</details>


### [278] [QuoteBench: How Matched Scores Can Hide Command-Path Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13547)
*Shangao Li,Yao Zhang,Volker Tresp,Yuanyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuoteBench基准，用于区分LLM编码代理在生成Bash命令时的生成错误与执行传输过程中引入的失败。通过56个一次性任务、14个事故衍生家族，在生成契约与执行传输的交叉点注入一个故意未转义的解析器，评估模型在披露边界条件下的恢复能力。结果显示，通过附加解析器重放相同回复会使成功率下降55.4至73.2个百分点，而披露边界信息可恢复30.4至60.7个百分点（对六个配置有效）。模型在原始生成上已接近饱和，边界适应能力成为区分模型的关键。GPT-5.6-sol的匹配差距-3.6分隐藏了-64.3的损伤和+60.7的补偿。部署配置会重新排序模型，26个可比较对中有1个明确反转和4个处于单任务边缘。论文呼吁评估报告应包含模型配置、生成契约、执行路径、工作点和最终状态验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的匹配执行分数无法区分命令生成错误与生成后引入的失败（如接口序列化、包装和重新解析导致的问题）。这一混淆掩盖了模型真实能力的差异，导致评估结果失真。作者旨在建立能够精确测量这一边界的基准，揭示哪些"失败"实际源于执行传输层而非模型本身的生成能力。

Method: 构建QuoteBench基准，包含56个一次性任务、14个事故衍生家族。在生成契约与执行传输的交叉点注入一个故意未转义的解析器，通过精确最终状态验证进行评测。在插值点进行转义以重现每个重放回复的原始路径结果，确保任何在披露边界下的恢复必须来自模型改变其生成。跨八个相同窗口配置进行重放比较，并分析不同模型的原始生成饱和度与边界适应能力。

Result: 跨八个相同窗口配置，通过附加解析器重放相同回复使成功率下降55.4至73.2个百分点；披露边界信息可恢复30.4至60.7个百分点（六个配置有效），另外两个配置恢复为零或轻微负值。原始生成在模型前沿已接近饱和，边界适应能力成为模型区分的关键。GPT-5.6-sol的匹配差距-3.6分实际隐藏了-64.3点的损伤和+60.7点的补偿。部署配置重新排序了模型排名：26个可比较对中有1个明确反转，4个处于单任务边缘。

Conclusion: 评估命令发起代理时，应报告模型的配置、生成契约、执行路径、工作点和最终状态验证器，而非将匹配分数视为模型的内在属性。单纯的匹配分数具有误导性，可能掩盖显著的损伤与补偿。模型的边界适应能力（即在执行接口约束下生成正确命令的能力）是当前模型间区分度的主要来源，而原始生成能力已接近饱和。

Abstract: LLM coding agents issue Bash commands through interfaces that may serialize, wrap, and reparse model output. Matched execution scores alone cannot distinguish command-generation errors from failures introduced after generation. QuoteBench measures this boundary with exact final-state validation on 56 one-shot tasks from 14 incident-derived families, crossing the generation contract with the execution transport around one deliberately unescaped added parser. Escaping at the interpolation point reproduces each replayed reply's raw-path outcome, so any recovery under a disclosed boundary must come from the model changing its generation. Across eight same-window configurations, replaying the same reply through the added parser lowers success by 55.4 to 73.2 percentage points; disclosure recovers 30.4 to 60.7 points for six configurations, and zero or slightly negative for the other two. Raw generation is nearly saturated at the frontier; boundary adaptation is what still separates models. GPT-5.6-sol's matched gap of -3.6 points hides -64.3 points of damage and +60.7 points of compensation. The deployment configuration reorders models: one reversal among 26 comparable pairs is unambiguous and four more sit on single-task margins. Evaluations of command-issuing agents should report the model configuration, generation contract, execution path, operating point, and final-state validator rather than treat a matched score as an intrinsic model property.

</details>


### [279] [OmniScientist: An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558)
*Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Tianjie Ju,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniScientist，一个端到端的全模态AI科学家系统，能够直接从异构原始证据（如图像、信号、音频、视频、3D结构等）进行多学科研究。系统通过感知层和三个自主代理（构思、实验、写作）在确定性流程中运行，并在代码层面执行新颖性筛查、统计有效性、执行溯源和数值可追溯性检查。在36个覆盖5大学科家族、4类科学证据家族的真实数据案例中，系统全部完成了从原始数据到成稿论文的完整流程，平均论文评分为6.3，直接感知相比仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体在所有7个评估维度上均更优，且在85%的头部对比中胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统通常只对文本、代码、标签或预计算摘要进行推理，无法访问对科学发现至关重要的空间、时间、跨通道和程序性关系。工作流覆盖率不足以提供完整证据访问，需要让代理直接从原始异构证据中感知科学信息。

Method: OmniScientist由感知层和3个自主代理（构思、实验、写作）组成，运行在确定性流水线中。系统在代码层面执行构思、严谨性和声明检查，以实现新颖性筛查、统计有效性、执行溯源和数值可追溯性。感知允许观测数据在整个研究生命周期中塑造研究问题、实验决策和最终声明。

Result: 在36个真实数据案例（覆盖5个学科家族、4类科学证据家族，以及图像、信号、音频、视频、3D结构、轨迹、表格、公式和图等模态）中，系统全部完成从原始数据到编译稿的完整路径，平均论文评分为6.3。与仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体相比，直接感知在所有7个评估维度上更优，并在85%的头部对比中获胜。

Conclusion: 生命周期全模态感知对于证据驱动的科学发现至关重要，OmniScientist为构建广泛能力的AI科学家提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled AI scientists to automate increasingly complete research workflows, from hypothesis generation and code execution to manuscript preparation. Yet workflow coverage alone does not provide access to the full evidence on which scientific discovery depends. Existing systems typically reason over text, code, labels, or precomputed summaries, leaving scientifically decisive spatial, temporal, cross-channel, and procedural relations unavailable to the agent. We introduce OmniScientist, an end-to-end, omni-modal AI scientist that conducts multidisciplinary research directly from heterogeneous raw evidence. A perception layer and 3 autonomous agents for ideation, experiment, and writeup operate within a deterministic pipeline, allowing observations to shape research questions, experimental decisions, and final claims throughout the research lifecycle. By running idea, rigour, and claim checks in code, the system enforces novelty screening, statistical validity, execution provenance, and numerical traceability. We evaluate OmniScientist on 36 real-data cases spanning 5 discipline families, 4 families of scientific evidence, and modalities including images, signals, audio, video, 3-D structures, trajectories, tables, formulae, and graphs. The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [280] [Offering Microsecond-Scale Cross-VM Core Elasticity on Colocated Lightweight Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12633)
*Yibo Yan,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperFlux，一种基于commodity-KVM的超轻量级VM基板，使VM的并行宽度（物理核心数）在运行时具有弹性，可在13μs内跨VM移动核心，冷启动仅需1.37ms，内存占用3.2MB，同时支持多核并行，显著降低尾部延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器平台将多种工作负载共置于快速启动、内存精简的VM中以提高部署密度。但为每个VM过度配置峰值资源会损害密度，需要基础设施能在微秒时间尺度上将物理核心动态分配给突发的延迟敏感型VM，并在突袭平息后回收。现有VM基板均无法实现：传统VM的vCPU热插拔需毫秒级，Firecracker在启动时固定核心数，超轻量VM则完全放弃多核执行。

Method: HyperFlux基于commodity-KVM构建超轻量VM基板，使VM的并行宽度（支撑VM的物理核心数）在运行时具有弹性。通过优化核心迁移路径，实现跨VM的核心迁移仅需13μs（即使从繁忙的捐赠者VM强制回收），比vCPU热插拔快数个数量级。保持了3.2MB内存占用的超轻量特性，冷启动1.37ms，同时唯一支持多核并行。

Result: 核心迁移仅需13μs，冷启动1.37ms，内存占用3.2MB。在共置场景下，高负载时高优先级VM的尾部延迟相比Firecracker和Cloud Hypervisor的静态核心共享降低高达10倍；在变化的负载突发下，相比cgroup和vCPU热插拔方案，提供更低且更稳定的尾部延迟。

Conclusion: HyperFlux首次在同一基板上同时实现了超轻量VM的快速启动/低内存占用与动态多核弹性并行，弥合了当前无服务器基础设施中密度与尾部延迟保护之间的鸿沟，为无服务器平台提供了更优的共置调度基板。

Abstract: Serverless platforms commonly colocate many diverse workloads, each in a fast-booting, memory-lean virtual machine (VM), to improve deployment density. Overprovisioning each VM for its peak protects tail latency during traffic bursts but hurts density; maintaining high density while effectively protecting tail latency requires the infrastructure to be able to shift physical cores, at a microsecond timescale, to whichever latency-sensitive VM is bursting and reclaim them as the burst subsides. No VM substrate delivers this: conventional VMs resize a guest's cores only through a millisecond-scale vCPU hot-plug path, Firecracker fixes a VM's core count at boot, and the ultralight VMs that boot fastest drop multicore execution entirely.
  We present HyperFlux, a commodity-KVM ultralight VM substrate that makes a VM's parallelism width (the number of physical cores backing it) elastic at runtime. We show that HyperFlux can move a core across VMs in merely 13$μ$s, even when forcibly reclaiming it from a busy donor, orders of magnitude faster than vCPU hot-plug. A HyperFlux VM incurs only a 3.2MB memory footprint and can cold-boot in 1.37ms, on par with the fastest-booting ultralight VMs, while uniquely supporting multicore parallelism. Under colocation, it can reduce high-priority VMs' tail latency by up to 10x under high load compared to static core-sharing with Firecracker and Cloud Hypervisor, and deliver a lower and more stable tail latency compared to using cgroup and vCPU hot-plug under changing load bursts.

</details>


### [281] [InFactPlanner: Planning Sustainable Geo-Distributed LLM Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12915)
*Nicoletta Tsiopani,Moysis Symeonides,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了InFactPlanner，一个基于追踪驱动的决策支持框架，用于对LLM推理的可持续AI数据中心部署进行"what-if"分析，涵盖单站点和地理分布式场景，能够估计功耗、能量、碳排放、水消耗、延迟和服务器利用率，并支持硬件选择、可再生能源部署、地理位置和路由策略等多种决策分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的快速增长使得可持续性关注点从一次性训练转向持续服务，但基础设施决策往往需要在大规模基础设施建成之前进行比较，直接测量成本高、速度慢甚至不可行。因此需要一种在部署前就能评估不同方案可持续性影响的决策支持工具。

Method: InFactPlanner将查询追踪、硬件模型配置文件、候选站点配置、PUE/WUE参数、可再生能源生成模型和时变电网碳强度结合起来，估算功耗、能量、碳排放、水消耗、延迟和服务器利用率。框架将底层服务效应抽象为可配置的硬件模型配置文件，支持快速比较站点选择、容量布局、硬件、模型、可再生能源整合和路由选择。

Result: 通过重现参考LLM推理能量估计来验证能量核算流程，偏差小于10%；在多数据中心和服务器数量场景下评估了可扩展性；展示了硬件选择、可再生能源布局、地理部署和碳感知路由等多种场景驱动的决策分析。结果显示，可持续性最优的选择可能与延迟最优的选择不同，且部署的碳价值强烈依赖于当地电网结构。

Conclusion: InFactPlanner为AI数据中心运营者在基础设施建成前提供了一种低成本、高效的可持续性决策支持手段。研究表明可持续性最优方案与延迟最优方案可能存在分歧，且电网碳强度是决定部署碳价值的关键因素，强调了决策分析在可持续AI基础设施规划中的重要性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of LLM inference is shifting sustainability concerns from one-time training to continuous serving, where infrastructure decisions shape energy use, carbon emissions, water consumption, and service quality. Yet operators often need to compare deployment alternatives before large-scale infrastructure is built, making direct measurement costly, slow, and sometimes infeasible. We present InFactPlanner, a trace-driven decision-support framework for what-if analysis of sustainable AI data center deployment for LLM inference across single and geo-distributed sites. InFactPlanner combines query traces, hardware-model profiles, candidate site configurations, PUE/WUE parameters, renewable generation models, and time-varying grid carbon intensity to estimate power, energy, carbon emissions, water use, latency, and server utilization. The framework abstracts low-level serving effects into configurable hardware-model profiles, enabling rapid comparison of site selection, capacity placement, hardware, model, renewable integration, and routing choices. We validate the energy accounting pipeline by reproducing reference LLM inference energy estimates with less than 10% deviation, evaluate scalability across multiple data centers and server counts, and demonstrate scenario-driven decision analyses for hardware selection, renewable placement, geographic deployment, and carbon-aware routing. Our results show that sustainability-optimal choices can differ from latency-optimal ones, and that the carbon value of deployment depends strongly on the local grid mix.

</details>


### [282] [Efficient Randomized LL/SC that Preserves History Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12946)
*Dante Bencivenga,Homa Habashi,Philipp Woelfel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究在多进程系统中实现线性化LL/SC对象的随机化算法，在弱适应性对手模型下使用FADD、CAS和寄存器，将空间复杂度从确定性算法的O(n²τ+m)改进到O(nτ+m)，且支持quiescently history-independent（QHI）性质，使STOC 2025的QHI动态哈希算法可在现有硬件上实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LL/SC对象是分布式并发系统中的重要原语，但硬件不直接支持该操作。已知最佳确定性算法需要Ω(n²τ+m)个CAS和寄存器，且此前没有可比的随机化算法。此外，STOC 2025提出的QHI动态哈希算法依赖硬件LL/SC对象，但硬件并不提供该原语，需要高效的软件实现来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用随机化技术，在CAS和寄存器之外引入FADD（fetch-and-add）操作，设计了一种满足线性化的LL/SC对象实现算法，能够抵抗弱适应性对手（weak adaptive adversary）。同时该实现具备QHI性质：当对象上无待处理操作且无进程持有未完成的LL操作时，内部内存状态由m个LL/SC对象的值唯一确定。

Result: 空间复杂度达到O(nτ+m)，相比确定性算法的O(n²τ+m)有显著改进。当m=O(1)时，该界限匹配使用CAS和寄存器的下界（Aghazadeh & Woelfel, PODC 2015）。将算法应用于STOC 2025的QHI动态哈希算法时，在m=Ω(n)的合理假设下，渐进步复杂度和空间复杂度均不增加。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明了利用随机化（结合FADD原语）可以有效降低LL/SC软件实现的空间开销，同时保持QHI等关键性质，从而使依赖LL/SC的先进并发算法（如STOC 2025的QHI动态哈希）能够在现有硬件上直接部署。

Abstract: We study the fundamental problem of implementing $m$ linearizable LL/SC objects with constant expected step complexity in a system of $n$ processes, using bounded base objects commonly available in hardware. Assuming that each process may have at most $τ$ outstanding LL operations, the best known deterministic algorithm requires $Ω(n^2τ+ m)$ base objects (CAS and registers) [Blelloch and Wei, DISC 2020]. Previously, no comparable randomized algorithm was known.
  By employing randomization and FADD in addition to CAS and registers, we obtain a space bound of $O(nτ+m)$ against the weak adaptive adversary. For $m=O(1)$ this matches a lower bound for algorithms using CAS and registers [Aghazadeh and Woelfel, PODC 2015].
  In addition, our object can be employed by quiescently history-independent (QHI) algorithms: Whenever no operation on the object is pending and no process has an outstanding LL operation, its internal memory state is uniquely determined by the values of the $m$ LL/SC objects. An important application is a QHI dynamic hashing algorithm presented at STOC 2025, which uses $Θ(m)$ hardware LL/SC objects to maintain a hash table of size $m$ [Attiya, Bender, Farach-Colton, Oshman, and Schiller, STOC 2025]. But LL/SC is not available in hardware, and prior to our work no wait-free or efficiently lock-free software implementation of LL/SC with similar properties was known. Our work demonstrates that one can actually implement the hashing algorithm on available hardware, without an asymptotic increase in step and space complexity, under the reasonable assumption that $m=Ω(n)$.

</details>


### [283] [TEMPO: Makespan-Aware Expert-Parallel Load Balancing Across Memory- and Compute-Bound Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13057)
*Jie Li,Chenxin Jia,Jinliang Shen,Cunzhuang Liu,Ruiyi Ding,Jianwen Xian,Kang He,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出现有专家并行（EP）MoE调度器假设专家执行时间与token数或激活专家数线性相关，但实测显示真实模型呈"最大仿射"分段特性（HBM权重流主导的平坦区 + 分组GEMM的线性区）。作者将每批次调度形式化为固定费用makespan问题，提出makespan感知调度器Sys，在毫秒级求解，在8-GPU测试台上优于最佳固定基线最多15.5%，端到端在Qwen3-235B上提升4-6%吞吐并降低约15.6% p99延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EP MoE调度器（如EPLB、LPLB、UltraEP、METRO）依赖不准确的线性假设来平衡GPU负载，实际测量证明这些代理指标在真实硬件上失真，导致调度质量欠佳，需要建立更精确的负载模型与调度方法。

Method: 通过两代数据中心GPU的微基准测试，建立最大仿射执行时间模型 t=max(a+bG, c+βN)，刻画HBM权重流主导的平坦区和分组GEMM的线性区；将每批次调度形式化为固定费用makespan问题（两个完全复制GPU上NP-hard）；设计makespan感知调度器Sys，离线关键路径毫秒级求解，SGLang集成中把调度与计数采集融合为一个图内内核。

Result: 8-GPU Testbed A上，Sys在所有场景下与最佳固定基线差距在1%以内，在冷热专家混合场景最多领先15.5%。Testbed B上，Qwen3-235B吞吐提升4-6%，p99延迟降低约15.6%；DeepSeek-V3（通信主导场景）仅表现为机制开销，无明显收益。

Conclusion: 核心主张是一张"相图"而非普适性胜利：它能在部署前预测何时该调度器有效、何时无效。两个不同模型的端到端结果（一胜一平）恰好验证了该相图的可预测性。

Abstract: In expert-parallel (EP) MoE serving, every layer synchronizes at the slowest GPU. Dispatchers balance token counts (EPLB, LPLB, UltraEP) or activated-expert counts (METRO), assuming expert time is linear in one. Measurements on two datacenter GPU generations show it is neither: below $\nstar\!\approx\!156$--$168$ tokens, HBM weight streaming dominates---cost attaches to \emph{activated replicas}, not tokens; above it, grouped GEMM rounds tokens to 128-tile $M$-tiles, so \emph{splitting} an expert adds padded compute. A max-affine profile $t=\max(a+bG,\,c+βN)$ captures both regimes. Realistic decode batches hold hot experts in the linear regime and cold in the flat \emph{simultaneously}; recorded batches show proxy dispatches differ by $1.4$--$1.6\times$ in modeled block time (p95 up to $1.7\times$), and \emph{which} proxy wins flips with the regime. We formalize per-batch dispatch as a fixed-charge makespan problem---NP-hard on two fully replicated GPUs, polynomial in degenerate limits---and present \sys{}, a makespan-aware dispatcher solving it in milliseconds off the critical path; its SGLang integration runs out-of-process and fuses dispatch with count collection into one in-graph kernel. Anchored by an 8-GPU Testbed~A microbenchmark, \sys{} stays within 1\% of the best fixed baseline everywhere and wins by up to $15.5\%$ where regimes mix. End-to-end on Testbed~B, Qwen3-235B (inside the win region) gains $4$--$6\%$ throughput and cuts p99 latency by ${\sim}15.6\%$; DeepSeek-V3 (outside, communication-dominated) shows only mechanism cost. A phase diagram, not a universal win, is the claim: it predicts both outcomes before deployment.

</details>


### [284] [Meshlib: In-Process Policy Enforcement for Sidecar-less Service Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13107)
*Habib Mostafaei,Tom van Liempd*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Meshlib是一个基于Cilium构建的无Sidecar服务网格扩展，通过应用内嵌库执行L7策略、利用eBPF数据平面处理传输层功能，消除了Sidecar代理带来的延迟，在TrainTicket基准上实现了最低的端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务网格依赖每个Pod的Sidecar代理，导致每次请求都进行冗余的应用层解析，带来显著的延迟和资源开销。如何在消除Sidecar的同时不牺牲安全保证是核心挑战。

Method: Meshlib作为Cilium的控制平面扩展，引入非侵入式应用绑定库在应用进程内强制执行L7策略，同时将传输层身份和路由委托给Cilium基于eBPF的数据平面。这种职责分离消除了Sidecar导致的延迟，并保持服务网格的安全语义，架构与未修改的服务完全互操作，支持增量部署。

Result: 在TrainTicket基准上，对37个服务强制执行126条安全策略，与Istio、Linkerd和未修改的Cilium对比，Meshlib在所有评估配置中实现了最低的端到端延迟，同时资源开销相当。

Conclusion: Meshlib成功证明了可以通过分离L7策略执行（应用内库）与传输层功能（eBPF数据平面）来构建无Sidecar的服务网格，在消除延迟开销的同时保持安全语义和兼容性，为服务网格架构提供了一种新的设计范式。

Abstract: Service meshes facilitate service-to-service communication and enforce security policies in microservice architectures. However, they often depend on per-pod sidecar proxies, which introduce significant latency and resource overhead due to redundant application-layer parsing on every request. Eliminating sidecars without compromising security guarantees remains a central challenge. To address this, we introduce Meshlib, a sidecar-less service mesh extension built on Cilium as a control-plane extension. Meshlib incorporates a non-intrusive application-bound library that enforces Layer-7 policies within the application process, while delegating transport-level identity and routing to Cilium's eBPF-based data plane. This separation of responsibilities removes sidecar-induced latency and maintains the security semantics of the service mesh. The architecture remains fully interoperable with unmodified services, enabling incremental adoption within existing deployments. We evaluate Meshlib against Istio, Linkerd, and unmodified Cilium on the TrainTicket benchmark, enforcing 126 security policies across 37 services and show that it achieves the lowest end-to-end latency of all evaluated configurations with comparable resource overhead.

</details>


### [285] [Validation-Centric AI-Assisted GPU Porting of a 250,000+ Line Legacy Weather Simulation Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13122)
*Tetsuya Hoshino,Masaya Kato,Kazuhisa Tsuboki,Daichi Mukunoki,Takahiro Katagiri,Toshihiro Hanawa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过CReSS（25万行Fortran天气模拟代码）案例研究，提出了一种以验证为中心的AI辅助GPU移植工作流。该工作流利用AI智能体提取OpenMP区域、生成基于转储（dump）的内核基准测试、应用OpenACC转换，并通过元素级比较和应用级验证来确保移植后数值正确性。在真实台风模拟中，工作流为162个目标内核生成了通过数值验证的GPU实现，实现了5.1倍应用级加速，并检测出5个内核因浮点精度和内在函数差异导致的数值不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型遗留科学应用不仅是旧代码，更是经过长期发展、观测对比和域研究积累的科学资产。GPU移植必须在适应GPU中心化HPC系统的同时保持科学有效性。然而现有AI辅助移植缺乏以验证为中心的流程设计，难以保证移植后的数值正确性。

Method: 提出验证中心的AI辅助GPU移植工作流：（1）使用AI智能体提取OpenMP区域；（2）从物理有意义的模拟状态生成基于转储的内核基准测试；（3）应用OpenACC转换；（4）通过与转储参考数据逐元素比较及应用级验证来确认结果正确性。

Result: 在真实台风模拟中，工作流成功为162个目标内核生成数值验证过的GPU实现，实现5.1倍应用级加速，且开发时间在实用范围内。同时检测到5个内核因浮点数与内在函数差异（包括阈值敏感的分支发散和抵消效应）导致的数值偏差，为应用开发者提供了反馈。

Conclusion: 对于需要基于转储验证的大型遗留科学应用，实用的AI辅助GPU移植必须管理跨会话上下文、运行时状态重建以及从小型静态分析遗漏中恢复的高成本。AI辅助GPU移植不仅需要代码生成，更需要以验证为中心的工作流设计。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have made CLI-based AI agents a practical tool for accelerating GPU porting of large legacy scientific applications. Such applications, however, are not merely old code bases; they are scientific assets whose credibility has been accumulated through long-term development, comparison with observations, and use in domain studies. GPU porting must therefore preserve this scientific validity while adapting the implementation to GPU-centric HPC systems. This paper presents a validation-centric AI-assisted GPU porting workflow through a case study of CReSS, a legacy Fortran weather simulation code with more than 250,000 lines. The workflow uses an AI agent to extract OpenMP regions, generate dump-based kernel benchmarks from physically meaningful simulation states, apply OpenACC transformations, and validate results through element-wise comparison with dumped reference data and application-level validation. Using a real typhoon simulation, the workflow produced numerically validated GPU implementations for 162 target kernels and achieved a 5.1x application-level speedup within practical wall-clock development cost. In particular, it detected numerical discrepancies in five kernels caused by floating-point and intrinsic-function differences, including threshold-sensitive branch divergence and cancellation effects, enabling feedback to the application developers. The case study suggests that, for large legacy scientific applications requiring dump-based validation, practical AI-assisted GPU porting must manage session-spanning context, runtime-state reconstruction, and costly recovery from small static-analysis omissions. These findings demonstrate that AI-assisted GPU porting requires not only code generation, but validation-centric workflow design.

</details>


### [286] [LipCache: A Local Inference Proxy with Certified Caching for Edge Image Classification Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13144)
*Zhengzhe Xiang,Yinlin Chen,Fuli Ying,Binbin Zhou,Hailiang Zhao,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LipCache是一个面向图像分类的认证语义缓存框架，通过在部署的主模型MainNet之外引入一个受Lipschitz约束的轻量级网络GuardNet，将缓存命中判断从经验阈值测试转化为基于局部分类边界和分类头谱范数的几何认证决策，在CIFAR、Tiny-ImageNet和SVHN上实现最高1.65倍加速，同时保证所有缓存命中满足certified-consistency条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘侧视觉服务日益追求低延迟、高吞吐，但现有语义缓存方法依赖经验性相似度阈值，虽提升命中率却常在决策边界附近引入静默误分类，缺乏理论保障，难以在推理成本与可靠性之间取得可靠平衡。

Method: 在不修改已部署的主模型MainNet的前提下，引入轻量级网络GuardNet将输入映射到受Lipschitz约束的低维特征空间；基于局部分类边界和分类头的谱范数计算每个样本的认证复用半径；运行时仅当查询特征落入认证复用球内才复用缓存结果，否则回退到MainNet，将缓存命中从经验阈值测试转化为具有明确理论边界的几何认证决策。

Result: 在CIFAR、Tiny-ImageNet和SVHN等标准图像分类任务上实现最高1.65倍测量加速比，端到端精度损失有限；所有接受的缓存命中均满足GuardNet侧的certified-consistency条件；增强的GuardNet训练方案在Tiny-ImageNet多类扩展中显著提升缓存命中率，同时保持100%的certified-consistency率。

Conclusion: 逐样本认证复用能够在保持理论一致性的前提下减少主模型回退次数，为边缘侧提供了一种兼顾可靠性与效率的缓存辅助推理方案。

Abstract: As edge-side vision services continue to expand toward low-latency, high-throughput scenarios, reducing the inference cost of vision models without sacrificing reliability has become a central concern. Existing semantic caching methods largely rely on empirical similarity thresholds; while such thresholds improve hit rates, they tend to introduce silent misclassifications near decision boundaries. To address this, we propose \texttt{LipCache}, a certified semantic caching framework for image classification. Without modifying the existing deployed main model, \texttt{MainNet}, the framework introduces a lightweight network, \texttt{GuardNet}, that maps inputs into a low-dimensional feature space subject to a Lipschitz constraint. It then computes a per-sample certified reuse radius from the local classification margin and the spectral norm of the classification head. At runtime, a cached result is reused only when the query feature falls inside the certified reuse ball; otherwise, the query falls back to \texttt{MainNet}. Thus, cache hits are transformed from empirical threshold tests into geometric certification decisions with explicit theoretical boundaries. Across standard image classification tasks like CIFAR, Tiny-ImageNet, and SVHN, \texttt{LipCache} achieves a measured speedup of up to $1.65\times$ with limited end-to-end accuracy degradation, while all accepted cache hits satisfy the \texttt{GuardNet}-side certified-consistency condition. Furthermore, an enhanced \texttt{GuardNet} training recipe substantially improves cache hit rates in the Tiny-ImageNet multi-class extension while maintaining a certified-consistency rate of $100\%$. These results demonstrate that per-sample certified reuse can reduce main-model fallback while preserving theoretical consistency, providing a feasible approach to reliable cache-assisted inference at the edge.

</details>


### [287] [Triangle-Free Coloring in LOCAL via Resilient Lovász Local Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13357)
*Peter Davies-Peck,Xusheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过采用Davies的"弹性"（resilience）定义，对Pettie-Su的分布式三角形无关图着色算法进行改造，使得其中涉及的Lovász Local Lemma（LLL）实例能够在$\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮内求解，从而将三角形无关图着色的复杂度降至$O(k)+\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮，首次实现用$\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮对三角形无关图使用$o(\Delta)$种颜色着色，并同样适用于girth-5图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLL在分布式算法中具有核心重要性，但分布式LLL本身求解困难（最快算法需$O(\log_\Delta n)$或$O(\Delta/\log\Delta)+\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮）。三角形无关图着色是检验次对数随机分布式算法技术的基准问题，而现有最优算法Pettie-Su需多次调用分布式LLL，LLL成为瓶颈。作者希望消除这一瓶颈。

Method: 作者改编Pettie-Su算法，利用Davies [SODA 2023]提出的"resilience"定义，使算法中产生的LLL实例能在$\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮内求解。当$k=\log^{\omega(1)}\log n$时甚至无需调用LLL，从而完全消除LLL作为瓶颈的问题。该框架同时被扩展到girth-5图的着色。

Result: 首次获得$\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮的算法，可用$o(\Delta)$种颜色对三角形无关图着色。同时获得girth-5图的伴随算法，使用$(1+\varepsilon)\Delta/\ln\Delta$种颜色，在$O(k)+\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮内完成，匹配已知最优存在性上界。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙的resilience技术将LLL步骤从算法瓶颈中消除，显著推进了三角形无关图的分布式着色研究，实现了次对数复杂度下的$o(\Delta)$着色，为分布式LLL在着色问题中的应用提供了新的路径。

Abstract: The Lovász Local Lemma (LLL) is a probabilistic tool that has been shown to be of central importance in the study of distributed algorithms. For example, the constructive LLL is known to be complete for the class of locally-checkable labeling problems with $o(\log n)$ randomized complexities in the LOCAL model. One classic application of the LLL is in coloring graphs with some sparse structure, such as triangle-free graphs. Triangle-free coloring therefore serves as a benchmark problem for techniques for sublogarithmic randomized distributed algorithms. The state-of-the-art distributed triangle-free coloring algorithm of Pettie and Su [ICALP 2013, Information and Computation 2015] uses $\fracΔ{k}$ colors (where $k$ can be up to $(\frac14 - \varepsilon)\ln Δ$) and consists of $O(k+\log^* n)$ applications of the distributed LLL. However, the distributed LLL is itself a difficult problem; despite significant study, the fastest algorithms known require $O(\log_Δn)$ or $O(\fracΔ{\logΔ})+\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds. In this work, we adapt the Pettie-Su's algorithm so that the resulting LLL instances can be solved in $\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, by employing the 'resilience' definition of Davies [SODA 2023]. This gives an $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ complexity (since the LLL is not needed when $k= \log^{ω(1)}\log n$), essentially causing the LLL steps to no longer be the bottleneck of the algorithm. As a corollary we obtain the first $\log^{O(1)}\log n$-round algorithms for coloring triangle-free graphs with $o(Δ)$ colors. The same framework also yields a companion girth-$5$ algorithm, using $(1+\varepsilon)Δ/\ln Δ$ colors in $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, matching the best known existential upper bound for the number of colors.

</details>


### [288] [Fast Tendermint: Speeding Up a Foundational Consensus Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13434)
*Preston Vander Vos,Daniel Cason*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Fast Tendermint，将Tendermint共识协议适配到n > 5f的拜占庭容错设置，在良好情况下将决策延迟从三步降至两步，同时保留Tendermint的领导者轮换结构，并给出了形式化规范与模型检查验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tendermint是最广泛研究和部署的BFT共识协议之一，但其在部分同步模型下需要三步通信才能达成决策。随着对低延迟区块链的追求，最近的研究表明在f < n/5条件下共识可以在两步内解决，作者希望在此设置下进一步优化Tendermint。

Method: Fast Tendermint将Tendermint的prevote和precommit两步合并为单一投票步骤，并将locked和valid状态合并，从而将良好情况下的通信步骤从三步压缩到两步。协议保留了Tendermint原有的领导者轮换结构，并引入了n > 5f的更强假设。验证方面使用Quint（TLA+的现代语法）进行形式化规范编写和模型检查。

Result: 论文给出了Fast Tendermint协议关于一致性（agreement）、有效性（validity）和终止性（termination）的形式化证明，并提供了用Quint编写的形式化规范用于模型检查协议的正确性。

Conclusion: Fast Tendermint成功将Tendermint的决策延迟从三步降低到两步（基于更强的n > 5f容错假设），同时保持了原有协议的结构化优点，并通过形式化方法验证了协议的安全性。

Abstract: Tendermint is among the most widely studied and deployed Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols, owing in part to its native leader-rotation mechanism that subsumes complex view changes. Like most partially-synchronous BFT protocols, Tendermint tolerates $f < n/3$ Byzantine processes and decides in three communication steps. Motivated by the push for lower-latency blockchains, a recent line of work shows that consensus can be solved in two communication steps when $f < n/5$. We present Fast Tendermint, an adaptation of Tendermint to the $n > 5f$ setting that decides in two communication steps in the good case, while preserving Tendermint's leader-rotation structure. Fast Tendermint collapses Tendermint's prevote and precommit steps into a single voting step and merges the $locked$ and $valid$ state. We give proofs of agreement, validity, and termination, and a formal specification in Quint, a modern surface syntax for TLA+, used to model-check the protocol.

</details>


### [289] [OpScale: Operator-level Provisioning and Autoscaling for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13499)
*Xingqi Cui,Chieh-Jan Mike Liang,Ziang Tang,Jiarong Xing,Haoran Qiu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OpScale是一个面向LLM云GPU集群的算子级（operator-level）弹性伸缩编排框架，将缩放单元从整个模型细化为单个算子，通过profiling、provisioning、placement和runtime serving四阶段设计，在40块A100和24块GB200上验证，相比现有方案最高可减少36.3%的GPU数量、降低28%的功耗，或在固定预算下提升44%的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理服务的自动扩缩容以整个模型为最小缩放单元，这种粗粒度方式无法捕捉推理工作负载的细粒度动态特性，导致在突发流量下违反SLO或造成GPU利用率低下。论文通过特征分析发现算子层面存在显著的异构性，揭示了算子级弹性是一种可行的缩放原语。

Method: OpScale框架包含四个核心阶段：1）profiling：对算子级资源需求进行特征刻画；2）provisioning：基于算子级弹性进行资源供给决策；3）placement：将算子合理放置到GPU上；4）runtime serving：运行时调度执行。针对算子级操作带来的高复杂度和搜索空间爆炸问题，设计了相应的求解策略。

Result: 在生产负载数据集上、最高40块A100和24块GB200的实验环境中，OpScale在满足SLO的前提下最多可节省36.3%的GPU数量和28%的功耗；或者在固定成本预算下可获得44%的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 算子级弹性伸缩相比模型级（monolithic）缩放能更精细地匹配推理工作负载的动态需求，OpScale证明了在克服复杂度和空间爆炸挑战后，算子级编排是提升LLM云集群成本效益的有效路径。

Abstract: Achieving cost efficiency while meeting strict user-facing SLOs (e.g., time-to-first-token) remains a fundamental challenge for cloud GPU clusters serving large language models (LLMs). Autoscaling is the key mechanism for cluster resource management, yet a basic system design question is open for serving LLMs: what should be the unit of scaling? Existing approaches primarily treat the entire model as a monolithic scaling unit--simple but unable to capture the fine-grained dynamics of inference workloads. As a result, such coarse-grained scaling often leads to either SLO violations under bursty demand or significant GPU under-utilization.
  Our characterization reveals substantial operator heterogeneity, exposing operator-level elasticity as a viable scaling primitive. We present OpScale, a practical operator-level orchestration framework of profiling, provisioning, placement, and runtime serving. OpScale is designed to tackle the high complexity and the space explosion problem, arising from operating at this finer granularity. Evaluated with production traces on up to 40 A100s and 24 GB200s, OpScale attains SLOs with up to 36.3% fewer GPUs and 28% less power, or achieves 44% higher throughput under fixed cost budgets.

</details>


### [290] [RoutePack: Expert Placement and Attention-Aware Data Packing for MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12146)
*Yibo Shen,Xudong Han,Xiaowei Zhu,Gen Li,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RoutePack是一个用于混合专家（MoE）强化学习训练的层级规划器，通过协调逐层专家重路由和联合注意力-专家感知的数据打包，在优化器步窗口内解决序列组合与token路由的双重负载均衡问题，在Ling-3.0-Tiny和Ling-3.0-Flash上分别提升吞吐量8.85%和14.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型的强化学习训练中，序列组合决定了数据并行微批次的密集注意力计算量，而token路由决定了专家并行秩上的稀疏专家计算量，两者相互耦合且独立优化其中任意一个都可能将瓶颈转移到另一个，因此需要联合优化。

Method: RoutePack采用层级规划方法：（1）首先利用rollout阶段的路由重放信息，在每一MoE层独立放置专家；（2）将样本打包进最小已认证（或已知最优可行）数量的token上限执行行，并优化其数据并行布局；（3）使用窗口归一化的线性二次注意力代理与逐层物理EP秩峰值相结合的目标函数，最小化最慢EDP分片的累积成本；（4）采用并行种群退火算法搜索固定行可行布局，同时保持样本覆盖、容量、非空单元、微批次数量相等及通信拓扑等约束；（5）通过状态一致的具体化保持逻辑top-k路由和现有MoE内核，无需微批次级别专家复制。

Result: 在Ling-3.0-Tiny和Ling-3.0-Flash上，专家重路由分别提升平均token吞吐量3.80%和10.50%，路由感知打包额外增加4.86%和3.98%，整体RoutePack相比基线提升8.85%和14.89%的吞吐量。

Conclusion: RoutePack通过联合优化专家路由和数据打包，有效解决了MoE RL训练中的双重负载均衡问题，证明了层级规划方法在不同规模模型上均能带来显著的吞吐量提升，且无需修改现有MoE内核。

Abstract: Training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models for reinforcement learning (RL) couples two load-balancing problems: sequence composition determines dense attention work in each data-parallel microbatch, while token routing determines sparse expert work on expert-parallel ranks. Optimizing either alone can shift the bottleneck to the other. In MoE RL, rollout-time routing replay exposes every sample's sequence length and layer-wise expert demand before its training step. We present RoutePack, a hierarchical planner that coordinates state-consistent, layer-wise expert rerouting with joint attention- and expert-aware data packing over an optimizer-step window. RoutePack first places experts independently at each MoE layer using aggregate routing demand. It then packs samples into the smallest certified, or best-known feasible, number of token-capped execution rows and optimizes their DP layout with a projected EDP-shard-aware objective. The objective combines a window-normalized linear-quadratic attention proxy with per-layer physical EP-rank peaks and minimizes the accumulated cost of the slowest EDP shard. Parallel population annealing searches fixed-row feasible layouts while preserving sample coverage, capacity, nonempty cells, equal microbatch counts, and communicator topology. State-consistent materialization preserves logical top-k routing and existing MoE kernels without microbatch-level expert replication. Across Ling-3.0-Tiny and Ling-3.0-Flash, expert rerouting improves mean trainer-measured token throughput by 3.80% and 10.50%, while routing-aware packing adds another 4.86% and 3.98%, respectively. Overall, RoutePack improves throughput by 8.85% and 14.89% over the baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [291] [DCBA: Detection of Collaborative Black-Hole Attacks in Connected Dominated Set using Baiting Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12347)
*Lata B T,Venugopal K R*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的协作黑洞攻击检测与隔离技术（LW-CBH），通过增强AODV路由协议，利用基于定时器的诱饵过程和反向追踪机制，在MAC层通过控制状态消息（R-SEQ和C-SEQ）检测并隔离恶意节点，仿真结果表明该技术在吞吐量、分组投递率、平均端到端时延和归一化路由开销方面均有良好表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MANET网络由于缺乏中心管理、节点动态移动等特点，容易遭受各种攻击，其中黑洞攻击（特别是协作式黑洞攻击）会严重降低网络性能。现有的AODV路由协议在动态拓扑变化时无法有效检测恶意节点，因此需要提出一种轻量级且高效的方案来检测和隔离协作黑洞攻击。

Method: 提出LW-CBH方案，通过增强AODV路由协议实现：1) 采用基于定时器的诱饵（baiting）过程主动引诱恶意节点暴露；2) 利用反向追踪设置定位恶意节点；3) 通过连通支配集节点的MAC层控制状态消息（R-SEQ和C-SEQ）检测恶意行为并实施隔离。

Result: 使用NS-2.35离散事件模拟器对提出的技术进行仿真，评估指标包括吞吐量、分组投递率、平均端到端时延和归一化路由开销。

Conclusion: LW-CBH技术能够有效检测并隔离协作黑洞攻击节点，在动态拓扑变化的MANET环境中优于现有AODV协议，提高了网络的整体性能和可靠性。

Abstract: Mobile Ad-hoc Network (MANET) is temporary and dynamic network topology, wherein nodes are mobile in nature and distributed randomly in a network area. In MANET, nodes cooperate with each other to operate and forward data through multihop communication between source and destination. MANET is exposed to different types of attacks due to absence of central administration. However, some nodes decline to cooperate, misbehaves and appears to be malicious affecting network functionality and connectivity. Providing security and identifying malicious node has become one of the challenging research topics in MANET. Black-hole attack is considered to be most popular attack that degrades the overall network performance. Black-hole node falsely advertises the shortest path to destination intentionally to disrupt the network communication resulting in packet drop. In collaborative black-hole attacks, multiple black-hole nodes cooperate and launch attacks in order to degrade network reliability. In this article we propose a Lightweight technique to detect and isolate Collaborative Black-Hole attacks (LW-CBH) by enhancing existing AODV routing protocol. In this scheme a timer based baiting process and reverse tracing setup is used to detect malicious node through control status message in MAC layer which are Reply Sequence (R-SEQ) and Code Sequence (C-SEQ) message of connected dominated set of nodes. However existing AODV routing protocol fails to detect malicious node during dynamic topology changing in MANET. Simulation of proposed technique is performed using discrete event simulator tool NS-2.35. The simulation results are evaluated for throughput, packet delivery ratio, average end-to-end delay and normalized routing overhead.

</details>


### [292] [The energetic cost of mitigating AI attacks in cellular networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12431)
*Adrián Losada,Hao Qiang Luo-Chen,David Segura,Carlos S. Alvarez-Merino,Milan Groshev,Emil J. Khatib,Raquel Barco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了O-RAN架构中基于AI/ML的蜂窝网络所面临的安全漏洞与防御策略能耗问题，指出防御措施虽然有效但会带来被忽视的计算负载与能源成本，并刻画了防御技术的能耗特征，阐述了ML准确性、鲁棒性与能效三者之间的权衡挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝网络全面集成AI/ML算法（如O-RAN的RIC）带来了优化收益，但数据依赖性也引入了新的安全漏洞；现有缓解策略虽有效却产生额外计算负载和能源成本，而这些能耗在当前能源意识语境下常被忽视。因此需要系统地刻画防御技术的能耗并揭示相关权衡挑战。

Method: 本文对一种防御技术的能耗消耗进行特征化表征，并分析ML准确性、鲁棒性与能效三者构成的权衡问题（triad）所带来的挑战。文章采用分析与框架化方法，而非提出新的防御算法。

Result: 刻画了特定防御技术在O-RAN/蜂窝网络场景下的能耗特征，揭示了防御机制在保障模型鲁棒性的同时产生的计算开销所形成的能效代价，并明确了准确性、鲁棒性、能效三重目标之间的冲突关系。

Conclusion: 在能源意识日益重要的背景下，防御策略的设计不能仅关注安全有效性，还需将能耗成本纳入考量；未来需要在ML准确性、鲁棒性和能效之间寻求平衡，这是一个亟待解决的重要研究挑战。

Abstract: The integration of Artificial Intelligence (AI), generally as Machine Learning (ML) algorithms, in all levels and aspects of cellular networks demonstrates the success of data-driven algorithms; for example, the Radio Intelligence Controller (RIC) of the O-RAN paradigm bestows the network with optimised radio resource allocation, load balancing or energy efficiency functions, among others. Nevertheless, this dependency on data opens new security vulnerabilities, as attackers can alter data properties and steer ML models to underperform or degrade. Conversely, the developed mitigation strategies are effective, but they generate a computational load which, in consequence, results in an energy cost generally overlooked, even in the current energy-awareness context. In this work, consumption of a defence technique is characterised, and the challenges raised by the triad of ML accuracy, robustness and energy efficiency are outlined.

</details>


### [293] [Non-Degenerate Risk Certification for Automated Security Decisions: A Decision-Contract Theory with ATT\&CK-Aligned Triage as a Worked Instance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12444)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个结构性缺陷：许多声称保证无条件风险上界的自动化决策系统可以通过“永不行动”的选择器将风险界归零，从而空洞满足。作者提出“决策契约”（decision contract）理论，包含误差守恒定律、无标签容量证明及非退化可行动性证书，并在基于LLM的入侵检测告警分流场景中完成实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化决策的风险证书存在空洞性（vacuous）失效问题——一个从不行动的决策器即可使得风险上界趋零，导致理论保证失去实际意义。作者指出这种认证方法上的真空源于缺乏对“决策契约”的严格定义，即系统作用的输入以及对“正确输出”的语义关系。该问题在LLM入侵检测告警分流场景中被具体暴露，需要系统性理论修复。

Method: 作者建立决策契约理论：(1) 误差守恒定律，证明误差只在有害自动化、人工延迟和语义遮蔽之间重新分配；(2) 无标签“单一容量证书”，识别结构性无能（structural incapacity），并提供风险可行修正来区分可恢复的阈值错位与风险约束下的无能；(3) 非退化可行动性证书，通过构造排除“全部弃权”的平凡解。实证部分使用3个IDS数据集、6种LLM和4个错误率阈值，模拟ATT&CK对齐的告警分流流程。

Result: 在3个IDS数据集、6个LLM、4个错误率阈值共90.3%的配置中，经验性误归因风险保持在目标值或以下；83.4%的平均正确自动化率。容量诊断解释了所有低效用配置；其精化版本可区分真实错位与风险约束性无能，且备选阈值证实了此区分；训练稳定性重测未发现任何确认的结构性无能实例；真实细粒度攻击子类型标签确认了在真实的“多对一”映射下粗化迁移恒等式成立，并存在小而非零的遮蔽质量。

Conclusion: 风险证书若无决策契约约束则必然是空洞的；通过将风险声明绑定到特定决策契约，可以构造出排除平凡弃权解的非退化证书。所提出的框架不仅能识别结构性无能，还能将阈值错位与风险约束性无能力量分离，为自动化决策的可靠认证提供了理论基础，并在LLM入侵检测中展示了实际可部署的认证流程。

Abstract: An unconditional risk bound on automated decisions can be satisfied without automating anything, since a selector that never acts drives the bound to zero. We show this is structural: any risk certificate is defined over a decision contract, the inputs a system acts on plus the semantic relation under which an output counts correct, and weakening either hides base-classifier error. We develop a decision-contract theory: an error-conservation law showing error is only reassigned among harmful automation, human deferral, and semantic masking; a label-free singleton capacity certifying structural incapacity, with a risk-feasible refinement separating recoverable threshold misalignment from risk-constrained incapacity; and a non-degenerate actionability certificate excluding all-abstain solutions by construction. We instantiate this on ATT\&CK-aligned alert triage for LLM-based intrusion detection, the setting that exposed the vacuity failure. Across 3 IDS datasets, 6 LLMs, and 4 error-rate thresholds, empirical false-attribution risk stays at or below target in 90.3% of configurations, with 83.4% mean correct automation. The capacity diagnostic explains every low-utility configuration; its refinement separates genuine misalignment from risk-constrained incapacity, confirmed by an exhibited alternative threshold; a training-stability re-run finds no confirmed structural-incapacity instance; and real fine-grained attack-subtype labels confirm the coarsening-transfer identity under a genuine many-to-one map, with small but non-zero masking mass.

</details>


### [294] [SoK: From Generation to Consumption of Privacy Documents in Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12511)
*Shidong Pan,Clark LaChance,Zhen Tao,Sepideh Ghanavati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文献是一篇系统化知识综述（SoK），从软件工程视角对2010-2025年间290篇隐私文档相关论文进行了生命周期统一审视，围绕定义与范围、生成、分析与提取、一致性与合规性检查、可用性评估五个研究问题展开，识别出15项研究趋势和21个开放机遇，并规划了四大研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私文档（如隐私政策）是数字服务披露数据实践和获取用户同意的核心机制，但随研究领域扩张，从创建、分析、评估到维护的完整生命周期缺乏统一连贯的视角，难以清晰把握研究全貌。

Method: 采用SoK（Systematization of Knowledge）方法，系统性地回顾和分析了2010至2025年间发表的290篇论文，围绕五个研究问题（定义与范围、生成、分析与提取、不一致与不合规检查、可用性评估与改进）进行组织与归纳，并在此基础上综合出研究趋势、开放机遇和未来方向。

Result: 识别出15项关键研究趋势和21项开放机遇，并构建了四大未来研究方向：(i) AI-centric平台的新兴挑战，(ii) 多样化且及时更新的数据基础需求，(iii) 基于LLM的统一策略-代码分析，(iv) 面向终端用户与开发者的双重可用性。

Conclusion: 该SoK为隐私政策和隐私文档领域的未来研究提供了共享基础，通过生命周期视角统一了分散的研究成果，并对前沿挑战（尤其是AI平台的隐私透明性）指明了研究方向。

Abstract: Privacy documents (e.g., privacy policies) are a central mechanism through which digital services disclose data practices and seek user consent. Over the past decades, research on privacy documents has expanded significantly, encompassing not only traditional privacy policies but also short notices (e.g., privacy labels) and interface-level transparency mechanisms. As this research area continues to grow, it has become increasingly difficult to obtain a coherent view of how privacy documents are created, analyzed, evaluated, and maintained across their lifecycle. This SoK provides a unified, lifecycle-oriented view of privacy documents from a software engineering perspective. We systematically review and analyze 290 papers published between 2010 and 2025, organizing them around five research questions that examine how privacy documents are (1) defined and scoped, (2) generated, (3) analyzed and extracted, (4) checked for inconsistencies and noncompliance, and (5) evaluated and improved for usability. Building on our findings, we identify 15 key research trends and 21 open opportunities. We further chart four broader research directions that highlight (i) emerging challenges in AI-centric platforms, (ii) the need for diverse and up-to-date data foundations, (iii) LLM-based unified policy-code analysis, and (iv) dual usability for end-users and developers. We hope this SoK provides a shared foundation for future research on privacy policies and privacy documents.

</details>


### [295] [Tracing Provenance and Detecting Tampering with Complementary LLM Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12713)
*Xiaoyan Feng,Yanjun Zhang,He Zhang,Leo Yu Zhang,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合水印方法，在LLM生成文本中同时嵌入鲁棒信号（用于溯源）和脆弱信号（用于篡改检测），通过独立密钥和不同播种窗口实现两种信号共存，支持Intact、Tampered和No-Watermark三种检测决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印虽能在编辑后保持溯源，但这种鲁棒性也允许攻击者修改关键内容仍保有归属，即piggyback spoofing漏洞。需要一种既能溯源又能提供篡改证据的水印方案。

Method: 在每次生成的token中共同嵌入鲁棒和脆弱两种信号。两种信号共享相同机制但使用独立密钥和不同的文本归一化播种窗口——鲁棒信号对编辑有弹性，脆弱信号对读者可见的更改敏感。采用多轮无偏锦标赛重加权保持期望生成分布，周期性轮次分配模式控制两种信号间的权衡。检测时两种信号得分构成二维空间，支持Intact/Tampered/No-Watermark三种决策。

Result: 在两个大语言模型和两个提示数据集上，该方法在篡改检测率上优于所有对比方法，同时保持有竞争力的溯源鲁棒性和困惑度。消融研究表明可靠的三态检测需要明确的完整性定义、两种信号的共同嵌入以及对编辑具有互补的敏感性。

Conclusion: 通过鲁棒与脆弱信号的联合嵌入，实现了溯源与篡改证据的双重目标，为LLM生成文本的完整性和来源追踪提供了更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: Watermarking LLM-generated text is an important task for tracing its provenance. Existing LLM watermarks preserve provenance under editing, but this same robustness allows an adversary to alter critical content while retaining attribution, a vulnerability known as piggyback spoofing. We introduce an innovative watermark that jointly provides provenance and tamper evidence. It co-embeds a robust signal and a fragile signal into each generated token. The signals share the same mechanism but use independent keys and different seeding windows over normalized text, making one resilient to edits and the other sensitive to reader-visible changes. Multiple rounds of unbiased tournament reweighting preserve the expected generation distribution, while a periodic round-allocation pattern controls the trade-off between the two signals. At detection, their scores form a two-dimensional space supporting three decisions: Intact, Tampered, and No-Watermark. Across two large language models and two prompt datasets, our method demonstrates the highest tamper-detection rate among the evaluated methods while maintaining competitive attribution robustness and perplexity. Ablation studies show that reliable three-state detection requires a well-defined notion of intactness, co-embedding of the two signals, and complementary sensitivity to edits.

</details>


### [296] [PIPES: Securing Agent Perception with Provenance and Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12789)
*Sanjay Kariyappa,Severin Klingler,G. Edward Suh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了PIPES（基于溯源信息与先验强制的筛选）防御机制，用于防止工具使用代理遭受状态腐败攻击（state-corruption attacks）。该攻击利用工具响应中缺乏来源标注的漏洞，让攻击者控制的内容超越其信息权威，篡改代理对环境的认知。PIPES利用语义先验和来源溯源信息对响应单元进行筛查，在三个VitaBench和三个AgentDyn数据集上，将平均攻击成功率从84.7%降至2.3%，同时保持良性实用率（92.5% vs 90.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用代理从不同信任级别的来源消费外部数据，但工具响应很少标明每个组件的来源或意图。这种信息缺口使攻击者能够通过状态腐败攻击操纵代理的环境认知，使攻击行为看起来合理并绕过现有的防护机制。现有防御未能解决这一核心漏洞。

Method: PIPES方法包含两个核心机制：1) 当模式（schema）提供稳定预期时，使用静态字段契约进行筛查；2) 对于开放式内容，基于响应前的轨迹和可信溯源元数据来条件化筛查。PIPES会标记违反语义先验或溯源层次结构的单元，部署时可以选择移除、警告、阻止或升级处理检测到的违规。实验中对自适应PAIR风格攻击采用了原子移除的实例化方案。

Result: 以Gemma 4 31B IT作为目标代理，在三个VitaBench和三个AgentDyn数据集上，PIPES将平均攻击成功率从84.7%降至2.3%。同时，良性实用率得到保持甚至提升：使用PIPES时为92.5%，无防御时为90.6%。

Conclusion: PIPES通过将溯源信息与语义先验相结合，能有效抵御针对工具使用代理的状态腐败攻击，同时不损伤甚至略微提升良性性能。该方法为代理安全提供了一种可部署、可扩展的防御方案，研究结果表明溯源感知的筛查是保障代理环境认知完整性的关键方向。

Abstract: Tool-using agents consume external data from sources with different levels of trust, yet tool responses rarely identify who produced each component or what it should convey. We show that this gap enables state-corruption attacks, in which attacker-controlled content makes environmental claims beyond the informational authority of its response component and corrupts the agent's perceived environment, making the resulting action appear justified to existing guardrails. We introduce PIPES (Provenance-Informed, Prior-Enforced Screening), which screens response units using semantic priors and source provenance. PIPES uses static field contracts when schemas provide stable expectations, and conditions screening of open-ended content on the pre-response trajectory and trusted provenance metadata. It marks units that violate their semantic prior or the provenance hierarchy; deployments may remove, warn, block, or escalate detected violations. We instantiate atomic removal and evaluate PIPES against adaptive PAIR-style attacks. Across the three VitaBench and three AgentDyn splits with Gemma 4 31B IT as the target agent, PIPES reduces average attack success from 84.7% to 2.3%, while preserving average benign utility (92.5% with PIPES versus 90.6% without defense).

</details>


### [297] [RealmEye: Virtual Machine Introspection for Arm CCA Realm VMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12822)
*Ruofei Qu,Wei Feng,Hongzhan Ma,Menghan Jia,Muyan Shen,Yu Qin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RealmEye是首个针对Arm CCA Realm VM的虚拟机自省（VMI）系统，将全部自省逻辑放置在Realm Management Monitor（RMM）的R-EL2特权层，实现硬件强制的隔离，无需在Realm内部署代理，可检测进程隐藏和系统调用表钩子等rootkit攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密虚拟机（CVM）的硬件隔离在保护租户免受恶意云商攻击的同时，也使得租户无法感知虚拟机内部运行的内容——内核rootkit可通过网络或供应链攻击植入，利用同一层隔离隐藏进程、篡改内核数据、窃取模型权重。然而经典VMI技术预设了可信Hypervisor，CVM又将Hypervisor排除在TCB之外。现有最佳方案00SEVen依赖AMD SEV-SNP的VMPL0机制，但Arm CCA没有对应的机制，导致Realm VM缺乏自省方案。

Method: RealmEye将完整的内省逻辑放入Realm Management Monitor（RMM）的R-EL2层级，实现监控器与被监控VM之间的硬件强制隔离：不做VM内代理植入、不修改Realm本身。它通过读取Realm内存和寄存器、挂起VM以获得一致快照、捕获页面级访问来实现自省，完全不依赖VM内接口。采用周期性自驱动触发模式，使扫描时机仅存在于RMM内部，防止Hypervisor与Realm内rootkit串通。结果通过硬件认证通道返回给远程所有者，并提供CCA驱动后端使LibVMI和DRAKVUF等现有工具可以无缝对接。

Result: 在Arm FVP（Fast Model）平台上，RealmEye成功检测到Diamorphine rootkit的进程隐藏和系统调用表钩子行为。其RMM内自省开销可以根据原始调用次数进行线性预测，具备良好的性能可预估性。

Conclusion: RealmEye填补了Arm CCA Realm VM缺乏VMI解决方案的空白，通过将自省逻辑置于RMM内部实现了硬件强制隔离的自省能力，同时保持与现有VMI工具的兼容性，为Arm平台上的机密计算环境提供了可行的安全监控方案。

Abstract: Confidential VMs (CVMs) have become the dominant substrate for sensitive cloud workloads, from financial services to privacy-preserving AI inference. The hardware isolation that protects these CVMs from a malicious cloud also blinds their owners to what runs inside them: kernel rootkits planted via network or supply-chain attacks can hide processes, tamper with kernel data, and exfiltrate model weights under the cover of the same isolation that defends the VM. Tenants therefore need to inspect a running CVM from outside, yet classical VM introspection (VMI) presupposes a trusted Hypervisor, which CVMs exclude from the TCB. The state-of-the-art CVM-VMI system, 00SEVen, restores introspection on AMD SEV-SNP via an in-VM agent at a privileged tier (VMPL0), a mechanism that does not exist on Arm CCA, leaving Realm VMs without any introspection solution.
  We present RealmEye, the first VMI system for Arm CCA Realm VMs. RealmEye places the entire introspection logic inside the Realm Management Monitor (RMM) at R-EL2, achieving hardware-enforced separation between the monitor and the monitored VM: no agent runs inside the Realm, and the Realm remains unmodified. RealmEye reads Realm memory and registers, suspends the VM for consistent snapshots, and traps page-level accesses, without relying on any in-VM interface. A periodic, self-driven trigger mode keeps scan timing internal to the RMM, preventing the Hypervisor from colluding with in-Realm rootkits. Results are returned to the remote owner over a hardware-attested channel, and a CCA driver backend lets existing tools such as LibVMI and DRAKVUF interoperate with RealmEye unchanged. On the Arm FVP, RealmEye detects process hiding and syscall-table hooking by Diamorphine, and its in-RMM cost is linearly predictable from primitive invocation counts.

</details>


### [298] [Beyond Source: An Empirical Study of Python Bytecode Security Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12853)
*Baihong Chen,Tian Xie,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Python字节码（.pyc文件）作为安全工件进行了实证研究，在1,034,843个PyPI工件中发现7,388个含字节码的工件（含228,578个.pyc文件），评估了工具分析能力、CPython运行时鲁棒性及源码级复现性，发现字节码可导致解编译器崩溃、内存破坏，且行为无法从源码级复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python包安全审查主要基于源码，但Python运行时可以直接执行字节码（.pyc文件、编译-only模块、marshal代码对象），形成"检查-执行"鸿沟。攻击者可能通过提交恶意字节码绕过基于源码的安全审查，因此需要系统性地研究字节码作为安全工件的特性。

Method: 本研究通过四个维度进行实证分析：(1) 对1,034,843个PyPI工件进行字节码暴露度测量，识别出7,388个包含字节码的工件； (2) 使用版本感知工具链（CPython 3.8-3.14）评估204,904个在范围文件的解编译可行性； (3) 通过收集真实PyPI字节码和模糊测试生成的对抗性变异字节码，评估CPython运行时和反编译工具的鲁棒性； (4) 测试字节码发现是否能从普通Python源码复现。

Result: 在204,904个现代CPython字节码文件中，至少一个解编译能对204,901个输出源码（但仅测量发射而非功能等价）；工具非鲁棒性显著：PyPI实际字节码触发托管代码异常和超时，对抗性变异字节码引发解编译器原生进程崩溃，产生17种鲁棒性签名；模糊测试生成1,009个栈去重运行时发现，以指针解引用症状为主，其中261组有潜在内存破坏特征，至少91.7%的组越过文档标注的非安全摄取边界执行；所有字节码发现均无法从普通Python源码复现。

Conclusion: Python字节码是生态系统中可见的、实际可分析的、具有安全意义的解释器输入，其行为不必然与源码级行为一致。这揭示了当前基于源码的安全审查模式的盲区，需要将字节码纳入Python包安全审计的范围。

Abstract: Python package security is largely source-centric, yet Python runtimes can execute bytecode directly through .pyc files, compiled-only modules, and marshalled code objects, creating an inspection-execution gap. We present an empirical study of Python bytecode as a security artifact. We measure bytecode exposure in PyPI distributions, evaluate practical analyzability using version-aware tooling, assess CPython runtime robustness under adversarial bytecode, and test source-level reproduction of bytecode findings. Across 1,034,843 collected PyPI artifacts, we identify 7,388 bytecode-containing artifacts, including 228,578 .pyc files and 28,193 artifact-local source-less .pyc files. For modern CPython 3.8-3.14 bytecode, at least one selected decompiler emits source for 204,901 of 204,904 in-scope files, a result measuring emission rather than verified functional equivalence. Tools are non-robust: observed PyPI bytecode triggers managed-code exceptions and timeouts, while adversarial mutated bytecode also drives decompilers into native process failures; together these outcomes yield 17 distinct robustness signatures. Fuzzing produces 1,009 stack-deduplicated runtime findings dominated by pointer-dereference symptoms; 261 groups exhibit potential memory-corruption characteristics, and at least 91.7% of groups reach execution beyond the documented-unsafe ingestion boundary. None reproduce from ordinary Python source. Bytecode is thus a visible ecosystem artifact, a practical analysis target, and a security-relevant interpreter input whose behavior need not match source-level behavior.

</details>


### [299] [Discovering Persistent Behavioural Patterns for Interpretable Blockchain Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12864)
*Dorottya Zelenyanszki,Zhe Hou,Kamanashis Biswas,Vallipuram Muthukkumarasamy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展且与应用无关的框架，用于从大规模区块链活动中发现持久行为模式。该框架构建包含合约、代币和市场上下文的行为语句，通过两级嵌入（句子级和序列级）捕获个体行为，并使用可解释的行为分析器刻画社区特征。在以太坊3000多万笔交易上的评估表明，该框架能发现常规与恶意行为模式，且许多模式在独立观察窗口间保持稳定，支持长期行为识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DeFi分析方法大多针对特定应用场景，难以扩展且结果难以解释，缺乏一个既能大规模处理区块链数据、又能提供可解释性和持久性分析能力的通用框架。

Method: 提出一个与应用无关的可扩展框架，具体包括：（1）构建融合合约、代币和市场上下文的行为语句；（2）两步嵌入过程——句子级嵌入捕捉个体行为，序列级嵌入捕捉用户随时间的行为演变；（3）可解释的行为分析器，通过行为主题、日常惯例、时间动态、实体暴露度和可疑性证据来描述社区特征。

Result: 在以太坊超过3000万笔交易上进行评估，框架成功发现常规行为模式（如DEX交易、NFT活动）和恶意行为模式（如钓鱼、机器人操作、预言机操纵、Rug Pull），且大量模式在独立观察窗口间保持稳定，支持长期行为识别。

Conclusion: 该框架同时具备可扩展性、可解释性和持久性分析能力，可用于区块链取证调查、行为归因和威胁发现。

Abstract: Public blockchain data enables large-scale DeFi-related analysis, but many existing approaches are application-specific, difficult to scale, or hard to interpret. This research proposes a scalable, application-agnostic framework for \emph{persistent behavioural pattern discovery} from large-scale blockchain activity. It constructs behaviour sentences enriched with contract, token and market context, then applies a two-step embedding process: sentence-level embeddings capture individual actions, while sequence-level embeddings capture user behaviour over time. An interpretable behavioural profiler characterizes discovered communities through behavioural motifs, routines, temporal dynamics, entity exposure, and suspiciousness evidence. Evaluation on Ethereum using over 30 million transactions shows that the framework uncovers both routine and malicious behavioural patterns, including decentralised exchange (DEX) trading, NFT activity, phishing, bot operations, oracle manipulation, and rug-pull schemes. Importantly, many patterns remain stable across independent observation windows, enabling the identification of long-term behaviours beyond a single analysis period. The proposed framework combines scalability, interpretability, and persistence analysis, supporting blockchain forensic investigation, behavioural attribution, and threat discovery.

</details>


### [300] [Labels Are Not Endpoints: Treatment Leakage and Construct Validity in MCP Agent Security Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12880)
*Rana Muhammad Ahmed,Sabahat Abbas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对一次工具使用智能体的安全评估活动进行审计，发现历史评分器存在直接的处理泄漏（treatment leakage），即治疗元数据直接决定ATTACK_SUCCESS类别。通过治疗盲重建，纠正了58个历史标签，并将30多个锁定v2数据集中的攻击成功率修正为零，同时保留三个已验证的受保护数据传输案例和一个独立的未经授权转发案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估中经常将存储的标签等同于行为事实，但这种方法可能掩盖测量缺陷。作者希望检验历史评估标签的可靠性，揭示评分过程中是否存在系统性偏差，从而改进安全评估的测量严谨性。

Method: 对一次保留的评估活动进行完整追踪：追溯10,200条执行记录至180条模型级请求、45条语义请求和15个可观察刺激。进行了双schema处理，但未按计划提供外部载荷家族语料库。采用治疗盲重建方法纠正历史标签，并进行了双评审者盲法一致性审查，最终提出七环节完整性链（Integrity Chain）和可执行的、范围受限的端点完整性lint工具。

Result: 历史评分器存在直接治疗泄漏：治疗元数据门控ATTACK_SUCCESS类别，固定行为在治疗重标下可改变类别。治疗盲重建纠正了58条历史ATTACK_SUCCESS或HIJACK_ATTEMPT标签为已授权的良性完成，同时保留三个已验证的受保护数据传输和一个个别的未经授权转发案例。锁定v2普查中ATTACK_SUCCESS记录为零，转发案例仍为HIJACK_ATTEMPT（处于目标完成的语义边界）。双评审者盲法一致性审查在96条可解释请求上产生相同共识类别，但在四个构造边界案例上与锁定代码手册存在差异。

Conclusion: 该工作的贡献是一个限定在单一评估活动范围内的测量审计（campaign-bounded measurement audit），而非总体攻击率、模型排名、防御效能或因果效应的估计。研究强调安全评估应将存储标签与行为事实加以区分，并通过完整性链和lint工具提升评估透明度。

Abstract: Security evaluations of tool-using agents often equate stored labels with behavioral facts. We audit a preserved campaign by tracing 10,200 execution rows to 180 model-bound requests, 45 semantic requests, and 15 observable stimuli. Two schema treatments were delivered, but the planned external payload-family corpus was not. The historical grader exhibited direct treatment leakage: treatment metadata gated the ATTACK_SUCCESS class, so fixed behavior could change class under treatment relabeling. A treatment-blind reconstruction corrects 58 historical ATTACK_SUCCESS or HIJACK_ATTEMPT labels to authorized benign completions while preserving three verified protected-data transfers and one separate unauthorized-forwarding case. The locked v2 census contains exactly zero ATTACK_SUCCESS records, while the forwarding case remains a HIJACK_ATTEMPT at a semantic boundary concerning objective completion. A dual-reviewer blinded concordance review of all 96 requests deemed structurally interpretable by locked v2 produced identical reviewer-consensus classes but differed from the locked codebook on four construct-boundary cases. We contribute a seven-link Integrity Chain and an executable, scope-bounded endpoint-integrity linter. The result is a campaign-bounded measurement audit, not a population attack-rate, model-ranking, defense-efficacy, or causal estimate.

</details>


### [301] [Adversarial Robustness in Smishing Detection: A Comparative Analysis of Adversarial Fragility in Classical vs. Transformer-Based Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12889)
*Denzel Chiuseni,Athanase Bahizire,Silva Hama,Jema David Ndibwile*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了五种模型架构（三种经典词法模型和两种多语言Transformer）在短信钓鱼检测中的对抗鲁棒性，发现经典模型在字符混淆和结构扰动下几乎灾难性失败（RDR高达0.988），而Transformer模型表现出显著更强的韧性（RDR最高0.351）。研究证明干净文本性能不能预测对抗鲁棒性，强调了架构特定防御的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短信钓鱼检测系统通常在干净、单语文本上训练和评估，但在低资源环境下，攻击者常通过字符混淆、跨语言代码切换和结构扰动来规避检测系统。现有研究缺乏对这些对抗攻击下不同模型架构鲁棒性的系统比较，特别是经典词法模型与多语言Transformer之间的性能差异尚不明确。

Method: 使用27,037条消息的数据集，评估五种模型架构：三种经典词法模型（Random Forest、XGBoost、CNN+BiLSTM）和两种多语言Transformer（mBERT、XLM-RoBERTa）。经典模型受到黑盒通用攻击，Transformer模型则使用注意力引导目标攻击。每种模型在三种攻击类型和强度级别下进行测试，使用鲁棒性退化比（RDR）衡量性能，并通过Cliff's d效应量、Mann-Whitney U检验和Friedman检验进行统计验证。

Result: 经典模型在字符混淆和结构扰动下经历近灾难性失败（RDR最高0.988），而Transformer表现出显著更强的韧性（RDR最高0.351），结构扰动是Transformer最明显的弱点。效应量分析显示两类模型间存在显著差异。在Transformer组内，XLM-RoBERTa尽管干净文本基线更高，但退化程度大于mBERT。统计检验确认这些模式归因于模型架构而非采样。

Conclusion: 干净文本性能不能可靠预测对抗鲁棒性；短信钓鱼检测应被视为对抗性网络安全挑战而非静态分类任务，需要架构特定的防御策略。

Abstract: Smishing detection systems are commonly trained and evaluated on clean, monolingual text. In low-resource settings, however, attackers frequently circumvent these systems through character obfuscation, cross-lingual code-switching, and structural perturbation. This study evaluates adversarial robustness for five model architectures: three classical lexical models (Random Forest, XGBoost, CNN+BiLSTM) and two multilingual transformers (mBERT, XLM-RoBERTa), using a dataset of 27,037 messages. Classical models are subjected to black-box generic attacks, while transformers are evaluated with attention-guided targeting. Each model is tested across three attack types and intensity levels, with performance measured by the Robustness Degradation Ratio (RDR). The results reveal a distinct architectural boundary: classical models experience near-catastrophic failure under character obfuscation and structural perturbation (RDR up to 0.988), whereas transformers demonstrate significantly greater resilience (RDR up to 0.351), with structural perturbation representing their most pronounced vulnerability. Effect-size analysis (Cliff's d) indicates a substantial difference between the two model categories. Within the transformer group, XLM-RoBERTa, despite achieving a higher clean-text baseline, exhibits greater degradation than mBERT. These findings demonstrate that clean-text performance is not a reliable predictor of adversarial robustness. Statistical validation using Mann-Whitney U and Friedman tests confirms that these patterns are attributable to model architecture rather than sampling. The results underscore the necessity for architecture-specific defences and frame smishing detection as an adversarial cybersecurity challenge rather than a static classification task.

</details>


### [302] [Beyond Handcrafted Security: Towards Self-Evolving Defense for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12977)
*Jiajun Ruan,Peiyang Li,Yukun Chen,Fengting Li,Chao Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HARD框架，将LLM智能体的运行时防御从手工工程转变为自主进化过程，通过自动识别干预策略并基于失败轨迹迭代改进防御工件，在提升安全性能的同时保持良性任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的扩展能力引入了复杂的安全威胁，现有的运行时防御方法过度依赖手工设计的干预措施，缺乏构建和维护这些防御机制的原则性框架。

Method: 首先从harness（执行框架）层面形式化定义运行时防御，系统刻画harness机制如何支持防御构建，统一现有防御干预的视角；在此基础上提出HARD框架，通过自动识别适当干预策略并基于观察到的失败轨迹迭代改进防御工件，实现防御的自主进化。
