Result: 大量实验表明HARD在安全性能上优于现有手工设计的防御方法，同时保持了良性任务的效用。

Conclusion: 自主防御进化是保护已部署LLM智能体的一个有前景的新范式，使智能体能够识别防御弱点并持续改进其保护机制。

Abstract: The expanding operational capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents introduce sophisticated security threats. Runtime defenses have emerged as an effective approach to mitigating these risks by integrating security mechanisms into the agent execution loop. However, existing runtime defenses rely heavily on manually designed interventions and lack a principled framework for their construction and maintenance. In this work, we first develop a harness-level formulation of runtime defense that systematically characterizes how harness mechanisms enable defense construction and provides a unified view of existing runtime defense interventions from a harness perspective. Building on this formulation, we propose HARD (Harness-based Autonomous Runtime Defense Evolution), a self-evolving runtime defense framework that automatically identifies appropriate intervention strategies and iteratively improves defense artifacts based on observed failure traces. HARD transforms runtime defense development from manual engineering into an autonomous evolution process, and extensive experiments demonstrate that it improves security performance over existing handcrafted defenses while preserving benign task utility. Our findings highlight autonomous defense evolution as a promising new paradigm for securing deployed LLM agents, enabling agents to identify defense weaknesses and continuously improve their protection mechanisms.

</details>


### [303] [ATOBench: Tracing How Autonomous Penetration-Testing Agents Verify Vulnerabilities When Target Evidence Lies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12996)
*Qiyang Chen,Yixi Li,Fengwei Zhang,Junlin Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出ATOBench，一个通过运行时注入欺骗性目标响应并配对原生剧本来评估自主渗透测试代理验证过程的框架，揭示代理如何处理冲突证据、改变行动、决定停止以及将观察转化为漏洞声明。对5个模型路线450个剧集的分析表明，增加的活动量可能掩盖断裂的验证链，而成功恢复依赖于找到可用证据并贯穿报告保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主渗透测试代理依赖目标响应来指导后续动作和最终报告，但欺骗性响应可以同时偏转攻击轨迹和验证过程。然而，最终报告无法揭示代理如何解释冲突证据、改变路线、决定停止或将观察转化为漏洞声明，因此需要一个使验证过程可观察的评估框架。

Method: ATOBench在运行时注入注册的响应变换，并将每个变换后的剧集与同一环境下的原生剧集配对。每一对在第一个受影响响应处对齐，通过源链接重建跟踪后续动作、证据恢复、停止和报告支持。定义了三个冻结观察契约：利用证明、资源所有权和可重用工件。在450个剧集上评估5个模型路线。

Result: 分析表明，增加的活动量可以掩盖断裂的验证链；成功的恢复取决于找到可用证据并将其保留到报告阶段。ATOBench将欺骗性目标观察转化为可复现的证据处理探针。

Conclusion: ATOBench提供了超越最终结果的过程级视图，揭示了不可信观察如何塑造行动、验证和报道，扩展了进攻性渗透测试代理评估的范畴。

Abstract: Autonomous penetration-testing agents rely on target responses. These responses guide both subsequent actions and the final report. A deceptive response can therefore redirect both the attack trajectory and the agent's verification process. However, final reports reveal little about how an agent interprets conflicting evidence, changes course, decides to stop, or turns observations into a vulnerability claim. We introduce ATOBench, an evaluation framework that makes this verification process observable. ATOBench injects registered response transformations at runtime and pairs each transformed episode with a native episode under the same environment. Each pair is aligned at the first affected response. A source-linked reconstruction then follows later actions, evidence recovery, stopping, and report support. Three frozen observation contracts cover exploit proof, resource ownership, and reusable artifacts. We evaluate five model routes over 450 episodes. The analysis shows that increased activity can mask a broken verification chain, while successful recovery depends on finding usable evidence and preserving it through reporting. ATOBench turns deceptive target observations into a reproducible probe of evidence handling in autonomous penetration testing. This process-level view extends offensive pentest agent evaluation beyond final outcomes by revealing how untrusted observations shape actions, verification, and reporting.

</details>


### [304] [OmniSphinx: Active Mix Networks (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13008)
*Daniel Schadt,Christoph Coijanovic,Shabi Shabani,Thorsten Strufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniSphinx，一种可嵌入代码的混合网络数据包格式，能够在单一部署中仿真其他任意混合网络格式，解决现有混合网络格式不兼容的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合网络包格式各自为政、互不兼容，需要独立的软件和基础设施来部署不同格式的混合网络，缺乏统一的通用方案。

Method: OmniSphinx在数据包中嵌入代码，由发送者决定数据包的处理方式，从而构建一个主动混合网络，能够在单一部署中仿真仿真任何其他混合网络格式。

Result: 实证评估表明，相比原生执行，OmniSphinx对典型混合网络使用场景的仿真开销合理：对于最紧凑的Sphinx格式，计算时间增加约90微秒，数据包头大小增加33%。

Conclusion: OmniSphinx通过可编程的数据包格式实现了混合网络格式的统一，在单一部署中即可兼容并仿真其他格式，且开销在可接受范围内，为混合网络格式间的互操作性提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Mix networks are an important tool to implement anonymous communication, which protects not just the content but also the metadata of messages. Over time, various packet formats for mix networks have been proposed, usually with single, specific goals in mind. These formats are incompatible with each other, requiring separate software and infrastructure to be set up. In this paper, we propose a new format, OmniSphinx, which solves this issue. In OmniSphinx, senders embed code in their packets that determines how they must be processed. The resulting active mix network can emulate any other mix format within a single deployment. Our empirical evaluation shows that emulation in OmniSphinx incurs reasonable overhead compared to native execution for typical mix network use cases: For Sphinx, the most compact format, computation time increases by around 90μs, while headers increase by 33% in size.

</details>


### [305] [InterSAGE: The Secure and Verifiable Interoperability Protocol for An Internet of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13030)
*Zhenhua Zou,Sheng Guo,Qiuyang Zhan,Lepeng Zhao,Shuo Li,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了InterSAGE，一个信任原生的协议套件，为LLM驱动的智能体互操作提供四层安全基座（持久身份、能力感知发现、信任协商、问责），与MCP/A2A/ANP/AG-UI等通信协议互补共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体协议（如MCP、A2A等）仅定义消息交换方式，但缺乏对智能体身份证明、授权、能力声明及委派后问责等安全语义的支持，导致跨组织边界的智能体交互存在信任缺失。

Method: InterSAGE包含四个核心原语：(1) 绑定开发者、代码包、运营者与部署上下文的智能体身份卡；(2) 基于DID绑定的可验证凭证清单实现能力感知发现；(3) 结合单调能力衰减与两层访问控制的信任协商；(4) 内核级密码审计轨迹，无需共识账本即将使用、委派与执行痕迹绑定至智能体身份。

Result: 与50余项涵盖智能体协议、去中心化身份、OAuth/OIDC扩展、零信任治理、委派与审计架构的相关工作进行对比，证明现有架构均未将持久身份、能力感知发现、信任协商与问责统一为四层信任基座。

Conclusion: InterSAGE作为独立于通信协议的安全基座，保证信任语义显式、可移植、可验证，同时允许通信协议独立演进，为智能体安全互操作提供了缺失的安全底层。

Abstract: The emerging Internet of Agents enables LLM-powered agents to discover peers, invoke tools, and delegate tasks across organizational boundaries. Existing protocols increasingly define how agents exchange messages, but not how an agent proves its identity, authorization, advertised capabilities, or accountability after delegation. We present InterSAGE, a trust-native protocol suite that supplies this missing security substrate alongside, rather than in place of, communication protocols. InterSAGE comprises four layers: Persistent Identity, Discovery, Trust Negotiation, and Accountability. Its four core primitives are: (1) Agent Identity Cards that bind developer, code package, operator, and deployment context; (2) capability-aware discovery using DID-bound Verifiable Credential manifests; (3) trust negotiation combining monotonic capability attenuation with two-tier access control; and (4) kernel-mediated cryptographic audit trails that bind usage, delegation, and execution traces to agent identity without a consensus ledger. InterSAGE is designed to complement MCP, A2A, ANP, and AG-UI, allowing communication protocols to evolve independently while keeping trust semantics explicit, portable, and verifiable. We compare InterSAGE with more than 50 efforts spanning agent protocols, decentralized identity, OAuth/OIDC extensions, zero-trust governance, delegation, and audit architectures. We show that no prior architecture jointly enforces persistent identity, capability-aware discovery, trust negotiation, and accountability as a unified four-layer trust substrate for secure agent interoperability.

</details>


### [306] [InSPECtor: Improving SLEIGH Processor Specification Veracity via Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13042)
*Michael Chesser,Paul Quirk,Douglas Cooke,Guy Farrelly,Surya Nepal,Damith C. Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了InSPECtor，一个基于代理自动化Oracle验证策略的测试框架，用于系统性地验证开源SLEIGH处理器规格（主要在Ghidra中使用）。通过枚举可解码指令形式、生成定向初始状态，并对比基于规格的模拟器与硬件参考，在x86-64、AArch64、ARM/Thumb、RISC-V和MSP430五种规格中发现超过38,920处差异，最终归结为125个独特bug，并提出8条改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理器规格支撑着反汇编器、反编译器和模拟器等关键安全与分析工具，但其正确性很少被检验。规格错误会扭曲程序行为、掩盖漏洞并促成分析规避技术，而验证处理器规格是一项非平凡任务。目前缺乏对SLEIGH语言规格的系统性验证方法。

Method: 设计并实现了一个基于代理Oracle验证策略的自动化测试框架。该方法利用规格本身编码的结构来枚举可解码指令形式并生成定向初始状态，然后通过差分测试，将基于规格运行的模拟器与硬件参考进行对比，验证指令的解码和模拟的正确性。

Result: 在五种不同的开源规格（x86-64、AArch64、ARM/Thumb、RISC-V、MSP430）上应用InSPECtor，发现超过38,920处差异，归纳为125个独特bug并给出了修复建议，识别了解码和语义缺陷以及跨厂商不一致性，并将发现归纳为8条具体建议。

Conclusion: 该工作凸显了处理器规格正确性的重要性，并提供了一个实用工具来大幅提升SLEIGH处理器规格的保真度，从而增强下游安全与分析工具的可靠性。

Abstract: Processor specifications underpin critical security and program- analysis tools such as disassemblers, decompilers, and emulators, yet, their correctness is rarely examined. Errors in specifications distort program behaviour, obscure vulnerabilities, and enable analysis-evasion techniques. Validating processor specifications is a non-trivial task. Our study is a significant undertaking to enable, for the first time, the systematic validation of open-source SLEIGH language specifications, predominantly used by Ghidra. We design and implement a testing framework based on an automated oracle validation strategy by proxy. Our approach leverages the structure encoded in a specification itself to enumerate decodable instruction forms and generate targeted initial states. Then differentially test the successful decoding and emulation of those instructions by comparing emulators exercising the processor specification against hardware references.
  Applying InSPECtor across diverse, open-source specifications---x86-64, AArch64, ARM/Thumb, RISC-V, MSP430---embedding differences in specification styles, author preferences, and instruction set architecture designs, we uncovered over 38,920 discrepancies that led to 125 unique bugs with proposed fixes, identifying decoding and semantic defects as well as cross-vendor inconsistencies. We distill our findings into 8 concrete recommendations to drive future improvements. Our work underscores the importance of specification correctness and provides a practical tool to substantially improve the fidelity of SLEIGH processor specifications, strengthening the reliability of downstream security and analysis tools.

</details>


### [307] [Operationalizing Cyber Threat Intelligence with GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13050)
*Atul Kabra,Prakhar Paliwal,Manjesh K. Hanawal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了将网络安全报告（CTI报告）输入知识图谱检索系统（Microsoft GraphRAG）与传统向量相似性检索系统（Naive RAG）相比，能否生成更依赖持久性、难以规避的检测规则（金字塔顶端的线索）。案例研究显示，在轮换所有IP、域名和文件哈希后，GraphRAG方案仍保持100%检测率，而Naive RAG仅有29%；九份真实CTI报告的大规模对比也验证了这一模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全研究员发布的网络攻击报告需要被检测工程师转化为可用的检测规则，但大多数自动化尝试只提取简单的线索（恶意IP、域名、文件哈希）并转为封禁列表。这些简单线索容易被攻击者快速更改，导致检测规则很快失效。安全团队用"痛苦金字塔"（Pyramid of Pain）描述这一概念——越靠近塔底的线索越容易改变，越靠近塔顶的线索（如战术、技术、程序）越持久。作者希望探究知识图谱感知的检索系统是否能生成更依赖金字塔顶端持久性线索的检测方案。

Method: 作者设计了一个对照实验：将同一份CTI报告分别输入GraphRAG（微软知识图谱检索系统）和Naive RAG（标准向量相似性检索系统），使用相同的报告、相同的生成指令和相同的语言模型生成最终检测方案，仅改变检索步骤。先对一份APT28报告进行详细的案例研究，轮换报告中的所有IP地址、域名和文件哈希后测试两种方案的有效性；随后在来自四个厂商的九份真实CTI报告上重复对比实验。

Result: 在APT28案例研究中，GraphRAG方案在轮换所有IP、域名和文件哈希后仍保持100%检测率，而Naive RAG方案仅维持29%。在九份真实CTI报告的大规模对比中，GraphRAG方案始终能达到金字塔更高、更难规避的层级，即使两种系统在总分上接近时也是如此。此外，研究还发现生成提示词的措辞对效果的影响几乎与检索后端同等重要。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将知识图谱感知的检索视为自动生成SOC可部署狩猎计划的架构上正确的坚实基础，同时表明生成提示词的措辞与检索后端本身同样关键。

Abstract: When a security researcher publishes a report on a cyberattack, detection engineers are supposed to turn it into working detection rules. In practice, most automated attempts at this only extract the simplest clues from the report --- bad IP addresses, domain names, and file hashes --- and turn them into block lists. This is a weak strategy, because attackers can change these simple clues within hours or days, so the resulting detections stop working almost as soon as they are deployed. Security teams describe this idea with the Pyramid of Pain. This project asks whether feeding a report into a knowledge-graph retrieval system, Microsoft GraphRAG, rather than a standard vector-similarity retrieval system (Naive RAG), produces detection plans that rely more on these durable, top-of-pyramid clues. Both systems are given the same report, the same generation instructions, and the same language model to write the final plan; only the retrieval step differs. In a detailed case study of one APT28 report, the GraphRAG plan kept firing at 100\% of its detections after every IP address, domain, and file hash in the report was rotated, while the Naive RAG plan kept firing at only 29\%. Repeating the comparison across nine real CTI reports from four vendors confirms the same pattern: GraphRAG plans consistently reach higher, harder-to-evade levels of the pyramid, even when the two systems end up close on total score. The results support treating knowledge-graph-aware retrieval as the architecturally correct foundation for automatically generating SOC-deployable hunting plans, while showing that the wording of the generation prompt matters almost as much as the retrieval back-end itself.

</details>


### [308] [A Commitment-Based Hybrid Post-Quantum Cryptographic Model for Multi-File Cloud Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13138)
*Lemdi Frank Prikutse,Regina Esi Turkson,Alimatu-Saadia Yussiff,Abdul-Lateef Yussiff,Maame G. Asante-Mensah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于承诺的混合后量子云存储认证模型，通过SHA3-256批量承诺将批量文件绑定到一个固定大小的摘要上进行一次性签名，将每批后量子签名调用从n次降为1次，在n=1000时签名阶段耗时降低约600-700倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云存储客户端需要能够抵御未来量子攻击的认证方案，因此需要结合经典密码原语与标准化后量子替代方案的混合构造。然而，朴素地扩展混合认证到多文件上传时，每个文件都需要一次后量子格签名，其开销主导认证时间，在实际批量场景下代价过高。

Method: 该模型包含三个核心组件：AES-256-GCM批量加密、混合X25519与ML-KEM-768密钥封装机制、以及混合Ed25519与ML-DSA-65双签名。关键创新在于对所有密文计算SHA3-256批量承诺，将整个批次的密文绑定到一个固定大小的摘要上，仅对该摘要执行一次后量子签名，从而将签名调用次数从n次降为1次（与批量大小无关），其余加密和哈希操作由对称密码的快速吞吐量支撑。

Result: 在普通客户端平台上，基于20次重复的均值，该模型在批量增长时保持签名阶段耗时近似恒定，而逐文件基线方案呈线性增长。在n=1000时，对于100KB、1MB和10MB文件，模型分别将签名阶段时间降低629倍、606倍和725倍，对比基线为共享所有其他原语的逐文件双签名方案。

Conclusion: 该承诺式混合后量子批量认证模型有效解决了多文件上传场景中后量子签名开销的瓶颈问题，通过批量承诺加单次签名策略，在保持混合后量子安全性的同时实现了与批量大小无关的签名开销，大幅提升了大规模批量上传的认证效率。

Abstract: Cloud storage clients increasingly require authentication that remains secure against future quantum-capable adversaries, motivating hybrid constructions that combine classical primitives with standardized post-quantum alternatives. Extended naively to multi-file upload, such constructions incur a per-file lattice signing cost that dominates authentication time and becomes prohibitive at realistic batch sizes. This paper presents a commitment-based hybrid post-quantum model that addresses this bottleneck. It comprises AES-256-GCM bulk encryption, a hybrid X25519 with ML-KEM-768 key encapsulation mechanism, and a hybrid Ed25519 with ML-DSA-65 dual signature, computed over a SHA3-256 batch commitment. The commitment binds all ciphertexts in a batch to a single fixed-size digest that is signed once, reducing the number of post-quantum signature invocations per batch from n to one, independent of batch size; the remaining encryption and hashing is bounded by fast symmetric throughput. On a commodity client platform, averaged over 20 repetitions, this holds signing-phase time near-constant as the batch grows while the per-file baseline scales linearly. At n = 1000, the model reduces signing-phase time by factors of 629, 606, and 725 for 100 KB, 1 MB, and 10 MB files respectively, against a per-file dual-signing baseline sharing every other primitive.

</details>


### [309] [Smart Contract Invariants Protect Against Cybercriminals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13191)
*Sofia Bobadilla,Humaira Afrin,Angela Novelli,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出程序不变量是解决区块链智能合约安全问题的有力方案，构建了INVARIANTEVAL基准（28个真实以太坊漏洞）和PONDEREPLAY重放框架（回放108,637笔交易）进行验证，结果表明智能合约不变量能够有效阻止所有真实攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链是计算领域对抗性最强的环境之一，数十亿美元因漏洞被网络犯罪分子窃取，这是一个尚未解决的开问题，目前没有任何概念或技术被证明能真正产生显著效果。作者主张经典的程序不变量概念可能是最有力的解决方案。

Method: 设计了一个原创的实验协议，包含两部分：1) 研究不变量如何保护免受过去真实世界攻击；2) 检验最先进的自动化工具能否找到这些不变量。实验工具链包括INVARIANTEVAL基准（28个真实以太坊漏洞，每个配有人工编写的不变量）和PONDEREPLAY重放框架（重新执行交易以证明智能合约不变量的正确性和可靠性）。

Result: 证明智能合约不变量能够阻止INVARIANTEVAL中所有网络犯罪攻击，通过回放108,637笔历史交易进行了完全验证。大规模实验清楚地表明智能合约不变量可以有效地保护免受网络犯罪分子的攻击。

Conclusion: 智能合约不变量被证明是对抗区块链安全攻击的有效防护手段，该研究为区块链安全领域提供了可验证、可复现的防护验证框架，有望在对抗网络犯罪方面取得实质性突破。

Abstract: Blockchains are among the most adversarial environments in computing. Billions are stolen by cybercriminals who exploit vulnerabilities. This is an open problem and no concept or technique has proven to really make a difference. In this paper, we claim that the classical notion of program invariant is perhaps the most powerful solution to the problem. We devise anoriginal experimental protocol to 1) study how invariants would have protected against past real-world attacks and 2) whether state-of-the-art automated tools can find them. The experimental toolchain is sophisticated. It is based on INVARIANTEVAL, a benchmark of 28 real Ethereum exploits, each paired with a human-authored invariant that blocks the attack. We validate every invariant with PONDEREPLAY, a replay framework that re-executes transactions in order to prove the correctness and soundness of smart contract invariants. We demonstrate that smart contract invariants block all the cybercriminal attacks in INVARIANTEVAL, fully validated by replaying 108,637 historical transactions. Our large-scale experiments clearly demonstrate that smart contract invariants protect against cybercriminals.

</details>


### [310] [Slow and Steady: Preventing MEV with Verifiable Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13271)
*Zeta Avarikioti,Dimitris Karakostas,Karl Kreder,Shreekara Shastry*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对分布式账本中最大可提取价值（MEV）机会的防御机制，通过执行可验证延迟来强制交易生成过程增加不可跳过的等待时间，从而使区块生产者无法在MEV机会出现时即时响应而不违反活性原则。作者在拜占庭模型和理性参与者的博弈论模型中都取得了正面结果，同时给出了负面界限以说明该防御路线的局限性，并基于历史MEV数据验证了该机制在现实中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEV攻击是分布式账本中的重大安全隐患，区块生产者可以利用其对交易排序的权力提取额外价值，损害普通用户的交易公平性和协议安全性。现有防御手段（如阈值加密、提交-揭示方案）往往需要多方信任假设或复杂的密码学基础设施，本文旨在探索一种更轻量、基于时间延迟的防御思路。

Method: 提出在交易生成阶段强制引入可验证延迟（Verifiable Delay）的防御机制，使区块创建者无法在对MEV机会做出反应的同时保持系统活性（liveness）。作者从两个角度进行分析：一是拜占庭容错设定下的协议安全性；二是理性参与者模型下的博弈论分析。此外还给出了理论上的不可能性界限（negative bounds），并利用历史MEV数据进行了实证评估，探讨了可验证延迟的实际部署可行性。

Result: 在拜占庭设定和博弈论模型中均获得正面的安全性结果；同时证明该防御路线存在明确的理论局限性（负面界限）；基于历史MEV数据的实证分析表明该机制能够有效抵御大多数现有MEV威胁。

Conclusion: 基于可验证延迟的MEV防御机制在理论和实践层面都展现出显著潜力，尽管存在不可避免的局限性，但作为现有防御手段的补充，该方案具有现实部署价值。

Abstract: Our work presents a defense mechanism against Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) opportunities in distributed ledgers. The mechanism relies on the idea of enforcing a verifiable delay when generating transactions, such that a block creator cannot react to the appearance of a MEV opportunity without breaking liveness. We present positive results both in the Byzantine setting and in a game theoretic model of rational participants. We additionally present negative bounds that outline the limitations of this line of defense. Finally, we explore real-world implementation details of verifiable delays and show that, based on historical MEV data, our mechanism could realistically help prevent most existing MEV threats.

</details>


### [311] [VR-Themis: A Scalable Framework for Virtual Reality Application Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13290)
*Gengyang Xu,Hanyang Guo,Hong-Ning Dai,Weizhi Meng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VR-Themis，一个基于层次-对象-行为（HOB）的两阶段VR应用克隆检测框架，通过粗粒度聚类和细粒度相似度分析，在4,277个VR应用中成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用且无假阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用重打包（应用克隆）威胁用户安全隐私并侵犯开发者版权，但现有检测方法主要针对Android等移动平台，未能捕捉虚拟现实（VR）应用的独有特征，因此无法有效检测VR市场中常见的克隆VR应用。

Method: 提出VR-Themis框架，基于层次-对象-行为（HOB）进行两阶段检测。第一阶段（粗粒度）利用可检索的统计特征对应用进行聚类，使其可扩展到大规模VR应用数据集；第二阶段（细粒度）对第一阶段筛选出的可疑应用进行深入分析，使用定义的HOB指标计算相似度。

Result: 实验表明VR-Themis在收集的4,277个VR应用中成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用，无误报，验证了其有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: VR-Themis通过考虑VR应用的独特特征，采用两阶段检测策略，在保持可扩展性的同时实现了高精度的VR应用克隆检测。

Abstract: Repackaging of mobile applications (aka app cloning) not only threatens the security and privacy of mobile users but also infringes upon the copyright of the original app developers. However, existing detection methods that primarily focus on mobile platforms (such as Android) fail to capture the essential features of virtual reality (VR). Consequently, they are inadequate for effectively detecting cloned VR apps, which have often been targeted by illegal users in the VR market. Considering the unique features of VR apps, this paper proposes a two-stage app clone detection framework, namely VR-Themis, based on \emph{Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour} (HOB). Firstly, VR-Themis exploits the coarse-grained stage to cluster apps based on their retrievable statistical features, making this tool scalable to large-scale VR app datasets. Then, in the fine-grained stage, VR-Themis performs in-depth analysis of the suspicious apps (identified in the first stage) by calculating similarity using our defined \emph{HOB metrics}. Our extensive experiments indicate that VR-Themis successfully detects 307 suspected clone apps from the collected 4,277 VR apps without false positives, demonstrating its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [312] [TeleGapper: On the (un)reliability of Privacy Policies in Telegram Mini apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13390)
*Luca Ferrari,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TeleGapper，一个黑盒动态分析框架，用于评估Telegram Mini Apps的隐私姿态。通过捕获运行时网络流量，识别第三方通信，并将其与公开的隐私政策进行对比。对278个Mini Apps的评估发现：59.4%的应用联系了至少一个未披露的第三方，78.8%的应用仅依赖Telegram默认隐私政策，且没有任何应用提供同意或退出机制，暴露了该生态系统中严重的透明度与合规性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram Mini Apps作为嵌入在Telegram客户端中的Web应用，已经形成庞大的第三方服务生态系统，但其隐私属性至今未被充分研究。与WeChat等受控的专有执行框架不同，Telegram Mini Apps运行在WebView中，结合了平台提供的上下文与标准Web能力及不受限制的出站网络连接，使得应用可以通过普通Web请求将敏感信息传递给第三方。隐私披露（如隐私政策）对于透明度至关重要，但Telegram允许开发者使用通用的默认隐私政策，这可能导致实际数据实践与披露信息不符。因此，需要系统性评估该生态系统的隐私状况。

Method: TeleGapper是一个黑盒动态分析框架，通过三个步骤评估Mini Apps的隐私姿态：（1）捕获运行时网络流量；（2）识别第三方通信；（3）将观察到的数据流与披露的隐私信息进行对比。研究者从tApps Center（社区驱动的Telegram第三方应用目录）收集了278个可正常运行的Mini Apps作为评估样本。

Result: 评估结果显示：59.4%的Mini Apps联系了至少一个未在隐私政策中披露的第三方。78.8%的Mini Apps完全依赖Telegram的默认隐私政策，而非自定义政策。没有任何一个Mini App提供同意或退出（opt-out）机制。这些发现暴露了这一被广泛使用但研究不足的生态系统中存在的实质性透明度与合规性差距。

Conclusion: Telegram Mini Apps生态系统中存在严重的隐私透明度和合规性问题。绝大多数应用依赖通用默认隐私政策，大量应用与未披露的第三方共享数据，且缺乏任何用户同意或退出机制。这暗示现有的隐私保护框架在Telegram Mini Apps生态中远未达到应有的透明度和合规标准，亟需改进。

Abstract: Telegram Mini Apps are Web applications embedded within the Telegram client, forming an ecosystem of third-party services within one of the world's most widely used messaging platforms. Despite their growing adoption and access to Telegram-provided context, their privacy properties remain largely unexplored. Unlike ecosystems such as WeChat, which rely on tightly controlled, proprietary execution frameworks, Telegram adopts a different model: Mini Apps run inside a WebView, combining platform-provided context with standard Web capabilities and unrestricted outbound networking. This enables applications to transmit sensitive information to analytics, advertising, tracking, or other third parties through ordinary Web requests, often with limited visibility.
  Privacy disclosures are therefore critical for transparency. Telegram allows Mini Apps either to define an application-specific privacy policy or to rely on a platform-provided default policy. While the latter reduces the developer's disclosure burden, it may lead to generic statements that do not accurately capture actual data practices of individual Mini Apps.
  In this paper, we present TeleGapper, a black-box dynamic analysis framework to assess the privacy posture of Mini Apps by capturing runtime network traffic, identifying third-party communications, and comparing observed data flows against disclosed privacy information. We evaluate 278 working Mini Apps collected from tApps Center, a community-driven catalogue for discovering third-party applications in Telegram. We find that 59.4% contact at least one undisclosed third party, 78.8% rely exclusively on Telegram's default privacy policy, and none provides a consent or opt-out mechanism. These findings expose a substantial transparency and compliance gap in a widely used yet understudied ecosystem.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.GN'></div>

# q-bio.GN [[Back]](#toc)

### [313] [Static analysis-guided agentic AI translation enables Rust as a full stack bioinformatics language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13029)
*Johan Henriksson*

Main category: q-bio.GN

TL;DR: 本文展示了利用智能体AI结合静态分析，将生物信息学领域遗留代码（如Perl、Fortran编写）翻译为现代语言Rust的方法。以单细胞流水线Bascet为例，代码体积减少约80倍，构建时间缩短约10倍，关键步骤性能提升3倍以上，且消除了Unix依赖，使其成为唯一能在原生Windows（无需容器）上运行的单细胞流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物信息学领域长期依赖遗留代码，这些代码存在缺乏维护者、使用过时语言（Perl、Fortran）、技术债务高、动态类型语言对环境和现代硬件利用不足，以及安全性和可靠性问题不适合临床应用等挑战。亟需一种高效、经济的方式将遗留代码迁移到现代语言。

Method: 提出一种智能体AI（agentic AI）与静态分析相结合的方法，系统地翻译遗留代码至Rust语言。作者提供了提示词（prompts）和辅助软件工具，以支持系统化的翻译流程，并在NGS（下一代测序）和成像领域的常见软件上进行评估。

Result: 在Bascet软件上取得了显著成果：代码体积减少约80倍，构建时间降低约10倍，关键步骤的性能提升超过3倍。同时移除了Unix依赖，使Bascet成为唯一能够在原生Windows环境（无需容器）运行的单细胞流水线。

Conclusion: 大规模生物信息学软件重构现在可以在有限预算内实现，使得开发更复杂、更现代的工具成为可能。该工作证明了AI辅助代码迁移作为解决生物信息学领域技术债务问题的可行性方案。

Abstract: The field of bioinformatics struggles with legacy code - old code that is commonly used but may no longer have a maintainer, or may be written in an now-unfamiliar language (e.g. Perl, Fortran). This incurs maintenance cost (technical debt), but dynamically typed languages also negatively impacts the environment and fail to make use of modern hardware. Legacy code may also have security or safety problems that make it unsuited for use in clinical settings. Here we show that agentic AI, combined with static analysis, can be used to translate legacy code to the modern language Rust. We provide prompts and supporting software to aid systematic translation, and evaluate it on common software for NGS and imaging. We showcase the result on our software Bascet: Size was reduced by ~80x, build time decreased by ~10x, and performance of key steps improved >3x. Unix dependencies were also removed, making Bascet the only single-cell pipeline able to run on native Windows, without a container. Large-scale refactoring of bioinformatics software is thus now possible at a limited budget, enabling more complex tools to be developed.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [314] [SCALE-Sim EVA: Design Principles for an Extensible, Visualizable, and Adaptable Accelerator Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12354)
*Jingtian Dang,Ritik Raj,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出SCALE-Sim EVA，一个可扩展、可视化、自适应的IR感知加速器仿真框架，通过张量命令执行和可组合硬件组件建模，替代传统地址级周期轨迹回放，实现编译器级IR与硬件级执行粒度之间的桥接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI加速器融合异构计算单元、分层内存、局部缓冲和专用数据通路，导致固定加速器仿真器难以扩展到新执行模型之外。

Method: EVA将工作负载表示为基于张量的命令，跟踪运行时张量放置与就绪状态，在由用户定义功能单元和内存行为的可组合硬件组件上执行命令。它通过张量就绪性、硬件单元可用性和建模的操作/传输延迟计算周期时序，而非回放地址级周期轨迹；采用命令分解机制将编译器级IR粒度下降并调度到硬件层级，同时输出开放命令和存储轨迹用于可视化分析。

Result: EVA能够从张量就绪性、硬件单元可用性及操作/传输延迟计算周期时序，无需地址级周期轨迹回放；命令分解机制支持全局张量操作在硬件层级上降低和局部调度；开放命令和存储轨迹支持执行时间线、内存占用和资源争用的可视化分析。

Conclusion: EVA提供了一种可扩展、可可视化、自适应的仿真框架，能够跨越编译器级IR粒度与硬件级执行粒度进行建模，支持异构AI加速器的灵活仿真与分析。

Abstract: Modern AI accelerators increasingly combine heterogeneous compute units, hierarchical memories, local buffers, and specialized data movement paths. This diversity makes fixed accelerator simulators difficult to extend beyond their original execution model. We present SCALE-Sim EVA, an extensible, visualizable, and adaptable simulation framework for IR-aware accelerator modeling. EVA represents workloads as tensor-based commands, tracks runtime tensor placement and readiness, and executes commands on composable hardware components with user-defined functional units and memory behavior. Instead of replaying address-level cycle traces, EVA computes cycle timing from tensor readiness, hardware-unit availability, and modeled operation or transfer latency. Its command decomposition mechanism bridges compiler-level IR granularity and hardware-level execution granularity, allowing global tensor operations to be lowered and scheduled locally across a hardware hierarchy. EVA also emits open command and storage traces for visual analysis of execution timelines, memory occupancy, and resource contention.

</details>


### [315] [Lonic: Algorithm-Hardware Co-Design for Energy-Efficient Fully Local Online SNN Training with INT4 Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12500)
*Peilin Chen,Xiaoxuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Lonic是一个面向全局部在线监督SNN训练的算法-硬件协同设计框架，通过INT4低精度训练算法、可重构无乘数整数PE阵列、双优化零门控策略、时间前缀加速局部学习数据流和低精度权重搬移技术，在保持训练精度的同时显著提升能效和速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN局部在线训练算法虽能降低内存和计算开销，但未考虑算法优势能否有效转化为实际硬件设备上的效率。作者希望填补算法设计与硬件实现之间的鸿沟，实现算法-硬件协同优化。

Method: 算法层面：提出INT4低精度训练算法用于全局部在线SNN学习，保持精度同时降低计算复杂度。硬件层面：设计可重构无乘数整数PE阵列、双优化零门控策略（dual-optimization zero-gating）、时间前缀加速局部学习数据流（temporal prefix-accelerated local learning dataflow）和低精度权重搬移机制，全面提升训练效率。

Result: 相比Apple M4和Nvidia V100 GPU，Lonic分别实现平均能效提升17.44倍和66.28倍，加速比3.25倍和1.02倍；相比ASIC TPU类加速器和H2Learn加速器，能效提升15.95倍和14.64倍，面积效率提升1.52倍和7.28倍。

Conclusion: Lonic验证了算法-硬件协同设计在SNN局部在线训练中的有效性，证明通过低精度算法与专用硬件架构的深度配合，可以大幅超越通用GPU和现有ASIC加速器，推动SNN在实际设备上的高效部署。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) have recently attracted increasing attention as an energy-efficient learning paradigm. Existing works also propose temporally and fully local online SNN training algorithms to address memory and computation overhead. However, they do not consider whether the algorithmic advantages can be effectively translated into real-device efficiency. To address this challenge, we present Lonic, an algorithm-hardware co-design for energy-efficient and scalable fully local online supervised SNN learning. On the algorithm side, we implement an INT4 low-precision training algorithm for fully local online SNN learning while maintaining accuracy. On the hardware side, to leverage the benefits of the proposed algorithm, we introduce reconfigurable multiplier-free integer PE arrays, dual-optimization zero-gating strategy, temporal prefix-accelerated local learning dataflow, and low-precision weight movement to significantly improve training efficiency. Compared to Apple M4 and Nvidia V100 GPUs, Lonic achieves average energy efficiency improvements of 17.44x and 66.28x, respectively, along with speedups of 3.25x and 1.02x, respectively. Moreover, Lonic achieves 15.95x (14.64x) and 1.52x (7.28x) energy efficiency (area efficiency) over ASIC TPU-like and H2Learn accelerators, respectively. The code for Lonic is available at https://github.com/peilin-chen/Lonic.

</details>


### [316] [GateTruth: Auditing the Rigor of RTL Design Benchmarks via Mutation Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12635)
*Meet Bhadra*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出GateTruth，一种基于突变测试的引擎和方法论，用于审计RTL（寄存器传输级）硬件设计基准测试中testbench的可信度。通过对自身68任务基准套件和外部RTLLM v2.0基准进行审计，发现多数外部基准的testbench质量未能达到95%突变击杀阈值，并揭示了输出token截断等隐藏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RTL硬件设计大语言模型基准测试快速激增，但没有任何基准使用突变测试这一成熟的硬件验证技术来检验自身testbench的质量。一个从不失败的testbench不能证明设计正确，可能只是从未刺激到真正有缺陷的逻辑。因此需要一种系统方法来审计基准测试中testbench的严谨性。

Method: GateTruth包含两个核心组件：(1) 突变测试引擎：向参考设计注入确定性的、有种子控制的语义突变，测量testbench能捕获的突变比例（击杀率）；(2) 方法论：通过固定的确定性综合到时序流程进行打分，将正确性作为严格门槛。作者先在自建的68任务（60个spec-to-RTL生成+8个agentic修复）双轨套件上验证方法论，然后用同一引擎审计外部基准RTLLM v2.0。

Result: 自身套件中46/60的Track A testbench击杀至少95%的注入突变，其余14个失败的原因被披露（包括Goodhart效应）。审计RTLLM v2.0时，46个可审计设计中72%低于95%阈值，3个击杀率为0%。对NVIDIA CVDP基准的审计因缺少参考解（golden RTL）而无法进行。此外发现统一4096-token输出上限静默截断了7个被评估模型中的3个，将上限提升至16384 tokens后一个模型从第五名跃升至第一。

Conclusion: 突变击杀认证应成为RTL生成基准测试的标准报告要求。基准测试的testbench质量必须被量化验证，否则排行榜结果不可信；同时作者也指出当testbench本身成为优化目标时可能出现Goodhart效应，需要警惕。

Abstract: Benchmarks for evaluating large language models on register-transfer-level (RTL) hardware design have proliferated rapidly, yet none reports having applied mutation testing, an established hardware-verification technique for quantifying testbench quality, to ask whether its own testbenches are trustworthy. A testbench that never fails is not evidence of a correct design; it may simply never stimulate the logic that is actually broken. We introduce GateTruth, a mutation-testing engine and methodology for auditing RTL benchmark testbench rigor: inject a deterministic, seeded set of semantic mutants into a reference design and measure what fraction the testbench catches.
  We validate the methodology against our own 68-task, dual-track reference suite -- 60 specification-to-RTL generation tasks and 8 agentic-repair tasks, scored through a pinned, deterministic synthesis-to-timing flow with correctness enforced as a strict gate -- certifying that 46 of 60 Track A testbenches kill at least 95% of injected mutants under sequential, reproducible execution; we disclose why the other 14 do not, including a Goodhart effect on testbenches revised to pass this gate. We then point the same engine, unmodified, at RTLLM v2.0, a widely adopted external benchmark: of 46 auditable designs, 72% fall below the 95% floor our own suite is held to, and three score 0% outright.
  A comparable audit of NVIDIA's CVDP benchmark is structurally impossible: its public release withholds reference solutions, removing the golden RTL mutation testing requires. Auditing our own instrument also surfaced a second finding: an initially uniform 4096-token output cap silently truncated three of seven evaluated models, and re-running at 16,384 tokens moved one model from fifth place to first. We argue mutation-kill certification should become a standard reporting requirement for RTL-generation benchmarks generally.

</details>


### [317] [Spec-Driven Hardware Evolution via Executable Contract Refinement and Proof-Guided RTL Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12684)
*Shibo Zhao,Yang Zhang,Mengxia Tao,Baoqi Zhang,Kezhi Li,Qiang Xu,Binwu Zhu,Hao Yan,Min Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出"规范驱动的硬件演化"(spec-driven hardware evolution)框架，将RTL版本迭代从"提示词到RTL生成"范式转变为"合同中心"的语义演化范式，通过四阶段流程(Specify, Plan, Implement, Validate)在可执行规范合同指导下自动演进已验证的遗留RTL设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件开发本质上是渐进式的——重大版本迭代通常基于已验证的遗留实现进行行为变更和更新，而非从头重写RTL。然而，现有基于LLM的硬件研究仍主要关注"提示词到RTL生成"，缺乏对可信任遗留设计进行语义版本演化的支持。这一差距促使作者提出更符合硬件真实开发范式的合同驱动演化方法。

Method: 提出四个阶段：(1) Specify—将新特性请求精炼为已评审的可执行合同，包含行为级参考模型、显式的外部事务级观察与检查语义；(2) Plan—基于变异探测的语义探针，推导跨版本语义差异并定位受影响的RTL区域；(3) Implement—在遗留感知下执行RTL更新；(4) Validate—在证明引导的检查和迭代修复下进行验证。整个流程在合同批准后自动执行。

Result: 在代表性TPU数据通路模块的数据格式变更受控版本演化案例上进行了评估。结果支持合同驱动硬件演化的可行性，证明所提后端工作流能在已评审可执行合同的指导下，有效驱动已验证的遗留RTL向下一版本功能收敛。

Conclusion: 规范驱动的硬件演化为LLM支持的硬件版本迭代提供了新范式，通过合同中心化方法弥补了现有提示词到RTL生成框架在语义版本演化上的不足，展示了向可信任遗留设计进行可靠演化的可行性。

Abstract: Hardware development is inherently evolutionary: major revisions typically begin by changing intended behavior and then updating a previously validated implementation, rather than regenerating RTL from scratch. Yet most recent LLM-based hardware research still frames the task primarily as prompt-to-RTL generation, offering limited support for semantic version evolution of trusted legacy designs. We present spec-driven hardware evolution, a contract-centered formulation for RTL version iteration. Instead of treating a new feature request as a direct prompt for RTL generation, we refine it into a reviewed executable contract for the next version. This contract specifies what must hold at the externally visible transactional level through a behavior-level reference together with explicit observation and checking semantics, while leaving how the change is realized in RTL to the evolution process. Based on this formulation, we organize hardware evolution into four stages: Specify, Plan, Implement, and Validate. After contract approval, the remaining stages proceed automatically: Plan derives cross-version semantic deltas and localizes affected RTL regions, aided by mutation-based semantic probing; Implement and Validate then perform legacy-aware RTL update under proof-guided checking and iterative repair. We evaluate the framework on a controlled version-evolution case study of a representative TPU datapath block under data-format changes. The results support the feasibility of contract-driven hardware evolution and demonstrate that the proposed backend workflow can effectively drive validated legacy RTL toward next-version functional convergence under a reviewed executable contract. An anonymous artifact for reproducibility is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SDHE-3A6C.

</details>


### [318] [SynAct: A Reasoning-Acting Large Language Model Agent for Adaptive Synthesis Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12751)
*Fangzhou Liu,Peiyi Han,Jiawei Liu,Yuan Pu,Zhuolun He,Rongliang Fu,Tsung-Yi Ho,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SynAct是一个自适应闭环LLM推理-行动智能体，通过迭代诊断实时综合报告、基于当前电路状态推理并发出定向优化命令，在14个设计上将平均WNS降至bootstrap综合的27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑综合中PPA结果对优化命令的选择高度敏感，综合调优高维且昂贵。现有自动化方法在黑盒搜索、固定动作空间上缺乏决策级可解释性；基于LLM的方法通常一次性生成静态脚本，无法适应不断演化的电路状态。

Method: SynAct采用自适应闭环LLM推理-行动范式，迭代地：(1) 诊断实时综合报告，(2) 基于当前电路状态、检索的工具知识以及历史优化经验进行推理，(3) 发出针对性的综合命令。重点优化时序（尤其WNS），同时维持面积与功耗的均衡。

Result: 在商业综合工具上对14个设计进行实验，SynAct将平均WNS降低至bootstrap综合结果的27%。

Conclusion: SynAct通过闭环LLM智能体实现动态、可解释且自适应的综合调优，相较现有方法显著提升时序表现，同时兼顾面积和功耗权衡。

Abstract: Logic synthesis transforms RTL designs into gate-level netlists, where PPA results are highly sensitive to the choice of optimization commands, making synthesis tuning both high-dimensional and expensive. Previous approaches fall into two categories: automated methods, which perform black-box search over fixed action spaces with limited decision-level interpretability, and LLM-based methods, which typically generate static scripts upfront and cannot adapt to evolving circuit states. We present SynAct, an adaptive closed-loop LLM reasoning--acting agent that iteratively diagnoses live synthesis reports and reasons over the current circuit state, retrieved tool knowledge, and historical optimization experience to issue targeted commands. SynAct focuses on improving timing, particularly worst negative slack (WNS), while maintaining balanced area and power trade-offs. Experiments on a commercial synthesis tool across 14 designs show that SynAct reduces average WNS to 27% of that from bootstrap synthesis.

</details>


### [319] [Dryas: A Reprogrammable Engine for High-Speed Interconnect Tracing and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12934)
*Manuel Bröchin,Tom Kuchler,Michael Giardino,David Cock,Timothy Roscoe*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Dryas是一个开源工具，用于分析现代异构计算系统中的高速低延迟互连（如30 GiB/s、200 ns的CPU-FPGA互连）。它利用基于非确定性有限自动机（NFA）的运行时可重编程覆盖引擎，以极小的硬件资源实现对全速运行互连的实时追踪和过滤，可捕获缓存行粒度的罕见、复杂或瞬态事件，且无需重新编程FPGA或干扰运行中的应用即可在不到一秒内更换过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算系统中异构组件的普及带来了更高带宽、更低延迟的互连，但这些接口和协议极其复杂，FPGA实现开发、调试和分析需要大量工程工作。此外，功能实现完成后，控制器和相关软件的优化需要处理数百GB的追踪数据，传统调试和优化方法难以应对这些挑战。

Method: Dryas采用运行时可重编程的覆盖引擎，基于非确定性有限自动机（NFA），并使用状态转移元素（STEs）高效实现。该系统能以缓存行粒度追踪事件，且过滤器可在不到一秒内更换，无需FPGA重编程或干扰运行中的应用。论文还探讨了在真实CPU-FPGA相干研究平台上实现NFA的数学基础。

Result: 论文评估了Dryas在不同规模NFA下的可扩展性，并展示了两个使用案例：调试FPGA互连实现和分析缓存行为。Dryas以最小硬件资源实现了对30 GiB/s全速互连的实时分析和事件捕获。

Conclusion: Dryas为高速互连的调试和性能分析提供了一种高效、可重配置的解决方案，不仅能调试互连实现本身，还能分析加速应用的行为，在保持全速运行的同时实现细粒度的实时追踪与过滤。

Abstract: The proliferation of heterogeneous components in modern computing systems has been accompanied by new higher bandwidth and lower latency interconnects. These interfaces and protocols are enormously complex and the process of developing, debugging, and analyzing FPGA-based implementations requires significant engineering work. Moreover, once a functional implementation is completed, optimization of the controller and associated software requires processing potentially hundreds of gigabytes of trace data.
  In this paper, we present Dryas, an open source tool for analyzing such an interconnect. We developed our tool, using minimal hardware resources, alongside an FPGA implementation of a very high speed, low latency (30~GiB/s, 200~ns) interconnect. With our run-time reprogrammable overlay engine we can inspect this interconnect to find rare, complex, or transient events even at full operation. This filtering engine is based on non-deterministic finite automata (NFAs), efficiently implemented using state transition elements (STEs), allowing us to trace events at a cache-line granularity. Moreover we can change the filters in less than a second, without reprogramming the FPGA or interfering with the running application. This data enables not only debugging the implementation of the interconnect itself, but analyzing the behavior of accelerated applications.
  We examine the mathematical basis for using NFAs and describe their implementation on a real coherent CPU-FPGA research platform. We then evaluate the scalability of Dryas for various size NFAs, followed by two different use cases: debugging FPGA implementation of the interconnect and analyzing cache behavior.

</details>


### [320] [Why Do Prefetchers Fail? Let Agents Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13027)
*Xiangfeng Sun,Ceyu Xu,Ningzhi Ai,Zeyu Zhu,Yiyang Yuan,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于性能异常驱动的自动化研究流程，利用LLM代理（消耗19.1亿tokens）反复诊断硬件预取器失败原因并合成专门的子预取器，最终形成"预取器混合"（MoP）架构。在SPEC CPU2006/2017上，MoP相比无预取提升61.1%的geomean IPC，超越了人类设计的Alecto、Berti和Pythia预取器，并在6nm工艺下完成RTL综合，验证了代理驱动硬件设计流程的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件预取器设计仍依赖人工专家，需要人工检查数十亿条指令的执行和内存访问痕迹，识别模式并将其转化为在线硬件启发式算法，过程耗时且无法保证改进效果。人类专家无法系统性地检查跨多样真实世界工作负载的十亿级指令痕迹，亟需自动化设计流程。

Method: 提出"性能异常驱动的自动化研究流程"：每一轮迭代定位高影响未解释缺失到程序计数器（PC），为代理提供硬件日志、源代码和切片痕迹，通过可运行的最小案例验证诊断，针对重复出现的模式族合成专门的子预取器。测量性能和剩余异常反馈到后续迭代，实现"模拟器在环"的发现过程，超越模型先验。最终将所有专用子预取器组合成Mixture of Prefetchers（MoP）。

Result: 消耗19.1亿DeepSeek V4 Pro tokens；在SPEC CPU2006和SPEC CPU2017上，MoP实现61.1%的geomean IPC加速比（相对无预取），分别超过人类设计的Alecto、Berti、Pythia预取器14.5%、21.6%和23.6%；6nm工艺RTL综合报告110KB片上存储和0.0347mm²面积。

Conclusion: 这是首个实证证明代理（agent）驱动的硬件设计流程能够产出一个RTL可实现的预取器，并在未见工作负载上超越最先进的人类设计。MoP的成功验证了"诊断驱动"的自自动化设计范式在计算机架构领域的有效性。

Abstract: Hardware prefetchers are crucial to processor performance, yet their design remains labor-intensive and expert-driven. Architects inspect execution and memory-access traces, identify patterns, translate them into online hardware heuristics, and evaluate them in simulation, often with no guarantee of improvement. Human experts cannot systematically inspect billion-instruction traces across diverse real-world workloads.
  We present a performance-anomaly-driven autoresearch flow that repeatedly asks why a deployed prefetcher fails and uses the diagnoses to construct the Mixture of Prefetchers (MoP). Each iteration localizes high-impact unexplained misses to program counters, gives agents hardware logs, source code, and sliced traces, validates diagnoses through runnable minimal cases, and synthesizes specialized sub-prefetchers for recurring pattern families. Measured performance and remaining anomalies feed subsequent iterations, enabling simulator-in-the-loop discovery beyond model priors.
  The campaign consumes 1.91 billion DeepSeek V4 Pro tokens. On SPEC CPU2006 and SPEC CPU2017, MoP achieves a 61.1% geomean IPC speedup over no prefetching, outperforming the human-designed Alecto, Berti, and Pythia prefetchers by 14.5%, 21.6%, and 23.6%, respectively. RTL synthesis in a 6nm library reports 110 KB of on-chip storage and 0.0347 mm^2 area. To our knowledge, this is the first empirical demonstration that an agent-driven hardware-design process can produce an RTL-practical prefetcher that outperforms state-of-the-art human designs on unseen workloads.

</details>


### [321] [Potential Applications of HBF in LLM Serving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13127)
*Yihan Yin,Yinlun Zhao,Zhixin Yun,Guanying Wu,Feng Zhu,Kai Tao,Shu Li,Fei Huang,Zhe Zhang,Shuangchen Li,Hongzhong Zheng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文探讨将高带宽闪存（HBF）作为HBM的容量扩展引入GPU内存层次结构，以缓解LLM推理服务中的内存容量瓶颈。通过扩展模型权重的驻留集，HBF可提升MoE服务中的专家副本数量和多模型服务中的热模型复制能力，模拟表明其收益取决于保留HBM驻留执行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理服务日益受限于内存容量，因为模型权重、KV缓存和服务模型变体的数量持续增长，而HBM容量有限且昂贵。作者希望通过引入高带宽闪存（HBF）作为容量导向的扩展，在不破坏计算芯片所需带宽的前提下，突破HBM容量限制。

Method: 本文采用系统分析与建模方法：首先讨论HBF如何集成到GPU内存层次结构中而不过度影响计算芯片期望的带宽；随后对增加容量带来的系统级价值进行建模，将容量扩展视为对只读型模型状态对象的扩展驻留；并通过仿真实验评估HBF在MoE和多模型服务场景下的收益。

Result: 仿真结果显示，HBF能够通过扩展模型权重的驻留集带来收益——在MoE服务中支持更多专家副本，在多模型服务中减少模型加载频率并支持热模型复制。但这些收益依赖于保留HBM驻留的执行路径，即核心执行仍走HBM，HBF仅用于扩大模型权重的驻留规模。

Conclusion: HBF作为HBM的容量扩展在LLM推理服务中具有实用价值，其关键设计原则是将HBF用于扩展只读模型权重的驻留集而非替代HBM的执行路径，从而在不牺牲带宽的前提下缓解内存容量瓶颈，提升MoE和多模型服务的整体系统效率。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly constrained by memory capacity as model weights, KV caches, and the number of served model variants continue to grow. This report examines High-Bandwidth Flash (HBF) as a capacity-oriented extension to HBM-based serving systems. We first discuss how HBF can be integrated into the GPU memory hierarchy without undermining the bandwidth expected by the compute die. We then model the system-level value of added capacity as expanded residency for read-mostly model-state objects. Under this view, HBF can improve MoE serving by enabling more expert replicas and can improve multi-model serving by reducing model loading and supporting hot-model replication. Our simulation results show that these benefits depend on preserving the HBM-resident execution path while using HBF to expand the resident set of model weights.

</details>


### [322] [ROLoad-PMP: Securing Sensitive Operations for Kernels and Bare-Metal Firmware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13287)
*Wende Tan,Chenyang Li,Yangyu Chen,Yuan Li,Chao Zhang,Jianping Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出ROLoad-PMP，一种轻量级的硬件-软件协同设计方案，通过新指令仅从带特定密钥的只读内存加载数据来保护敏感操作不被劫持，在FPGA上基于RISC-V实现原型，硬件开销<1.40%，性能开销<0.853%，安全性优于ARM BTI和Intel CET。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击者常通过劫持敏感操作（如控制流转移）来破坏受害者系统。现有方案要么只覆盖部分目标，要么性能开销过高，难以部署在资源受限的系统（如IoT设备）或底层软件（内核、裸机固件）上。

Method: 提出ROLoad-PMP硬件-软件协同设计方案：(1) 新增指令，仅能从带特定密钥的只读内存区域加载数据，确保被（可能损坏的）数据指针指向对象的完整性；(2) 提供程序加固机制，在编译期将敏感操作的操作数分类并放入不同密钥的只读内存，运行时用ROLoad-PMP指令加载；(3) 基于RISC-V实现FPGA原型。

Result: FPGA原型验证显示：额外硬件资源开销小于1.40%；实现多种轻量级防御（开销<0.853%）；相比ARM BTI和Intel CET提供更广泛、更强的安全保证。

Conclusion: ROLoad-PMP以极低的开销为低层软件提供轻量级、可部署的敏感操作保护方案，在前向边控制流完整性等防御场景中展示了优异效果，优于现有主流硬件安全方案。

Abstract: A common way for attackers to compromise victim systems is hijacking sensitive operations (e.g., control-flow transfers) with attacker-controlled inputs. Existing solutions in general only protect parts of these targets and have high performance overheads, which are impractical and hard to deploy on systems with limited resources (e.g., IoT devices) or for low-level software like kernels and bare-metal firmware. In this paper, we present a lightweight hardware-software co-design solution ROLoad-PMP to protect sensitive operations from being hijacked for low-level software. First, we propose new instructions, which only load data from read-only memory regions with specific keys, to guarantee the integrity of pointees pointed by (potentially corrupted) data pointers. Then, we provide a program hardening mechanism to protect sensitive operations, by classifying and placing their operands into read-only memory with different keys at compile-time and loading them with ROLoad-PMP-family instructions at runtime. We have implemented an FPGA-based prototype of ROLoad-PMP based on RISC-V, and demonstrated an important defense application, i.e., forward-edge control-flow integrity. Results showed that ROLoad-PMP only costs few extra hardware resources (< 1.40%). Moreover, it enables many lightweight (e.g., with negligible overheads < 0.853%) defenses, and provides broader and stronger security guarantees than existing hardware solutions, e.g., ARM BTI and Intel CET.

</details>


### [323] [YAVIN: A Unified Architecture for Secure Edge Processing in Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13496)
*Shouzhi Fang,William C. Tegge,Md Omar Faruque,Peipei Zhou,Endadul Hoque,Alex K. Jones*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: YAVIN提出了一种统一的可信计算基（TCB），将可信执行环境（TEE）从处理器扩展到专用的PIM内存区域，在内存总线被视为不可信的情况下，实现处理器和PIM执行单元内的数据解密、处理和重新加密。该系统首次在PIM上实现了LightSaber KEM后量子密码系统和ASCON-128认证加密，并通过密码学-PIM协同设计在张量工作负载上以极低开销满足认证加密的排序约束。相比最新的PIM AES实现，YAVIN实现了超过20倍的加速，同时INT8和INT32量化边缘LLM的执行开销分别仅为34%和9.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代边缘计算系统面临安全、私有的多租户执行挑战，跨越处理器、内存和加速器。现有TEE仅在处理器内建立信任，无法在内存中直接执行可信计算，因为内存总线被视为不可信资源。这限制了在内存中直接进行受信任的计算处理。

Method: YAVIN构建统一的TCB，将TEE扩展至处理器之外的专用内存区域以支持可信PIM执行。它利用传统TEE架构中已有的受保护内存区域，实现数据在TEE内由处理器或PIM解密、处理和重新加密。YAVIN首次实现了PIM版本的LightSaber KEM后量子密码系统和ASCON-128认证加密，并针对DRAM执行进行了算法协同设计。此外，通过密码学-PIM协同设计，对张量工作负载重新组织计算以满足认证加密的排序约束，同时采用比特切片排序限制明文临时暴露。

Result: 相比最新的PIM AES实现，YAVIN实现了超过20倍的加速。在INT8和INT32量化边缘LLM上，相对于明文执行，YAVIN仅分别引入34%和9.3%的性能开销。

Conclusion: YAVIN成功证明了将TEE信任边界从处理器扩展到PIM内存区域的可行性，通过密码学算法的PIM化实现和协同设计，在保持安全性的同时实现了高性能的可信内存计算，为边缘计算中的安全多租户执行提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Secure, private multi-tenant execution spanning processors, memory, and accelerators remains one of the most significant challenges in modern edge computing systems. Simultaneously, processing-in-memory (PIM) has emerged as an effective approach for reducing the Von Neumann bottleneck by moving computation closer to data. Existing trusted execution environments (TEEs) establish trust only within the processor, protecting data while it traverses untrusted resources such as the memory bus. Consequently, trusted computation cannot be performed directly within memory. We present YAVIN, a unified trusted computing base (TCB) that extends the TEE beyond the processor to encompass both processor execution and a dedicated memory region supporting trusted processing-in-memory execution while treating the memory bus as untrusted. Leveraging the dedicated protected memory regions already established by conventional TEE architectures, YAVIN enables data to be decrypted, processed, and re-encrypted by either processor or PIM execution while remaining within the TEE. To realize this unified TCB, YAVIN presents the first PIM implementations of the LightSaber KEM post-quantum cryptosystem and ASCON-128 authenticated encryption, co-designing both algorithms for efficient DRAM execution to establish and maintain shared cryptographic state. Finally, we demonstrate how cryptography-PIM co-design for tensor-based workloads reorganizes computation to satisfy the ordering constraints imposed by authenticated encryption with minimal performance overhead while simultaneously enabling bit-sliced ordering that limits temporary plaintext exposure. Compared to the latest PIM AES implementation, YAVIN achieves more than a 20x speedup while incurring only 34% and 9.3% overhead when executing INT8 and INT32 quantized edge-class LLMs, respectively, relative to plaintext execution.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [324] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LoKiFormer，一种新颖的大语言模型架构，通过局部融合注意力（LFA）和知识记忆模块（KMM）分别解决自注意力缺乏局部归纳偏置以及MoE将知识存储与计算路径耦合的问题，预训练收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构预训练效率低下，原因有二：(1) 自注意力缺乏对局部信息的显式归纳偏置，导致对序列内部局部信息进行冗余建模；(2) 混合专家（MoE）将知识存储与计算路径隐式耦合，阻碍了对序列外部全局知识的灵活访问。

Method: 在标准decoder基础上增加两个专用模块：1) 局部融合注意力（LFA），将卷积融合融入注意力机制，显式捕获局部模式，使注意力能基于更具信息量的表示进行操作；2) 知识记忆模块（KMM），引入参数化键值记忆，将全局知识显式存储在可寻址槽位中，实现存储与计算的解耦并支持直接知识检索。

Result: LoKiFormer在预训练中的收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍，展现出其在信息整合效率和效果上的优越性。

Conclusion: LoKiFormer通过解耦局部信息建模与全局知识存储，在预训练效率上取得了显著提升，验证了该架构设计思路的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [325] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于免费卫星数据（雷达干涉与气象监测），提出并评估了预测尼泊尔喜马拉雅冰川湖溃决风险、降雨触发的滑坡以及冰川小洪水三种灾害的模型。天气数据可准确预测触发时机（ROC 0.73-0.83），而地形仅能部分评估易感区域（修正后ROC 0.76、0.71、0.54）；深度学习模型未显著优于梯度提升基线，最终给出一个尼泊尔风险优先排序清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期可行性研究表明，雷达干涉测量的形变数据可指示哪个湖正在失稳，卫星天气可指示何时存在风险，但未提出预测模型。为填补这一空白，本研究旨在构建并评估能够预测"哪个地点易受影响"以及"何时触发"的模型。

Method: 使用589个已注明日期的溃决事件（HMAGLOFDB）和数千个已编目的滑坡，将每个事件与相似但未失效的地点进行匹配。采用空间交叉验证（按地图瓦片整体扣留），以简单基线作为基准。天气前置数据用于预测触发时机，地形用于评估易感性，并比较了五个深度学习模型与简单梯度提升基线。

Result: 天气触发时机预测ROC分别为：大型溃决0.73、滑坡0.83、小洪水0.82。地形易感性的诚实评分分别为0.76、0.71和0.54（小洪水不优于随机）。尼泊尔单一地区溃决信号达0.89。五个深度学习模型未显著优于梯度提升基线，湖灾害中基线胜出，并被包含崎岖度和季风降雨的三规则决策树所复现。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了基于免费数据的灾害风险评估工具，其中天气能可靠预测触发时机，地形对大型灾害有部分预测力但对小型洪水无效。研究最终给出了尼泊尔风险优先排序清单作为辅助参考，并指出了免费数据的能力边界。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [326] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARCH（Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History）架构，通过定期缓存循环状态检查点作为"状态锚点"，并利用内容条件化的锚键进行注意力聚合，使状态空间模型（SSM）能够在保持计算效率的同时，将固定大小的记忆扩展到随上下文长度增长的可变记忆库，在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上优于多种线性注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer虽具备随上下文长度增长的分词级记忆，但训练时有二次方计算复杂度，推理时键值缓存线性增长；而递归替代架构（如SSM）虽解码高效，但将全部历史压缩为固定大小状态，早期关联常被后续更新覆盖，在召回密集任务中表现不佳。因此需要一个既能扩展记忆容量又保持计算效率的架构。

Method: MARCH的核心设计包括：(1) 周期性缓存累积的递归状态检查点作为"状态锚点"，并构建可随上下文增长的记忆库；(2) 每个锚点关联一个紧凑的、基于内容条件化的锚键（anchor key）；(3) 每个token生成锚查询（anchor query），对所有因果可及的状态锚点进行注意力计算；(4) 输出通过注意力风格的聚合方式，将历史所有锚点与当前状态相结合。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上持续优于多种线性注意力变体，证明了内容路由的状态缓存能够显著增强递归模型的长程记忆能力，同时保持其原生的计算路径。

Conclusion: MARCH通过内容路由的状态缓存机制，在不破坏递归架构高效计算优势的前提下，实现了记忆容量随上下文长度可扩展的目标，为长序列建模提供了介于固定状态和完整分词记忆之间的可控折衷方案。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [327] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VGG-MADiffRL算法与MDCA三层闭环控制架构，用于多AUV自组网中的协同目标跟踪问题。该方法将扩散策略与价值梯度相结合，通过孪生价值网络和软目标更新缓解过估计问题，在动态水下环境中实现更快的收敛速度和更高的跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多AUV自组网目标跟踪面临声学通信受限、拓扑动态变化和海洋扰动不确定等挑战。现有基于多智能体强化学习的方法存在高维联合状态-动作空间建模困难、策略生成对噪声敏感等问题，导致训练不稳定和跟踪性能退化。

Method: 提出VGG-MADiffRL（值梯度引导的多智能体扩散强化学习算法）和MDCA（基于扩散的三层分层控制架构）。MDCA包含全局智能控制层、局部在线训练层和物理动作执行层三层闭环结构，实现任务分配、局部决策和执行反馈的协同优化。VGG-MADiffRL基于扩散策略，在逆向去噪过程中引入值梯度引导动作生成；采用孪生价值网络联合优化和软目标更新机制，抑制过估计和训练振荡。同时建模声呐检测机制和洋流扰动，将协同跟踪问题形式化为MDP。

Result: 实验结果表明，VGG-MADiffRL在协同跟踪场景中实现了更快的收敛速度、更高的跟踪精度和更平滑的训练动态，验证了其在动态水下环境中的有效性和实际工程价值。

Conclusion: VGG-MADiffRL结合扩散策略与值梯度引导，配合MDCA三层分层架构，为多AUV自组网协同目标跟踪提供了一种稳定且高效的解决方案，具有实际工程应用潜力。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [328] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成式建模统一表述为路径积分框架，其中基于流、扩散、变分和对抗的模型均为同一"主作用量"的不同评估原则。通过MSRJD形式将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，从而开启费曼图微扰论。该展开给出确定性采样器的一圈修正且无需额外随机采样成本，在可解与非线性漂移上验证可将53%的树级误差降至1.6%。不完美的学习得分以插入项形式进入框架，导出响应加权的得分匹配目标，对称等变漂移设计被重构为具有EFT功率计数的算符展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（flow、diffusion、variational、GAN）虽表面不同，但缺乏统一的数学框架来理解它们之间的深层联系；同时确定性采样器（如ODE-based）存在系统误差，而随机采样成本高昂。作者希望构建一个统一框架，既能揭示各类生成模型的内在联系，又能借助微扰理论系统性改进现有采样方法。

Method: 将生成建模表述为路径积分（主作用量），利用Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de Dominicis (MSRJD)形式将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，从而建立费曼图微扰论。通过该展开推导出一圈修正项，用于改善确定性采样器；同时将不完美学习的得分函数视为微扰插入项，推导出响应加权得分匹配目标；对称等变漂移设计被转化为EFF功率计数的算符展开。

Result: 在可解漂移和非线性漂移上验证了一圈修正的有效性，将树级误差从53%降低至1.6%，且不增加随机采样成本。

Conclusion: 该工作为生成式建模提供了一个统一的场论视角，不仅揭示了流、扩散、变分和对抗模型之间的深层结构关联，还通过微扰论提供了可计算的高阶修正途径，为确定性采样器的误差校正和等变生成模型设计提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [329] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
*Iyad Assaad Nekka,Hamida Seba,Khaled Walid Hidouci,Karima Amrouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了X-AddGraph，一个严格事后（post-hoc）的可解释性框架，为动态图中的边级异常检测模型AddGraph提供可解释性。该框架基于双重时空归因（DSTA）机制，分别对齐AddGraph的空间、短期时间与长期时间三个架构模块，在不改变检测性能（Delta AUC=0）的前提下提供归因解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的动态图异常检测器虽然达到了很强的准确率，但缺乏可解释性——当一条边被标记为异常时，分析人员只得到一个分数而不知道原因。在需要审计和可信赖的协作式、受监管信息系统中，这种不透明性是不可接受的。AddGraph作为图卷积+门控循环单元的基础框架，此前从未配备任何形式的可解释性机制。

Method: 提出X-AddGraph框架，构建于双重时空归因（DSTA）机制之上，包含三个与AddGraph架构模块对齐的组件：(1) 基于梯度的空间相关性归因，作用于当前邻接结构；(2) 直接读取推理期间已计算好的上下文注意力权重的短期时间归因（零额外成本）；(3) 通过循环隐藏状态进行梯度回滚的长期时间归因。检测器保持冻结状态，因此检测性能精确不变（Delta AUC=0，经验验证到小数点后十位）。

Result: 在UCI Message基准上，训练的AddGraph基线平均每快照AUC达到0.8705，超过原始发表结果；X-AddGraph完全复现每个分数同时增添解释。在四类边样本（高置信度真阳性、低置信度真阳性、假阳性、随机样本）的评估中，长期归因识别出的历史快照携带显著更多的反事实信号（0.127 vs 0.074），这是空间盲解释器无法提供的能力。

Conclusion: X-AddGraph成功为AddGraph提供了零成本、零性能损失的可解释性，其长期时间归因能够识别具有反事实信号的历史快照，这是以往纯空间归因方法无法实现的。作者发布实现代码以确保完全可复现性。

Abstract: Deep learning detectors for anomalies in dynamic graphs have reached strong accuracy, yet they remain opaque: when an edge is flagged, the analyst receives a score but no reason. This opacity is untenable in the cooperative, regulated information systems where such detectors are deployed, where automated decisions must be auditable and trustworthy. We address this gap for AddGraph, the foundational GCN+GRU framework for edge-level anomaly detection in dynamic graphs, which to our knowledge has never been equipped with any form of explainability. We present a strictly post-hoc explainability framework, X-AddGraph, built on a Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA) mechanism whose three components are each aligned with one of AddGraph's architectural modules: a gradient-based relevance attribution over the current adjacency structure (spatial), a direct reading of the contextual attention weights already computed during inference (short-term temporal, at zero additional cost), and a gradient rollback through the recurrent hidden states (long-term temporal). Because the detector is frozen, detection performance is preserved exactly (Delta AUC = 0, verified empirically to ten decimal places). On the UCI Message benchmark, our trained AddGraph baseline reaches an average per-snapshot AUC of 0.8705, exceeding the originally published result; X-AddGraph reproduces every score identically while adding explanations where none existed. Evaluated across four edge populations - confident true positives, low-confidence true positives, false positives, and random samples - the long-term attribution identifies historical snapshots carrying significantly more counterfactual signal than random selection (0.127 vs. 0.074), a capability that no spatially-blind explainer can provide. We release our implementation for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [330] [Personalized Scorer Modeling: A Learning-Based Framework for Deriving Robust Sleep Stage Labels from Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12446)
*Seyyed Ali Hoseini,Javad Baseri,Hamid Saadatfar,Edris Hoseini Gol,AmirHossein Eshghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的睡眠分期参考Hypnogram构建方法（LBH），通过建模每个评分者的混淆矩阵来聚合多位专家标注，在DOD-H和DOD-O数据集上使用EEG+EMG特征和随机森林分类器取得了约86%的准确率，优于数据集标准Hypnogram和最佳评分者Hypnogram。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动睡眠分期研究通常将单一参考Hypnogram作为金标准评估模型，但不同评分者之间存在显著的标注变异性。作者意识到多评分者数据集中的标注信息未被充分利用，希望利用多位专家标注的集体行为构建更可靠、更具代表性的参考标签。

Method: 使用DOD-H和DOD-O公开数据集，提取EEG(C3-M2)和chin EMG信号的30秒epoch，每个模态提取30个特征共60个特征。提出学习型Hypnogram（LBH），通过机器学习模型为每个评分者构建混淆矩阵，经列归一化后估计每个真实睡眠阶段在该评分者标签下的概率，然后跨评分者聚合这些概率以分配最终epoch标签。使用随机森林、SVM和多层感知机分类器，在EEG-only和EEG+EMG两种设置下评估，并与数据集Hypnogram（DH）和最佳评分者Hypnogram（BSH）对比。

Result: LBH在所有设置下一致地提升了整体性能。最佳结果由随机森林结合EEG+EMG取得：DOD-H上达到86.07%准确率、85.46%精确率和85.29% F1分数；DOD-O上达到86.04%准确率、85.21%精确率和84.70% F1分数。

Conclusion: 个性化评分者建模能够在不丢弃任何单个专家信息的前提下改善参考Hypnogram的构建质量，为多评分者睡眠分期数据集提供了一种更可靠的标签聚合策略。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification is important for the diagnosis and management of sleep disorders, yet most automatic staging studies evaluate models against a single reference hypnogram despite known inter-scorer variability. This study investigates whether multi-scored datasets can be used to construct more reliable reference labels from the collective behavior of multiple experts. We use the publicly available DOD-H and DOD-O datasets. EEG (C3-M2) and chin EMG signals were segmented into 30-s epochs, and 30 features were extracted from each modality, yielding 60 features for EEG+EMG. We propose a learning-based hypnogram (LBH) that models the stage-specific behavior of each scorer using confusion matrices derived from machine-learning models. After column normalization, these matrices estimate the probability of each true sleep stage given each scorer's label; probabilities are aggregated across scorers to assign the final label for each epoch. LBH was evaluated with random forest, support vector machine, and multilayer perceptron classifiers under EEG-only and EEG+EMG settings, and compared with the dataset hypnogram (DH) and best-scorer hypnogram (BSH). LBH consistently improved overall performance. The best results were obtained with random forest and EEG+EMG, reaching 86.07% accuracy, 85.46% precision, and 85.29% F1-score on DOD-H, and 86.04% accuracy, 85.21% precision, and 84.70% F1-score on DOD-O. These findings suggest that personalized scorer modeling can improve reference hypnogram construction without discarding information from individual experts.

</details>


### [331] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 训练后的Transformer模型会发展出特权基（privileged bases），本文发现预测方向（prediction direction）作为内容定义的特权锚点，将残差流的几何与行为结构按与预测的邻近度分层。这种分层在18个模型（7B-120B）中均成立，窄且尺度不变的预测界面集中了读出相关结构，而广大的预测远端补集随模型规模扩张。预测方向与主方差轴近乎正交，导致基于方差的分析只能部分恢复该组织。锚定分析揭示陡峭的几何梯度：预测近端区域高度结构化并聚类相关提示，远端则更平坦且反判别。干扰靠近预测的方差方向会导致立即发散和任务框架偏移，而干扰次层仅延迟发散。该工作确立了预测方向作为区别于已描述坐标轴的独特特权锚点，并提供了高维计算与线性读出共存的几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索训练后的Transformer模型中特权基所选择的方向性质，特别是预测方向是否作为内容定义的特权锚点，以及残差流如何围绕该锚点组织。

Method: 在18个模型（密集和混合专家模型、7B-120B、基础和指令微调）上，以预测方向（unembedding方向）为锚点测量残差流变异的几何与行为结构。对残差流进行邻近度分层，比较预测近端与远端的结构特性，并通过因果干预（扰动不同层级的方差方向）验证各区域的功能角色。

Result: 预测方向在所有18个模型中均作为内容定义的特权锚点起作用。残差流按与预测的邻近度分层：预测近端形成窄而结构化的接口区域，聚类相关提示；远端区域平坦且反判别。由于预测方向与主方差轴近乎正交，方差分析只能部分捕捉该组织且随提示异质性增大而差距扩大。干扰预测近端方差方向导致立即发散和任务框架偏移，干扰次层仅延迟发散。预测方向附近区域的读出对齐按方向较弱，但因果和时间上承重，行为由方向而非幅度驱动。

Conclusion: 预测方向是区别于先前描述坐标轴的独特特权锚点。该工作给出了高维计算如何与线性读出共存的几何解释：窄的预测接口集中有效信息，而宽大的预测远端为计算提供承载力，行为由方向而非幅度驱动。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>


### [332] [Exemplar-based objective classification of gust-induced loads across multiple flight conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12448)
*Paolo Olivucci,Kowshik Srivatsan,David E. Rival*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习表示与概要化（summarization）的客观分类准则，用于对风洞实验中随机阵风诱导的气动载荷进行归类。在3480组跨六种飞行姿态的压力测量数据中，识别出九种在不同姿态间复现的基本响应类型，既能保持与基于飞行姿态的标签同等可解释性，又能提供更细粒度的物理洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阵风诱导载荷的复杂性跨越多个飞行条件，传统基于粗粒度参数（如飞行姿态）的分类标签不够客观且难以揭示深层物理机制。作者希望找到一种既客观又保持可解释性的分类准则，以组织海量实验观测数据。

Method: 首先通过机器学习表征方法（machine-learned representation）对大量实验观测进行编码，然后应用概要化（summarization）程序选择一组最小但高度具有代表性的样本（exemplars）。这些示范样本构成基于相似性的客观分类准则，用于对所有观测进行分类。数据来源为飞翼模型的3480组由随机阵风诱导的压力载荷测量，覆盖六种飞行姿态。

Result: 该方法识别出九种在不同飞行姿态中反复出现的基本响应类型。通过对每种类型瞬态响应的分析，能够提供对底层流体力学机制的物理直觉。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功实现了对阵风载荷的客观、可解释分类，既保留了与粗粒度参数标签相当的直观性，又通过示范样本揭示了跨姿态的普适响应模式，为专家进一步检查和精细化实验提供了便利的基础。

Abstract: Is it possible to find an objective classification criterion that organizes the complexity of gust-induced loads across many flight conditions? And one that remains as interpretable as a labelling based on coarse parameters, such as the flight attitude? Our approach encodes a large number of experimental observations through a machine-learned representation and applies a summarization procedure to select a minimal subset of highly significant exemplars. The exemplars provide a similarity-based objective classification criterion of all the observations, they can be more conveniently inspected by experts and can become subject of more refined experiments. We demonstrate the approach on a database of 3480 pressure-load measurements induced by random gusts on a flying-wing model across six flight attitudes. We find nine fundamental response types that recur across multiple attitudes; analysis of a type's transient response enables physical intuition into the underlying fluid mechanics.

</details>


### [333] [Learning Under Treatment-Induced Label Indeterminacy with Expert Annotations of Counterfactual Outcomes: A Case Study in Neurological Prognostication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12477)
*Xiaobin Shen,Chloe Y. H. Huang,Jonathan Elmer,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对"治疗决策导致临床结局不可观测"场景下的预测模型评估框架，将患者分为确定（certain）和不确定（uncertain）两类分别评估，并提出了一个能在这两类目标标签之间进行权衡的简单预测模型。以心脏骤停后神经预后预测为案例（2,497名患者），发现仅关注确定病例的传统评估指标会掩盖不确定病例上的重要失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型通常假设每个患者的结局都能被清晰观测，但在现实中，治疗决策可能使有临床意义的结果永久不可观测（如心脏骤停后因治疗干预导致神经预后无法确定）。传统评估指标（如AUROC、Brier score）会忽略这些不确定病例，而这恰恰是预后支持最需要的患者群体，导致关键失效模式被掩盖。

Method: 作者提出了一个将评估明确拆分到确定和不确定病例两类的框架，因为两类病例的目标标签性质不同（确定病例有真实结局标签，不确定病例只有临床专家的反事实猜测）。同时提出了一个简单预测模型，能够同时使用两类标签并允许在两者之间进行权衡（trade-off）。实验使用2,497名心脏骤停患者的队列数据（含1,429名不确定病例），对比了所提出的神经模型与多个表格基线模型的性能。

Result: 研究发现，在确定病例上具有相似AUROC的模型，在其他指标上（确定病例的Brier score和不确定病例的概率估计）可能差异显著。对不确定病例目标标签对齐的改善通常以确定病例上准确性下降为代价，揭示了传统评估所掩盖的显式权衡。

Conclusion: 当治疗决策决定临床上有意义的结局是否保持可观测时，传统评估指标会遗漏那些最需要预后支持的患者的严重失效模式。未来的预测模型开发和评估应考虑将确定与不确定病例分开对待，并正视两者之间的权衡。

Abstract: Clinical prediction models are often developed as if the outcome of interest were cleanly observed for every patient. This assumption fails when treatment decisions make the clinically relevant outcome permanently unobservable. As a case study of this problem, we consider post-cardiac-arrest neurological prognostication using a cohort of 2,497 patients, including 1,429 patients whose outcomes were rendered indeterminate by treatment decisions. These patients with indeterminate outcomes were reviewed by independent clinical experts, who provided their guesses of counterfactual outcomes about what would have happened to the patients. We refer to these patients as uncertain cases. We also have patients for whom we observe their clinically relevant outcomes; we refer to these patients as certain cases. We propose a framework for evaluating prediction models that explicitly splits the evaluation between certain and uncertain cases. Here, we cannot easily evaluate both types of cases in a uniform manner as the available target labels differ. We then propose a simple prediction model that uses target labels from both certain and uncertain cases in a manner that allows us to trade off between them. Across the proposed neural model and a collection of tabular baselines, models with similar certain-case AUROC can nevertheless differ substantially in both certain-case Brier score and their probability estimates for uncertain cases. Improving alignment with target labels of uncertain cases for our proposed model generally comes at the cost of worse accuracy on certain cases, highlighting an explicit tradeoff that standard evaluation conceals. These results show that when treatment decisions determine whether clinically meaningful outcomes remain observable, conventional evaluation metrics can miss important failure modes in the very patients for whom prognostic support matters most.

</details>


### [334] [A Contract-Grade Verifier for LLM-Generated GPU Kernels, and a Native Blackwell Backward for the Gated-Linear-Recurrence Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12700)
*Rishi Shah,Rishav Shrestha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个由12道对抗性检查门组成的"合同级"GPU内核验证器，对2,638个人工智能生成且已被现有标准测试接受的内核进行审计，发现39.5%的内核存在无法用容差解释的错误，62.1%至少有一项违规。同时作者用该验证器证明了自己新设计的Blackwell tcgen05训练反向内核（GDN家族）的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用语言模型生成GPU内核的系统报告了高正确率，但这些正确率仅来自一次宽松测试（在固定形状下用几个随机输入运行，输出接近参考即通过）。这种测试无法捕捉各种静默错误，例如NaN/Inf被普通数值替代、运行间结果不一致、形状变化时出错、精度累积错误等。作者希望构建一个能严格检验内核正确性的工具，揭示真实正确率与报告数字之间的巨大差距。

Method: 作者构建了一个"合同级"验证器，包含12个对抗性的检查门（gates），每个门是正确内核必须满足的性质，其中多个是免容差的（tolerance-free），无法通过调整阈值来规避失败。验证器向外用于审计2,638个已被公开系统自身测试判定为正确的人工智能生成内核；向内用于验证作者自研的Blackwell tcgen05训练反向内核（GDN家族，含reverse-state阶段）的正确性，以双精度oracle为参照。结果通过四项独立手段防御性验证：7/7阳性对照、阈值校准扫描、与参考基准自身正确性代码98.5%一致性、分层人工审计。

Result: 验证器发现39.5%的被审内核在超出任何容差争论的意义上完全不可用，62.1%至少存在一项违规。现有标准测试接受了1,487个验证器拒绝的内核，而验证器接受而被标准测试拒绝的仅14个。作者自己的GDN内核通过了验证器检验，并成功训练了五个家族成员。

Conclusion: 内核生成领域的正确性信号远弱于报告数字所示，一组免容差的合同式验证条款能显著缩小这一差距。这也说明现有的单次宽松测试对内核正确性的判断高度不可靠，需要更严格、可复现的验证机制。

Abstract: Systems that generate GPU kernels with language models report high correctness rates. Those rates come from a single loose test: run the kernel on a few random inputs at one fixed shape and accept it if the output is close to a reference. A kernel can pass that test and still be silently wrong. It can return an ordinary number where the true answer is a NaN or an infinity, differ from run to run, break when the shape changes, or accumulate in fp16 where the reference keeps an fp32 total. We build the instrument that checks correctness properly: a contract-grade verifier of twelve adversarial gates, each a property a correct kernel must satisfy, several of them tolerance-free, so no choice of threshold can explain a failure away. Aimed outward, the verifier audits 2,638 machine-generated kernels that a public system's own harness had already accepted as correct. It finds 39.5% broken beyond any tolerance argument and 62.1% carrying at least one violation. The field's standard test accepts 1,487 kernels the verifier rejects, against only 14 the other way. We defend the finding four independent ways: a 7/7 positive control, a threshold-calibration sweep, 98.5% agreement with the reference benchmark's own correctness code, and a stratified hand-audit. Aimed inward, the verifier judges a kernel of our own: the first native Blackwell tcgen05 training backward for the gated-linear-recurrence (GDN) family, including the reverse-state stage the field still runs on a fallback. We establish its correctness independently, against a double-precision oracle, and train five family members through it.
